const OPEN_BOX_CAPACITY = 10;

/* ---------- Dati di esempio ---------- */

function seeded(seed, max) {
  let h = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < seed.length; i++) h = (h * 31 + seed.charCodeAt(i)) % 9973;
  return h % (max + 1);
}

function buildClasses(weekday) {
  return (SCHEDULE_BY_WEEKDAY[weekday] || []).map((time) => ({
    time,
    name: "Functional",
    booked: Math.min(CLASS_CAPACITY, 6 + seeded("cl" + time, CLASS_CAPACITY - 5)),
    coach: seeded("k" + time, 1) === 0 ? "Simone" : "Elena",
    confirmed: false,
  }));
}

function buildOpenBox(weekday) {
  return (OPEN_BOX_BY_WEEKDAY[weekday] || []).map((time) => {
    const [h] = time.split(":").map(Number);
    const end = `${String(h + 1).padStart(2, "0")}:00`;
    return { time, end, booked: seeded("ob" + time, OPEN_BOX_CAPACITY) };
  });
}

/* ---------- Stato occupazione ---------- */

function fillTone(booked, capacity) {
  const pct = booked / capacity;
  if (booked >= capacity) return "full";
  if (pct >= 0.85) return "high";
  return "ok";
}

const TONE_DOT = {
  ok: "bg-emerald-500",

  full: "bg-red-500",
};

function Logo({ size = 20, subtitle = true }) {
  // Il file contiene già scritta e sottotitolo: l'altezza totale
  // è circa il doppio dell'altezza delle sole lettere.
  const height = subtitle ? size * 2 : size * 1.25;
  return (
    <img
      src={LOGO_SRC}
      alt="PEAK Functional Fitness"
      className="shrink-0 object-contain object-left"
      style={{ height, width: "auto" }}
    />
  );
}

/* ---------- Elementi base ---------- */

/** Blocca lo scorrimento della pagina sotto un pannello aperto.
    Non basta overflow:hidden: su iOS viene ignorato, e su desktop la
    scomparsa della barra di scorrimento fa saltare il contenuto di lato.
    Fissiamo il body alla posizione corrente e compensiamo la barra. */
function useLockBodyScroll() {
  useEffect(() => {
    const b = document.body;
    const y = window.scrollY || document.documentElement.scrollTop || 0;
    const gap = window.innerWidth - document.documentElement.clientWidth;
    const prev = {
      position: b.style.position,
      top: b.style.top,
      left: b.style.left,
      right: b.style.right,
      width: b.style.width,
      overflow: b.style.overflow,
      paddingRight: b.style.paddingRight,
    };

    b.style.position = "fixed";
    b.style.top = `-${y}px`;
    b.style.left = "0";
    b.style.right = "0";
    b.style.width = "100%";
    b.style.overflow = "hidden";
    if (gap > 0) b.style.paddingRight = `${gap}px`;

    return () => {
      Object.assign(b.style, prev);
      window.scrollTo(0, y);
    };
  }, []);
}

function Card({ children, className = "", as: Tag = "div", ...rest }) {
  return (
    <Tag
      className={`bg-neutral-900/60 border border-neutral-800 rounded-2xl ${className}`}
      {...rest}
    >
      {children}
    </Tag>
  );
}

function Display({ children, className = "", size = "text-3xl" }) {
  return (
    <div
      className={`text-neutral-50 leading-none ${size} ${className}`}
      style={{ fontFamily: "Barlow, sans-serif", fontWeight: 800 }}
    >
      {children}
    </div>
  );
}

function SectionHead({ children, action, onAction }) {
  return (
    <div className="flex items-end justify-between mb-3">
      <h2
        className="text-neutral-50 text-[19px]"
        style={{ fontFamily: "Barlow, sans-serif", fontWeight: 700 }}
      >
        {children}
      </h2>
      {action && (
        <button
          onClick={onAction}
          className="text-[12px] text-neutral-400 hover:text-neutral-100 flex items-center gap-0.5"
        >
          {action}
          <ChevronRight size={14} />
        </button>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

function Bar({ value, max }) {
  const tone = fillTone(value, max);
  const pct = Math.min(100, Math.round((value / max) * 100));
  return (
    <div className="h-1.5 w-full bg-neutral-800 rounded-full overflow-hidden">
      <div
        className={`h-full rounded-full ${TONE_BAR[tone]}`}
        style={{ width: `${pct}%` }}
      />
    </div>
  );
}

/* ---------- Barra superiore con switch di ruolo ---------- */

function TopBar({ role, onRole, title, onBack }) {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  return (
    <header className="sticky top-0 z-20 bg-black/95 backdrop-blur">
      <div className="px-5 lg:px-8 py-4 flex items-center gap-3">
        {onBack ? (
          <button
            onClick={onBack}
            className="flex items-center gap-1 text-neutral-200 text-[15px] shrink-0"
          >
            <ChevronLeft size={20} />
            {title}
          </button>
        ) : (
          <Logo size={16} />
        )}

        <div className="flex-1" />

        <button className="text-neutral-300 relative shrink-0">
          <Bell size={21} strokeWidth={1.6} />
          <span className="absolute -top-0.5 -right-0.5 w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-neutral-50" />
        </button>

        <div className="relative shrink-0">
          <button
            onClick={() => setOpen((o) => !o)}
            className="flex items-center gap-1.5"
          >
            <span className="w-9 h-9 rounded-full bg-gradient-to-br from-neutral-700 to-neutral-900 border border-neutral-700 flex items-center justify-center text-[12px] text-neutral-300 font-semibold">
              S
            </span>
            <span className="text-[14px] text-neutral-200">
              {role === "admin" ? "Admin" : "Socio"}
            </span>
            <ChevronDown size={15} className="text-neutral-400" />
          </button>
          {open && (
            <>
              <div className="fixed inset-0 z-10" onClick={() => setOpen(false)} />
              <div className="absolute right-0 mt-2 w-52 bg-neutral-900 border border-neutral-800 rounded-2xl p-1.5 z-20 shadow-xl shadow-black/50">
                <div className="px-3 py-2 text-[10px] text-neutral-500 tracking-wider uppercase">
                  Visualizza come
                </div>
                {[
                  { key: "admin", label: "Admin", hint: "Console di gestione" },
                  { key: "socio", label: "Socio", hint: "App come la vedono gli altri" },
                ].map((r) => (
                  <button
                    key={r.key}
                    onClick={() => {
                      onRole(r.key);
                      setOpen(false);
                    }}
                    className={`w-full text-left px-3 py-2.5 rounded-xl ${
                      role === r.key ? "bg-neutral-800" : "hover:bg-neutral-800/60"
                    }`}
                  >
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                      <span className="text-[13px] text-neutral-100">{r.label}</span>
                      {role === r.key && (
                        <Check size={14} className="text-neutral-100" />
                      )}
                    </div>
                    <div className="text-[11px] text-neutral-500">{r.hint}</div>
                  </button>
                ))}
              </div>
            </>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </header>
  );
}

/* ---------- Navigazione ---------- */

const NAV = [
  { key: "dashboard", label: "Dashboard", icon: LayoutDashboard },
  { key: "utenti", label: "Utenti", icon: Users },
  { key: "classi", label: "Classi", icon: Calendar },
  { key: "wod", label: "WOD", icon: BarChart3 },
  { key: "openbox", label: "Open Box", icon: Dumbbell },
  { key: "comunicazioni", label: "Comunicazioni", icon: Megaphone },
  { key: "abbonamenti", label: "Abbonamenti", icon: CreditCard },
  // Visibile solo all'owner. Per gli altri admin questa voce non esiste.
  { key: "staff", label: "Staff", icon: Shield, ownerOnly: true },
  { key: "impostazioni", label: "Impostazioni box", icon: Settings },
  { key: "notifiche", label: "Notifiche automatiche", icon: BellRing },
  { key: "account", label: "Account admin", icon: UserCog },
];

const MOBILE_PRIMARY = ["dashboard", "utenti", "classi", "wod"];

// Profilo attualmente connesso. In produzione arriva dal backend.
const IS_OWNER = true;
const VISIBLE_NAV = NAV.filter((n) => !n.ownerOnly || IS_OWNER);

function Sidebar({ active, onChange, badge }) {
  return (
    <aside className="hidden lg:flex flex-col w-60 shrink-0 border-r border-neutral-800 bg-black">
      <div className="px-5 py-6">
        <Logo size={16} />
      </div>
      <nav className="flex-1 px-3 pb-6 space-y-0.5 overflow-y-auto">
        {VISIBLE_NAV.map(({ key, label, icon: Icon }) => {
          const isActive = active === key;
          return (
            <button
              key={key}
              onClick={() => onChange(key)}
              className={`w-full flex items-center gap-3 px-3 py-2.5 rounded-xl text-[13px] ${
                isActive
                  ? "bg-neutral-900 text-neutral-50 border border-neutral-800"
                  : "text-neutral-500 hover:text-neutral-200 border border-transparent"
              }`}
            >
              <Icon size={17} strokeWidth={isActive ? 2 : 1.6} />
              <span className="flex-1 text-left">{label}</span>
              {key === "utenti" && badge > 0 && (
                <span className="min-w-[18px] h-[18px] px-1 rounded-full bg-neutral-50 text-black text-[10px] font-bold flex items-center justify-center">
                  {badge}
                </span>
              )}
            </button>
          );
        })}
      </nav>
    </aside>
  );
}

function TabBar({ active, onChange, onMore, badge }) {
  return (
    <div className="lg:hidden fixed bottom-0 left-0 right-0 bg-black border-t border-neutral-800 flex justify-around items-center pt-2.5 pb-4 z-20">
      {NAV.filter((n) => MOBILE_PRIMARY.includes(n.key)).map(
        ({ key, label, icon: Icon }) => {
          const isActive = active === key;
          return (
            <button
              key={key}
              onClick={() => onChange(key)}
              className="flex flex-col items-center gap-1.5 px-2 relative"
            >
              <Icon
                size={21}
                strokeWidth={isActive ? 2.2 : 1.6}
                className={isActive ? "text-neutral-50" : "text-neutral-600"}
              />
              {key === "utenti" && badge > 0 && (
                <span className="absolute -top-1 right-0.5 min-w-[15px] h-[15px] px-1 rounded-full bg-neutral-50 text-black text-[9px] font-bold flex items-center justify-center">
                  {badge}
                </span>
              )}
              <span
                className={`text-[10px] ${
                  isActive ? "text-neutral-50 font-semibold" : "text-neutral-600"
                }`}
              >
                {label}
              </span>
            </button>
          );
        }
      )}
      <button onClick={onMore} className="flex flex-col items-center gap-1.5 px-2">
        <MoreHorizontal size={21} strokeWidth={1.6} className="text-neutral-600" />
        <span className="text-[10px] text-neutral-600">Altro</span>
      </button>
    </div>
  );
}

function MoreSheet({ onClose, onSelect }) {
  useLockBodyScroll();
  const rest = VISIBLE_NAV.filter((n) => !MOBILE_PRIMARY.includes(n.key));
  return (
    <div className="lg:hidden fixed inset-0 z-30 flex items-end">
      <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-black/70" onClick={onClose} />
      <div className="relative w-full bg-neutral-950 border-t border-neutral-800 rounded-t-3xl p-5 pb-9">
        <div className="w-10 h-1 bg-neutral-700 rounded-full mx-auto mb-5" />
        <div className="space-y-1">
          {rest.map(({ key, label, icon: Icon }) => (
            <button
              key={key}
              onClick={() => {
                onSelect(key);
                onClose();
              }}
              className="w-full flex items-center gap-3 px-3 py-3 rounded-xl text-neutral-300 hover:bg-neutral-900 text-[14px]"
            >
              <Icon size={18} strokeWidth={1.6} className="text-neutral-500" />
              <span className="flex-1 text-left">{label}</span>
              <ChevronRight size={15} className="text-neutral-600" />
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

/* ---------- Dashboard ---------- */

function KpiCard({ icon: Icon, value, label, onClick }) {
  return (
    <button
      onClick={onClick}
      className="text-left bg-neutral-900/60 border border-neutral-800 rounded-2xl p-4 hover:border-neutral-700"
    >
      <Icon size={26} strokeWidth={1.5} className="text-neutral-100" />
      <Display className="mt-3">{value}</Display>
      <div className="flex items-end justify-between gap-2 mt-1.5">
        <span className="text-[13px] text-neutral-400 leading-tight">{label}</span>
        <ChevronRight size={16} className="text-neutral-600 shrink-0 mb-0.5" />
      </div>
    </button>
  );
}

function NextActivityCards({ classes, openBox, hours, onOpen }) {
  const nextClass = classes[2] || classes[0];
  const nextSlot = openBox[5] || openBox[0];

  return (
    <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
      <button
        onClick={() => onOpen("classi")}
        className="text-left bg-neutral-900/60 border border-neutral-800 rounded-2xl p-4 hover:border-neutral-700"
      >
        <Users size={24} strokeWidth={1.5} className="text-neutral-100" />
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between mt-3">
          <span
            className="text-neutral-50 text-[15px] tracking-wide"
            style={{ fontFamily: "Barlow, sans-serif", fontWeight: 700 }}
          >
            CLASSI
          </span>
          <ChevronRight size={16} className="text-neutral-500" />
        </div>
        <div className="text-[13px] text-neutral-400 mt-1">
          {classes.length} classi oggi
        </div>
        {nextClass && (
          <div className="mt-5">
            <div className="text-[11px] text-neutral-500">Prossima classe</div>
            <div className="flex items-baseline justify-between mt-1">
              <Display size="text-2xl">{nextClass.time}</Display>
              <span className="text-[13px] text-neutral-400">
                {nextClass.booked}/{CLASS_CAPACITY}
              </span>
            </div>
            <div className="mt-2">
              <Bar value={nextClass.booked} max={CLASS_CAPACITY} />
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
      </button>

      <button
        onClick={() => onOpen("openbox")}
        className="text-left bg-neutral-900/60 border border-neutral-800 rounded-2xl p-4 hover:border-neutral-700"
      >
        <Dumbbell size={24} strokeWidth={1.5} className="text-neutral-100" />
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between mt-3">
          <span
            className="text-neutral-50 text-[15px] tracking-wide"
            style={{ fontFamily: "Barlow, sans-serif", fontWeight: 700 }}
          >
            OPEN BOX
          </span>
          <ChevronRight size={16} className="text-neutral-500" />
        </div>
        <div className="text-[13px] text-neutral-400 mt-1">Aperto oggi</div>
        <div className="text-[13px] text-neutral-200 mt-0.5 leading-snug">{hours}</div>
        {nextSlot && (
          <div className="mt-4">
            <div className="text-[11px] text-neutral-500">Prossimo slot</div>
            <div className="flex items-baseline justify-between mt-1">
              <Display size="text-2xl">{nextSlot.time}</Display>
              <span className="text-[13px] text-neutral-400">
                {nextSlot.booked}/{OPEN_BOX_CAPACITY}
              </span>
            </div>
            <div className="mt-2">
              <Bar value={nextSlot.booked} max={OPEN_BOX_CAPACITY} />
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
      </button>
    </div>
  );
}

function RecentList({ items }) {
  return (
    <Card className="divide-y divide-neutral-800 overflow-hidden">
      {items.map(({ icon: Icon, title, sub, when }, i) => (
        <div key={i} className="flex items-center gap-3.5 p-3.5">
          <Icon size={22} strokeWidth={1.5} className="text-neutral-400 shrink-0" />
          <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
            <div className="text-[13.5px] text-neutral-100 truncate">{title}</div>
            <div className="text-[12px] text-neutral-500 truncate">{sub}</div>
          </div>
          <div className="text-[12px] text-neutral-500 shrink-0">{when}</div>
        </div>
      ))}
    </Card>
  );
}

function Stat({ value, label, delta }) {
  return (
    <Card className="p-4">
      <Display size="text-2xl">{value}</Display>
      <div className="text-[11px] text-neutral-500 mt-2 leading-tight">{label}</div>
      {delta !== undefined && (
        <div
          className={`text-[11px] mt-1.5 flex items-center gap-1 ${
            delta >= 0 ? "text-emerald-400" : "text-neutral-400"
          }`}
        >
          <TrendingUp size={11} className={delta >= 0 ? "" : "rotate-180"} />
          {delta >= 0 ? "+" : ""}
          {delta}% sul mese
        </div>
      )}
    </Card>
  );
}

function AttendanceChart({ data }) {
  const max = Math.max(...data, 1);
  const labels = ["L", "M", "M", "G", "V", "S", "D"];
  return (
    <Card className="p-4">
      <div className="flex items-end justify-between gap-2 h-24">
        {data.map((v, i) => (
          <div key={i} className="flex-1 flex flex-col items-center gap-2 h-full">
            <div className="w-full flex items-end h-full">
              <div
                className={`w-full rounded-t-md ${
                  v === max ? "bg-neutral-50" : "bg-neutral-700"
                }`}
                style={{ height: `${(v / max) * 100}%` }}
              />
            </div>
            <span className="text-[10px] text-neutral-600">{labels[i]}</span>
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>
      <div className="text-[11.5px] text-neutral-500 mt-3 border-t border-neutral-800 pt-3">
        255 presenze confermate questa settimana · media 42 al giorno
      </div>
    </Card>
  );
}

function Dashboard({ classes, openBox, hours, pending, onGo, fillRate, activeMembers }) {
  const obBookings = openBox.reduce((s, o) => s + o.booked, 0);
  const now = new Date();
  const hour = now.getHours();
  const greeting =
    hour < 12 ? "Buongiorno" : hour < 18 ? "Buon pomeriggio" : "Buonasera";

  return (
    <div className="space-y-8">
      <div>
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-baseline justify-between gap-x-4">
          <h1
            className="text-neutral-50 text-[28px] leading-tight"
            style={{ fontFamily: "Barlow, sans-serif", fontWeight: 700 }}
          >
            {greeting}, Simone
          </h1>
          <span className="text-[13px] text-neutral-400">
            {DAY_FULL[now.getDay()]} {now.getDate()} {MONTH_FULL[now.getMonth()]}
          </span>
        </div>
        <p className="text-[14px] text-neutral-400 mt-1">Ecco la situazione di oggi.</p>
      </div>

      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-3">
        <KpiCard
          icon={Users}
          value={activeMembers}
          label="Soci in regola"
          onClick={() => onGo("utenti")}
        />
        <KpiCard
          icon={CalendarDays}
          value={classes.length}
          label="Classi oggi"
          onClick={() => onGo("classi")}
        />
        <KpiCard
          icon={BarChart3}
          value={obBookings}
          label="Prenotazioni Open Box oggi"
          onClick={() => onGo("openbox")}
        />
        <KpiCard
          icon={UserPlus}
          value={pending}
          label="Nuove richieste"
          onClick={() => onGo("utenti")}
        />
      </div>

      <section>
        <SectionHead action="Vedi dettagli" onAction={() => onGo("classi")}>
          Prossime attività
        </SectionHead>
        <NextActivityCards
          classes={classes}
          openBox={openBox}
          hours={hours}
          onOpen={onGo}
        />
      </section>

      <section>
        <SectionHead action="Vedi tutte" onAction={() => onGo("attivita")}>
          Ultime attività
        </SectionHead>
        <RecentList items={RECENT} />
      </section>

      <section>
        <SectionHead>Il box in numeri</SectionHead>
        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-3">
          <Stat value={activeMembers} label="Soci in regola" delta={4} />
          <Stat value={`${fillRate}%`} label="Riempimento classi oggi" />
          <Stat value="255" label="Presenze settimana" delta={7} />
          <Stat value="6" label="Nuovi iscritti nel mese" delta={-12} />
        </div>
        <div className="mt-3">
          <AttendanceChart data={ATTENDANCE_WEEK} />
        </div>
      </section>
    </div>
  );
}

/* ---------- Giornata: Classi / Open Box ---------- */

function Segmented({ value, onChange }) {
  return (
    <div className="bg-neutral-900 border border-neutral-800 rounded-2xl p-1 flex">
      {[
        { key: "classi", label: "Classi" },
        { key: "openbox", label: "Open Box" },
      ].map((t) => (
        <button
          key={t.key}
          onClick={() => onChange(t.key)}
          className={`flex-1 py-2.5 rounded-xl text-[14px] transition-colors ${
            value === t.key
              ? "bg-neutral-300 text-black font-semibold"
              : "text-neutral-400"
          }`}
        >
          {t.label}
        </button>
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}

function DayScreen({ tab, onTab, classes, openBox, hours }) {
  const now = new Date();
  return (
    <div className="space-y-5">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
        <h1
          className="text-neutral-50 text-[26px]"
          style={{ fontFamily: "Barlow, sans-serif", fontWeight: 700 }}
        >
          {DAY_FULL[now.getDay()]} {now.getDate()} {MONTH_FULL[now.getMonth()]}
        </h1>
        <button className="w-11 h-11 rounded-2xl bg-neutral-900 border border-neutral-800 flex items-center justify-center text-neutral-200">
          <CalendarDays size={20} strokeWidth={1.6} />
        </button>
      </div>

      <Segmented value={tab} onChange={onTab} />

      {tab === "classi" ? (
        <section>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3 mb-3">
            <Users size={26} strokeWidth={1.5} className="text-neutral-100" />
            <h2
              className="text-neutral-50 text-[19px] flex-1"
              style={{ fontFamily: "Barlow, sans-serif", fontWeight: 700 }}
            >
              Classi Functional
            </h2>
            <span className="text-[13px] text-neutral-400">
              {classes.length} classi oggi
            </span>
          </div>
          {classes.length ? (
            <Card className="divide-y divide-neutral-800 overflow-hidden">
              {classes.map((c) => {
                const tone = fillTone(c.booked, CLASS_CAPACITY);
                return (
                  <button
                    key={c.time}
                    className="w-full flex items-center gap-4 px-4 py-3.5 text-left hover:bg-neutral-900/60"
                  >
                    <span
                      className="text-neutral-100 text-[15px] w-14 shrink-0"
                      style={{ fontFamily: "Barlow, sans-serif", fontWeight: 600 }}
                    >
                      {c.time}
                    </span>
                    <span className="flex-1 text-[14px] text-neutral-300">
                      {c.name}
                    </span>
                    <span className="text-[14px] text-neutral-300 shrink-0">
                      {c.booked}/{CLASS_CAPACITY}
                    </span>
                    <span
                      className={`w-3 h-3 rounded-full shrink-0 ${TONE_DOT[tone]}`}
                    />
                    <ChevronRight size={16} className="text-neutral-600 shrink-0" />
                  </button>
                );
              })}
            </Card>
          ) : (
            <Card className="p-6 text-center text-[13px] text-neutral-400">
              Oggi il box è chiuso.
            </Card>
          )}
        </section>
      ) : (
        <section>
          <div className="flex items-start gap-3 mb-3">
            <Dumbbell size={26} strokeWidth={1.5} className="text-neutral-100 mt-0.5" />
            <div>
              <h2
                className="text-neutral-50 text-[19px]"
                style={{ fontFamily: "Barlow, sans-serif", fontWeight: 700 }}
              >
                Open Box
              </h2>
              <div className="text-[13px] text-neutral-400">Aperto oggi {hours}</div>
            </div>
          </div>
          {openBox.length ? (
            <Card className="divide-y divide-neutral-800 overflow-hidden">
              {openBox.map((s, i) => {
                const tone = fillTone(s.booked, OPEN_BOX_CAPACITY);
                const gapAfter =
                  openBox[i + 1] &&
                  Number(openBox[i + 1].time.split(":")[0]) !==
                    Number(s.time.split(":")[0]) + 1;
                return (
                  <React.Fragment key={s.time}>
                    <button className="w-full flex items-center gap-4 px-4 py-3.5 text-left hover:bg-neutral-900/60">
                      <span className="flex-1 text-[14px] text-neutral-200">
                        {s.time} – {s.end}
                      </span>
                      <span className="text-[14px] text-neutral-300 shrink-0">
                        {s.booked}/{OPEN_BOX_CAPACITY}
                      </span>
                      <span
                        className={`w-3 h-3 rounded-full shrink-0 ${TONE_DOT[tone]}`}
                      />
                      <ChevronRight size={16} className="text-neutral-600 shrink-0" />
                    </button>
                    {gapAfter && (
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-4 px-4 py-3 opacity-40">
                        <span className="flex-1 text-[14px] text-neutral-400">
                          {s.end} – {openBox[i + 1].time}
                        </span>
                        <span className="text-[13px] text-neutral-500">Chiuso</span>
                      </div>
                    )}
                  </React.Fragment>
                );
              })}
            </Card>
          ) : (
            <Card className="p-6 text-center text-[13px] text-neutral-400">
              Open Box chiuso oggi.
            </Card>
          )}
        </section>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

/* ============================================================
   Staff e permessi — visibile solo all'owner
   ============================================================ */

const PERMISSIONS = [
  {
    group: "Soci",
    items: [
      { key: "soci_view", label: "Vedere l'anagrafica" },
      { key: "soci_approve", label: "Approvare le iscrizioni" },
      { key: "soci_edit", label: "Modificare schede e certificati" },
    ],
  },
  {
    group: "Abbonamenti",
    items: [
      { key: "abb_view", label: "Vedere gli abbonamenti" },
      { key: "abb_edit", label: "Registrare rinnovi e pagamenti" },
    ],
  },
  {
    group: "Classi",
    items: [
      { key: "cl_view", label: "Vedere il palinsesto" },
      { key: "cl_edit", label: "Modificare il palinsesto" },
      {
        key: "cl_attendance",
        label: "Confermare le presenze",
        note: "Un coach conferma solo le classi che tiene lui",
      },
      { key: "cl_roster", label: "Iscrivere o rimuovere soci da una classe" },
    ],
  },
  {
    group: "Open Box",
    items: [{ key: "ob_manage", label: "Gestire slot e prenotazioni" }],
  },
  {
    group: "WOD",
    items: [
      { key: "wod_write", label: "Scrivere e pubblicare il WOD" },
      { key: "wod_scores", label: "Modificare i punteggi dei soci" },
    ],
  },
  {
    group: "Comunicazioni",
    items: [
      { key: "com_send", label: "Inviare comunicazioni a tutti" },
      { key: "com_board", label: "Pubblicare avvisi in bacheca" },
    ],
  },
];

const ALL_PERMS = PERMISSIONS.flatMap((g) => g.items.map((i) => i.key));

const PRESETS = {
  coach: ["soci_view", "cl_view", "cl_attendance"],
  admin: ALL_PERMS,
};

function presetFor(role) {
  return Object.fromEntries(
    ALL_PERMS.map((k) => [k, (PRESETS[role] || []).includes(k)])
  );
}



function initials(name) {
  return name
    .split(" ")
    .map((p) => p[0])
    .join("")
    .slice(0, 2)
    .toUpperCase();
}

function Avatar({ name, photo, size = 44 }) {
  // Il socio carica la foto in registrazione; se manca restano le iniziali.
  if (photo) {
    return (
      <img
        src={photo}
        alt={name}
        className="rounded-full object-cover border border-neutral-700 shrink-0"
        style={{ width: size, height: size }}
      />
    );
  }
  return (
    <span
      className="rounded-full bg-gradient-to-br from-neutral-700 to-neutral-900 border border-neutral-700 flex items-center justify-center text-neutral-300 font-semibold shrink-0"
      style={{ width: size, height: size, fontSize: size * 0.32 }}
    >
      {initials(name)}
    </span>
  );
}

function RoleBadge({ role }) {
  return (
    <span className="text-[10px] tracking-wider uppercase text-neutral-300 border border-neutral-700 rounded-md px-2 py-0.5">
      {role}
    </span>
  );
}

function Toggle({ on, onChange, disabled }) {
  return (
    <button
      onClick={onChange}
      disabled={disabled}
      className={`w-11 h-6 rounded-full p-0.5 shrink-0 transition-colors ${
        on ? "bg-emerald-500" : "bg-neutral-700"
      } ${disabled ? "opacity-40" : ""}`}
      aria-pressed={on}
    >
      <span
        className={`block w-5 h-5 rounded-full bg-white transition-transform ${
          on ? "translate-x-5" : ""
        }`}
      />
    </button>
  );
}

function BackLink({ onBack, label }) {
  return (
    <button
      onClick={onBack}
      aria-label={`Torna a ${label}`}
      className="flex items-center gap-2.5 text-neutral-300 hover:text-neutral-50 text-[14px] mb-1 group"
    >
      <span className="w-10 h-10 rounded-2xl border border-neutral-800 bg-neutral-900/60 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 group-hover:border-neutral-600">
        <ChevronLeft size={20} strokeWidth={2} />
      </span>
      {label}
    </button>
  );
}

function ConfirmDialog({ title, body, confirmLabel, danger, onCancel, onConfirm }) {
  useLockBodyScroll();
  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-40 flex items-end sm:items-center justify-center pt-10 sm:p-6">
      <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-black/75" onClick={onCancel} />
      <div
        className="relative w-full sm:max-w-sm bg-neutral-950 border border-neutral-800 rounded-t-3xl sm:rounded-3xl p-5"
        style={{ paddingBottom: "max(1.25rem, env(safe-area-inset-bottom))" }}
      >
        <div className="w-10 h-1 bg-neutral-700 rounded-full mx-auto mb-5 sm:hidden" />
        <h3
          className="text-neutral-50 text-[17px]"
          style={{ fontFamily: "Barlow, sans-serif", fontWeight: 700 }}
        >
          {title}
        </h3>
        <div className="text-[13px] text-neutral-400 mt-2.5 leading-relaxed">{body}</div>
        <div className="flex gap-2.5 mt-6">
          <button
            onClick={onCancel}
            className="flex-1 py-3 rounded-2xl border border-neutral-700 text-neutral-300 text-[13.5px]"
          >
            Annulla
          </button>
          <button
            onClick={onConfirm}
            className={`flex-1 py-3 rounded-2xl text-[13.5px] font-semibold ${
              danger
                ? "bg-red-600 text-white hover:bg-red-500"
                : "bg-neutral-200 text-black hover:bg-white"
            }`}
          >
            {confirmLabel}
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function StaffList({ staff, onOpen, onPromote }) {
  const [filter, setFilter] = useState("tutti");
  const shown = staff.filter((s) => filter === "tutti" || s.role === filter);
  const counts = {
    tutti: staff.length,
    admin: staff.filter((s) => s.role === "admin").length,
    coach: staff.filter((s) => s.role === "coach").length,
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-5">
      <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-4">
        <div>
          <h1
            className="text-neutral-50 text-[28px] leading-tight"
            style={{ fontFamily: "Barlow, sans-serif", fontWeight: 700 }}
          >
            Staff
          </h1>
          <p className="text-[14px] text-neutral-400 mt-1">Gestisci il tuo team.</p>
        </div>
        <button
          onClick={onPromote}
          className="px-4 py-2.5 rounded-2xl bg-neutral-200 text-black text-[13px] font-semibold hover:bg-white shrink-0"
        >
          Promuovi un socio
        </button>
      </div>

      <div className="bg-neutral-900 border border-neutral-800 rounded-2xl p-1 flex">
        {[
          { key: "tutti", label: `Tutti (${counts.tutti})` },
          { key: "admin", label: `Admin (${counts.admin})` },
          { key: "coach", label: `Coach (${counts.coach})` },
        ].map((t) => (
          <button
            key={t.key}
            onClick={() => setFilter(t.key)}
            className={`flex-1 py-2.5 rounded-xl text-[13px] ${
              filter === t.key
                ? "bg-neutral-200 text-black font-semibold"
                : "text-neutral-400"
            }`}
          >
            {t.label}
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>

      <div className="space-y-2.5">
        {shown.map((s) => (
          <button
            key={s.id}
            onClick={() => onOpen(s.id)}
            className="w-full flex items-center gap-3.5 bg-neutral-900/60 border border-neutral-800 rounded-2xl p-3.5 text-left hover:border-neutral-700"
          >
            <Avatar name={s.name} />
            <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <span className="text-[14.5px] text-neutral-50 truncate">{s.name}</span>
                <RoleBadge role={s.role} />
              </div>
              <div className="text-[12px] text-neutral-500 truncate mt-0.5">
                {s.email}
              </div>
            </div>
            <ChevronRight size={17} className="text-neutral-600 shrink-0" />
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>

      <p className="text-[12px] text-neutral-500 leading-relaxed border-t border-neutral-800 pt-4">
        Lo staff si compone promuovendo soci già registrati. Non è possibile creare un
        accesso da questa schermata.
      </p>
    </div>
  );
}

function PromoteScreen({ pool, onCancel, onConfirm }) {
  const [q, setQ] = useState("");
  const [picked, setPicked] = useState(null);
  const [role, setRole] = useState("coach");
  const [confirm, setConfirm] = useState(false);

  const results = pool.filter((m) =>
    m.name.toLowerCase().includes(q.toLowerCase())
  );

  return (
    <div className="space-y-5">
      <BackLink onBack={onCancel} label="Staff" />
      <h1
        className="text-neutral-50 text-[24px]"
        style={{ fontFamily: "Barlow, sans-serif", fontWeight: 700 }}
      >
        Promuovi un socio
      </h1>
      <p className="text-[13px] text-neutral-400 -mt-3">
        Scegli chi far entrare nello staff. I permessi si impostano subito dopo.
      </p>

      <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5 bg-neutral-900 border border-neutral-800 rounded-2xl px-3.5 py-3">
        <Search size={17} className="text-neutral-500 shrink-0" />
        <input
          value={q}
          onChange={(e) => setQ(e.target.value)}
          placeholder="Cerca tra i soci approvati"
          className="bg-transparent flex-1 text-[14px] text-neutral-100 placeholder:text-neutral-600 outline-none"
        />
      </div>

      <div className="space-y-2">
        {results.map((m) => (
          <button
            key={m.id}
            onClick={() => setPicked(m)}
            className={`w-full flex items-center gap-3.5 rounded-2xl p-3.5 text-left border ${
              picked?.id === m.id
                ? "border-neutral-500 bg-neutral-900"
                : "border-neutral-800 bg-neutral-900/60 hover:border-neutral-700"
            }`}
          >
            <Avatar name={m.name} size={40} />
            <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
              <div className="text-[14px] text-neutral-100 truncate">{m.name}</div>
              <div className="text-[12px] text-neutral-500 truncate">
                {m.email} · socio da {m.since}
              </div>
            </div>
            {picked?.id === m.id && (
              <Check size={18} className="text-neutral-100 shrink-0" />
            )}
          </button>
        ))}
        {!results.length && (
          <div className="text-[13px] text-neutral-500 text-center py-6">
            Nessun socio trovato con questo nome.
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {picked && (
        <Card className="p-4 space-y-4">
          <div>
            <div className="text-[11px] text-neutral-500 mb-2">Ruolo da assegnare</div>
            <div className="flex gap-2">
              {["coach", "admin"].map((r) => (
                <button
                  key={r}
                  onClick={() => setRole(r)}
                  className={`flex-1 py-2.5 rounded-xl text-[13px] border capitalize ${
                    role === r
                      ? "border-neutral-500 bg-neutral-800 text-neutral-50 font-semibold"
                      : "border-neutral-800 text-neutral-400"
                  }`}
                >
                  {r}
                </button>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>
          <div className="text-[12px] text-neutral-500 leading-relaxed">
            {role === "coach"
              ? "Preset coach: palinsesto, conferma presenze delle proprie classi, anagrafica in lettura."
              : "Preset admin: tutta la gestione operativa. Non può assegnare ruoli."}
          </div>
          <div className="flex gap-2.5">
            <button
              onClick={onCancel}
              className="flex-1 py-3 rounded-2xl border border-neutral-700 text-neutral-300 text-[13px]"
            >
              Annulla
            </button>
            <button
              onClick={() => (role === "admin" ? setConfirm(true) : onConfirm(picked, role))}
              className="flex-1 py-3 rounded-2xl bg-neutral-200 text-black text-[13px] font-semibold hover:bg-white"
            >
              Aggiungi allo staff
            </button>
          </div>
        </Card>
      )}

      {confirm && picked && (
        <ConfirmDialog
          title={`Rendere ${picked.name} admin?`}
          body="Un admin gestisce soci, abbonamenti, classi, WOD e comunicazioni del box. Non può assegnare ruoli. Puoi cambiare o revocare in qualsiasi momento."
          confirmLabel="Sì, rendi admin"
          onCancel={() => setConfirm(false)}
          onConfirm={() => {
            setConfirm(false);
            onConfirm(picked, role);
          }}
        />
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

function StaffDetail({ member, onBack, onSave, onRemove }) {
  const [role, setRole] = useState(member.role);
  const [perms, setPerms] = useState(member.perms);
  const [saved, setSaved] = useState(false);
  const [dialog, setDialog] = useState(null);

  const dirty =
    role !== member.role ||
    ALL_PERMS.some((k) => perms[k] !== member.perms[k]);

  function changeRole(r) {
    setRole(r);
    setPerms(presetFor(r));
    setSaved(false);
  }

  function toggle(k) {
    setPerms((p) => ({ ...p, [k]: !p[k] }));
    setSaved(false);
  }

  const preset = presetFor(role);
  const custom = ALL_PERMS.filter((k) => perms[k] !== preset[k]).length;

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      <BackLink onBack={onBack} label="Staff" />
      <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
        <Avatar name={member.name} size={64} />
        <div className="min-w-0">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <h1
              className="text-neutral-50 text-[22px] truncate"
              style={{ fontFamily: "Barlow, sans-serif", fontWeight: 700 }}
            >
              {member.name}
            </h1>
            <RoleBadge role={role} />
          </div>
          <div className="text-[13px] text-neutral-400 truncate">{member.email}</div>
          <div className="text-[12px] text-neutral-500">
            Nello staff dal {member.staffSince}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {member.self ? (
        <Card className="p-4 flex gap-3">
          <Info size={16} className="text-neutral-400 mt-0.5 shrink-0" />
          <div className="text-[12.5px] text-neutral-400 leading-relaxed">
            Questo è il tuo profilo. Ruolo e permessi non sono modificabili da qui, per
            evitare di bloccarti fuori dalla gestione.
          </div>
        </Card>
      ) : (
        <div>
          <div className="text-[11px] text-neutral-500 mb-2">Ruolo</div>
          <div className="flex gap-2">
            {["coach", "admin"].map((r) => (
              <button
                key={r}
                onClick={() => changeRole(r)}
                className={`flex-1 py-3 rounded-2xl text-[13.5px] border capitalize ${
                  role === r
                    ? "border-neutral-500 bg-neutral-800 text-neutral-50 font-semibold"
                    : "border-neutral-800 text-neutral-400"
                }`}
              >
                {r}
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
          <p className="text-[11.5px] text-neutral-500 mt-2">
            Cambiare ruolo riapplica il preset e azzera le eccezioni.
          </p>
        </div>
      )}

      <div>
        <div className="text-[11px] text-neutral-500 tracking-wider uppercase mb-2">
          Informazioni
        </div>
        <Card className="divide-y divide-neutral-800">
          {[
            { icon: User, label: "Nome", value: member.name },
            { icon: Mail, label: "Email", value: member.email },
            { icon: Phone, label: "Telefono", value: member.phone },
          ].map(({ icon: Icon, label, value }) => (
            <div key={label} className="flex items-center gap-3 px-4 py-3.5">
              <Icon size={17} className="text-neutral-500 shrink-0" />
              <span className="text-[13px] text-neutral-400 w-20 shrink-0">{label}</span>
              <span className="text-[13.5px] text-neutral-100 truncate">{value}</span>
            </div>
          ))}
        </Card>
        <p className="text-[11.5px] text-neutral-500 mt-2">
          I dati anagrafici si modificano dalla scheda socio.
        </p>
      </div>

      <div>
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-2">
          <span className="text-[11px] text-neutral-500 tracking-wider uppercase">
            Permessi
          </span>
          {custom > 0 && (
            <span className="text-[11px] text-neutral-400">
              {custom} {custom === 1 ? "eccezione" : "eccezioni"} al preset
            </span>
          )}
        </div>
        <p className="text-[12.5px] text-neutral-400 mb-3">
          Il ruolo precompila le caselle. Da qui puoi concedere qualcosa in più a questa
          persona.
        </p>

        <div className="space-y-3">
          {PERMISSIONS.map((g) => (
            <Card key={g.group} className="overflow-hidden">
              <div className="px-4 pt-3.5 pb-1 text-[11px] text-neutral-500 tracking-wider uppercase">
                {g.group}
              </div>
              <div className="divide-y divide-neutral-800">
                {g.items.map((it) => (
                  <div key={it.key} className="flex items-center gap-3 px-4 py-3">
                    <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                      <div className="text-[13.5px] text-neutral-100">{it.label}</div>
                      {it.note && perms[it.key] && role === "coach" && (
                        <div className="text-[11.5px] text-neutral-500 mt-0.5">
                          {it.note}
                        </div>
                      )}
                    </div>
                    <Toggle
                      on={!!perms[it.key]}
                      disabled={member.self}
                      onChange={() => toggle(it.key)}
                    />
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            </Card>
          ))}
        </div>

        <Card className="p-4 mt-3 flex gap-3">
          <Info size={16} className="text-neutral-400 mt-0.5 shrink-0" />
          <div className="text-[12.5px] text-neutral-400 leading-relaxed">
            Assegnazione dei ruoli, impostazioni del box ed eliminazione degli account
            non sono concedibili e restano fuori da questo elenco.
          </div>
        </Card>
      </div>

      {!member.self && (
        <div className="space-y-2.5 pt-1">
          <button
            onClick={() => {
              if (role !== member.role) {
                setDialog("role");
              } else {
                onSave(member.id, role, perms);
                setSaved(true);
              }
            }}
            disabled={!dirty}
            className={`w-full py-3.5 rounded-2xl text-[14px] font-semibold ${
              dirty
                ? "bg-neutral-200 text-black hover:bg-white"
                : "bg-neutral-800 text-neutral-500"
            }`}
          >
            {saved && !dirty ? "Modifiche salvate" : "Salva modifiche"}
          </button>
          <button
            onClick={() => setDialog("remove")}
            className="w-full py-3.5 rounded-2xl border border-red-900/70 text-red-400 text-[14px] flex items-center justify-center gap-2 hover:bg-red-950/30"
          >
            <Trash2 size={16} />
            Rimuovi dallo staff
          </button>
          <p className="text-[11.5px] text-neutral-500 text-center">
            Rimuovendolo torna socio: mantiene account, prenotazioni e progressi.
          </p>
        </div>
      )}

      {dialog === "role" && (
        <ConfirmDialog
          title={
            role === "admin"
              ? `Rendere ${member.name} admin?`
              : `Riportare ${member.name} a coach?`
          }
          body={
            role === "admin"
              ? "Passerà da coach ad admin: gestione completa di soci, abbonamenti, classi, WOD e comunicazioni. Non potrà assegnare ruoli. Le eccezioni impostate finora vengono sostituite dal preset admin."
              : "Perderà la gestione di soci, abbonamenti e comunicazioni. Resteranno palinsesto in lettura e conferma presenze delle sue classi."
          }
          confirmLabel="Conferma"
          onCancel={() => setDialog(null)}
          onConfirm={() => {
            setDialog(null);
            onSave(member.id, role, perms);
            setSaved(true);
          }}
        />
      )}

      {dialog === "remove" && (
        <ConfirmDialog
          title={`Rimuovere ${member.name} dallo staff?`}
          body="Perde subito l'accesso alla console. Resta socio con account, prenotazioni e progressi intatti. Puoi ripromuoverlo quando vuoi."
          confirmLabel="Rimuovi"
          danger
          onCancel={() => setDialog(null)}
          onConfirm={() => {
            setDialog(null);
            onRemove(member.id);
          }}
        />
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

function StaffSection({ members, setMembers }) {
  const [openId, setOpenId] = useState(null);
  const [promoting, setPromoting] = useState(false);

  // Nessun archivio separato: lo staff è chi, fra i soci, ha un ruolo.
  const staff = members.filter((m) => m.role);
  const member = staff.find((s) => s.id === openId);

  // Si promuove solo un socio già approvato e non sospeso.
  const pool = members
    .filter((m) => !m.role && !m.suspended)
    .map((m) => ({ id: m.id, name: m.name, email: m.email, since: m.joined }));

  if (promoting)
    return (
      <PromoteScreen
        pool={pool}
        onCancel={() => setPromoting(false)}
        onConfirm={(m, role) => {
          setMembers((all) =>
            all.map((x) =>
              x.id === m.id
                ? { ...x, role, perms: presetFor(role), staffSince: "oggi" }
                : x
            )
          );
          setPromoting(false);
          setOpenId(m.id);
        }}
      />
    );

  if (member)
    return (
      <StaffDetail
        member={member}
        onBack={() => setOpenId(null)}
        onSave={(id, role, perms) =>
          setMembers((all) =>
            all.map((x) => (x.id === id ? { ...x, role, perms } : x))
          )
        }
        onRemove={(id) => {
          // Torna socio: perde il ruolo, non l'account.
          setMembers((all) =>
            all.map((x) =>
              x.id === id ? { ...x, role: null, perms: null, staffSince: null } : x
            )
          );
          setOpenId(null);
        }}
      />
    );

  return (
    <StaffList staff={staff} onOpen={setOpenId} onPromote={() => setPromoting(true)} />
  );
}

/* ============================================================
   SEZIONE UTENTI
   Lista · Richieste da approvare · Scheda socio · Statistiche
   ============================================================ */

// L'abbonamento si rinnova in reception in un minuto: bastano pochi giorni.
// Il certificato richiede di prenotare una visita, quindi molto più preavviso.
const SUB_ALERT_DAYS = 7;
const CERT_ALERT_DAYS = 30;

const MONTH_SHORT = [
  "gen", "feb", "mar", "apr", "mag", "giu",
  "lug", "ago", "set", "ott", "nov", "dic",
];

function today() {
  const d = new Date();
  d.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0);
  return d;
}

function isoDay(date) {
  const y = date.getFullYear();
  const m = String(date.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, "0");
  const d = String(date.getDate()).padStart(2, "0");
  return `${y}-${m}-${d}`;
}

/** Data ISO spostata di n giorni da oggi. Serve solo ai dati di esempio. */
function offsetDay(n) {
  const d = today();
  d.setDate(d.getDate() + n);
  return isoDay(d);
}

function daysTo(isoDate) {
  if (!isoDate) return null;
  const d = new Date(`${isoDate}T00:00:00`);
  return Math.round((d - today()) / 86400000);
}

function fmtDay(isoDate) {
  if (!isoDate) return "—";
  const d = new Date(`${isoDate}T00:00:00`);
  return `${d.getDate()} ${MONTH_SHORT[d.getMonth()]} ${d.getFullYear()}`;
}

function fmtDayShort(isoDate) {
  if (!isoDate) return "—";
  const d = new Date(`${isoDate}T00:00:00`);
  return `${DAY_FULL[d.getDay()].slice(0, 3)} ${d.getDate()} ${MONTH_SHORT[d.getMonth()]}`;
}

/** "tra 9 giorni", "domani", "oggi", "12 giorni fa" */
function relativeDay(isoDate) {
  const n = daysTo(isoDate);
  if (n === null) return "";
  if (n === 0) return "oggi";
  if (n === 1) return "domani";
  if (n === -1) return "ieri";
  return n > 0 ? `tra ${n} giorni` : `${-n} giorni fa`;
}

/* ---------- Stato del socio ----------
   Lo stato tiene conto di abbonamento E certificato: con il certificato
   scaduto il socio non può prenotare, anche se l'abbonamento è valido. */

const MEMBER_STATE = {
  attivo: { label: "Attivo", dot: "bg-emerald-500", chip: "bg-emerald-500/15 text-emerald-300 border-emerald-500/30" },
  scadenza: { label: "In scadenza", dot: "bg-amber-400", chip: "bg-amber-400/15 text-amber-300 border-amber-400/30" },
  scaduto: { label: "Scaduto", dot: "bg-red-500", chip: "bg-red-500/15 text-red-300 border-red-500/30" },
  incompleto: { label: "Da completare", dot: "bg-sky-400", chip: "bg-sky-400/15 text-sky-300 border-sky-400/30" },
  sospeso: { label: "Sospeso", dot: "bg-neutral-500", chip: "bg-neutral-500/15 text-neutral-400 border-neutral-600/40" },
};

function memberState(m) {
  if (m.suspended) return "sospeso";
  if (!m.cert || !m.sub) return "incompleto";
  const s = daysTo(m.sub.end);
  const c = daysTo(m.cert);
  if (s < 0 || c < 0) return "scaduto";
  if (s <= SUB_ALERT_DAYS || c <= CERT_ALERT_DAYS) return "scadenza";
  return "attivo";
}

/** Età compiuta, calcolata dalla data di nascita. */
function ageFrom(isoDate) {
  if (!isoDate) return null;
  const b = new Date(`${isoDate}T00:00:00`);
  const t = today();
  let a = t.getFullYear() - b.getFullYear();
  const before =
    t.getMonth() < b.getMonth() ||
    (t.getMonth() === b.getMonth() && t.getDate() < b.getDate());
  return before ? a - 1 : a;
}

/** Soci che possono allenarsi: abbonamento e certificato ancora validi.
    È l'unica definizione di "attivo": la usano dashboard e statistiche. */
function isActive(m) {
  const s = memberState(m);
  return s === "attivo" || s === "scadenza";
}

/** Non rinnovati: scaduti da oltre un mese. Sotto questa soglia c'è solo
    chi rinnova con qualche giorno di ritardo, che è la normalità. */
const LAPSED_AFTER_DAYS = 30;

function isLapsed(m) {
  if (m.suspended || !m.sub) return false;
  return daysTo(m.sub.end) < -LAPSED_AFTER_DAYS;
}

/** Motivo per cui il socio non può prenotare. Null se può. */
function blockReason(m) {
  if (m.suspended) return "Accesso sospeso dallo staff.";
  if (!m.membership || !m.membership.paid)
    return "Quota associativa non versata.";
  if (daysTo(m.membership.end) < 0)
    return `Quota associativa scaduta il ${fmtDay(m.membership.end)}.`;
  if (!m.sub) return "Nessun abbonamento registrato.";
  if (!m.cert) return "Certificato medico non ancora registrato.";
  if (daysTo(m.sub.end) < 0) return `Abbonamento scaduto il ${fmtDay(m.sub.end)}.`;
  if (daysTo(m.cert) < 0) return `Certificato medico scaduto il ${fmtDay(m.cert)}.`;
  return null;
}

/* ---------- Catalogo pacchetti ----------
   Questo è solo il punto di partenza: i pacchetti si creano e si modificano
   dalla sezione Abbonamenti. I PREZZI SONO SEGNAPOSTO, vanno sostituiti
   quando saranno validati su concorrenza locale e costi fissi. */

/* `classes: false` significa Open Box e basta. Chi ha le classi ha sempre
   anche l'Open Box: i due livelli non sono alternativi, sono uno dentro
   l'altro, quindi basta un flag e non servono tre opzioni. */
const SUB_TYPES = [
  { key: "libero", kind: "abbonamento", label: "Mensile libero", entries: null, months: 1, price: 70, classes: true, active: true },
  { key: "12", kind: "abbonamento", label: "Mensile 12 ingressi", entries: 12, months: 1, price: 60, classes: true, active: true },
  { key: "8", kind: "abbonamento", label: "Mensile 8 ingressi", entries: 8, months: 1, price: 50, classes: true, active: true },
  { key: "trim", kind: "abbonamento", label: "Trimestrale", entries: null, months: 3, price: 195, classes: true, active: true },
  { key: "sem", kind: "abbonamento", label: "Semestrale", entries: null, months: 6, price: 360, classes: true, active: true },
  { key: "annuale", kind: "abbonamento", label: "Annuale", entries: null, months: 12, price: 660, classes: true, active: true },
  { key: "open", kind: "abbonamento", label: "Solo Open Box", entries: null, months: 1, price: 45, classes: false, active: true },
  { key: "prova", kind: "abbonamento", label: "Primo mese di prova", entries: null, months: 1, price: 50, classes: true, active: true },

  /* Personal: si affianca all'abbonamento, non lo sostituisce. Le sessioni
     si concordano col coach fuori dall'app, quindi il contatore lo scala
     lo staff a mano dalla scheda socio. */
  { key: "pt1", kind: "personal", label: "Personal · sessione singola", sessions: 1, months: 3, price: 45, active: true },
  { key: "pt5", kind: "personal", label: "Personal · 5 sessioni", sessions: 5, months: 6, price: 200, active: true },
  { key: "pt10", kind: "personal", label: "Personal · 10 sessioni", sessions: 10, months: 12, price: 380, active: true },
];

const isSubPlan = (p) => p.kind !== "personal";
const isPersonalPlan = (p) => p.kind === "personal";

/** Cosa apre il pacchetto del socio. Le classi includono sempre l'Open Box. */
function planAccess(m, plans = SUB_TYPES) {
  if (!m.sub) return null;
  const p = plans.find((t) => t.key === m.sub.type);
  return p ? (p.classes ? "classi" : "openbox") : null;
}

function accessLabel(includesClasses) {
  return includesClasses ? "Classi e Open Box" : "Solo Open Box";
}

/* Il catalogo è modificabile a runtime, quindi le etichette non possono
   arrivare da una costante: chi le mostra legge il catalogo dal contesto. */
const PlansContext = React.createContext(SUB_TYPES);
const usePlans = () => React.useContext(PlansContext);

function subLabel(key, plans = SUB_TYPES) {
  return plans.find((t) => t.key === key)?.label || "—";
}

/** Fine dell'anno sportivo (31 agosto) usata dai dati di esempio.
    La versione usata dalla sezione Abbonamenti è sportsYearEnd(). */
function seedSportsYearEnd() {
  const t = today();
  const y = t.getMonth() >= 8 ? t.getFullYear() + 1 : t.getFullYear();
  return `${y}-08-31`;
}

/* ---------- Dati di esempio ---------- */

function makeMember(id, name, email, phone, born, joined, subKey, subEnd, cert, stats, extra = {}) {
  const type = SUB_TYPES.find((t) => t.key === subKey);
  return {
    id,
    name,
    email,
    phone,
    born,
    town: "Maser (TV)",
    photo: null, // il socio la carica in registrazione; se manca restano le iniziali
    joined,
    card: `#${String(id).padStart(5, "0")}`,
    sub: subEnd
      ? {
          type: subKey,
          start: offsetDay(-30),
          end: subEnd,
          paid: extra.subPaid !== false,
          entriesLeft: type?.entries ? Math.max(0, type.entries - stats.month) : null,
          entriesTotal: type?.entries ?? null,
        }
      : null,
    // Quota associativa: distinta dall'abbonamento, scade il 31 agosto.
    membership: extra.membership ?? { paid: true, end: seedSportsYearEnd() },
    // Pacchetto Personal: si affianca all'abbonamento, non lo sostituisce.
    personal: extra.personal ?? null,
    cert,
    suspended: false,
    stats,
    ...extra,
  };
}

const MEMBERS_SEED = [
  // Chi ha un ruolo resta socio a tutti gli effetti: abbonamento, certificato,
  // presenze e progressi. Il ruolo è solo un campo in più su questa scheda.
  makeMember(1, "Simone Zen", "simone@peakfitness.it", "+39 349 111 2233", "1990-04-04", "15 gen 2024", "libero", offsetDay(300), offsetDay(240), { sessions: 388, streak: 52, week: 4.1, rate: 94, month: 16, last: offsetDay(0) }, { role: "admin", perms: presetFor("admin"), staffSince: "15 gen 2024", self: true }),
