/** Cronologia abbonamenti del singolo socio. Chi non ne ha mai avuto uno
    non deve vedere rinnovi che non gli appartengono. */
function subHistory(m) {
  if (!m.sub) return [];
  const out = [
    { type: m.sub.type, start: m.sub.start, end: m.sub.end, note: "Rinnovo in reception" },
  ];
  const previous = seeded(`${m.id}h`, 2);
  let cursor = daysTo(m.sub.start);
  for (let i = 0; i < previous; i++) {
    const end = cursor - 1;
    const start = end - (30 + seeded(`${m.id}l${i}`, 60));
    out.push({
      type: SUB_TYPES[seeded(`${m.id}s${i}`, SUB_TYPES.length - 1)].key,
      start: offsetDay(start),
      end: offsetDay(end),
      note: i === previous - 1 ? "Primo abbonamento" : "Rinnovo in reception",
    });
    cursor = start;
  }
  return out;
}

/** Presenze recenti, generate in modo stabile dall'id del socio. */
function recentAttendance(m) {
  const out = [];
  for (let d = 1; d <= 21 && out.length < 3; d++) {
    const date = offsetDay(-d);
    const weekday = new Date(`${date}T00:00:00`).getDay();
    if (!SCHEDULE_BY_WEEKDAY[weekday]?.length) continue; // box chiuso
    if (seeded(`${m.id}r${d}`, 2) !== 0) continue;
    out.push({
      date,
      kind: seeded(`${m.id}k${d}`, 3) === 0 ? "Open Box" : "Functional",
      present: seeded(`${m.id}p${d}`, 9) > 1,
    });
  }
  return out;
}

/** Prossime prenotazioni, generate in modo stabile dall'id del socio. */
function upcomingBookings(m) {
  // Chi ha abbonamento o certificato scaduto non può prenotarne di nuove,
  // ma quelle già fatte restano valide e disdicibili.
  if (!m.sub) return [];
  const out = [];
  for (let d = 1; d <= 10 && out.length < 2; d++) {
    const date = offsetDay(d);
    const weekday = new Date(`${date}T00:00:00`).getDay();
    const openBox = seeded(`${m.id}o${d}`, 3) === 0;
    const slots = openBox ? OPEN_BOX_BY_WEEKDAY[weekday] : SCHEDULE_BY_WEEKDAY[weekday];
    if (!slots?.length) continue; // box chiuso
    if (seeded(`${m.id}b${d}`, 2) !== 0) continue;
    out.push({
      id: `${m.id}-${d}`,
      date,
      time: slots[seeded(`${m.id}t${d}`, slots.length - 1)],
      kind: openBox ? "Open Box" : "Functional",
    });
  }
  return out;
}

/* ---------- Elementi specifici della sezione ---------- */

function StateChip({ state }) {
  const s = MEMBER_STATE[state];
  return (
    <span className={`text-[11px] rounded-lg px-2 py-0.5 border shrink-0 ${s.chip}`}>
      {s.label}
    </span>
  );
}

/** Barra ingressi: al contrario delle capienze, qui pochi ingressi = allarme. */
function EntriesBar({ left, total }) {
  const pct = total ? Math.min(100, Math.round((left / total) * 100)) : 0;
  const tone = pct <= 15 ? "bg-red-500" : pct <= 35 ? "bg-amber-400" : "bg-emerald-500";
  return (
    <div className="h-1.5 w-full bg-neutral-800 rounded-full overflow-hidden">
      <div className={`h-full rounded-full ${tone}`} style={{ width: `${pct}%` }} />
    </div>
  );
}

function Sheet({ title, subtitle, children, onClose, onConfirm, confirmLabel, disabled }) {
  useLockBodyScroll();
  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-40 flex items-end sm:items-center justify-center pt-10 sm:p-6">
      <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-black/75" onClick={onClose} />

      {/* Colonna a tre fasce: titolo e pulsanti restano fermi, scorre solo
          il contenuto. Con la tastiera aperta i pulsanti non spariscono. */}
      <div className="relative w-full sm:max-w-sm flex flex-col max-h-full bg-neutral-950 border border-neutral-800 rounded-t-3xl sm:rounded-3xl overflow-hidden">
        <div className="shrink-0 px-5 pt-4 pb-1">
          <div className="w-10 h-1 bg-neutral-700 rounded-full mx-auto mb-4 sm:hidden" />
          <h3
            className="text-neutral-50 text-[17px]"
            style={{ fontFamily: "Barlow, sans-serif", fontWeight: 700 }}
          >
            {title}
          </h3>
          {subtitle && (
            <div className="text-[13px] text-neutral-400 mt-2.5 leading-relaxed">{subtitle}</div>
          )}
        </div>

        <div className="flex-1 min-h-0 overflow-y-auto overscroll-contain px-5 py-5">
          {children}
        </div>

        <div
          className="shrink-0 flex gap-2.5 px-5 pt-3 border-t border-neutral-800 bg-neutral-950"
          style={{ paddingBottom: "max(1.25rem, env(safe-area-inset-bottom))" }}
        >
          <button
            onClick={onClose}
            className="flex-1 py-3 rounded-2xl border border-neutral-700 text-neutral-300 text-[13.5px]"
          >
            Annulla
          </button>
          <button
            onClick={onConfirm}
            disabled={disabled}
            className="flex-1 py-3 rounded-2xl text-[13.5px] font-semibold bg-neutral-200 text-black hover:bg-white disabled:opacity-40"
          >
            {confirmLabel}
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function DateField({ label, value, onChange, hint }) {
  return (
    <label className="block">
      <span className="block text-[11px] text-neutral-500 mb-2">{label}</span>
      <input
        type="date"
        value={value || ""}
        onChange={(e) => onChange(e.target.value)}
        className="w-full bg-neutral-900 border border-neutral-800 rounded-2xl px-3.5 py-3 text-[14px] text-neutral-100 outline-none focus:border-neutral-600 [color-scheme:dark]"
      />
      {hint && <span className="block text-[11.5px] text-neutral-500 mt-2">{hint}</span>}
    </label>
  );
}

function TextField({ label, value, onChange, placeholder, type = "text", hint }) {
  return (
    <label className="block">
      <span className="block text-[11px] text-neutral-500 mb-2">{label}</span>
      <input
        type={type}
        value={value}
        placeholder={placeholder}
        onChange={(e) => onChange(e.target.value)}
        className="w-full bg-neutral-900 border border-neutral-800 rounded-2xl px-3.5 py-3 text-[14px] text-neutral-100 placeholder:text-neutral-600 outline-none focus:border-neutral-600"
      />
      {hint && <span className="block text-[11.5px] text-neutral-500 mt-2">{hint}</span>}
    </label>
  );
}

/** Data di nascita a tre tendine: giorno, mese, anno.
    Un calendario costringerebbe a scorrere indietro di trent'anni, e una
    casella di testo lascerebbe scrivere ogni data in un formato diverso. */
function BirthDateField({ label, value, onChange, hint }) {
  const parts = (value || "").split("-");
  const [sel, setSel] = useState({
    y: parts[0] || "",
    m: parts[1] ? String(Number(parts[1])) : "",
    d: parts[2] ? String(Number(parts[2])) : "",
  });

  const thisYear = new Date().getFullYear();
  const years = [];
  for (let y = thisYear - 12; y >= thisYear - 90; y--) years.push(y);

  const daysInMonth =
    sel.m && sel.y ? new Date(Number(sel.y), Number(sel.m), 0).getDate() : 31;

  function update(next) {
    // Se cambiando mese o anno il giorno non esiste più (31 aprile,
    // 29 febbraio fuori dai bisestili) lo riporto all'ultimo valido.
    if (next.m && next.y && next.d) {
      const max = new Date(Number(next.y), Number(next.m), 0).getDate();
      if (Number(next.d) > max) next = { ...next, d: String(max) };
    }
    setSel(next);
    onChange(
      next.d && next.m && next.y
        ? `${next.y}-${String(next.m).padStart(2, "0")}-${String(next.d).padStart(2, "0")}`
        : ""
    );
  }

  const box =
    "bg-neutral-900 border border-neutral-800 rounded-2xl px-3 py-3 text-[14px] text-neutral-100 outline-none focus:border-neutral-600 appearance-none";

  return (
    <div>
      <span className="block text-[11px] text-neutral-500 mb-2">{label}</span>
      <div className="grid grid-cols-[1fr_1.4fr_1.2fr] gap-2">
        <select
          value={sel.d}
          onChange={(e) => update({ ...sel, d: e.target.value })}
          className={box}
        >
          <option value="">Giorno</option>
          {Array.from({ length: daysInMonth }, (_, i) => i + 1).map((d) => (
            <option key={d} value={d}>
              {d}
            </option>
          ))}
        </select>
        <select
          value={sel.m}
          onChange={(e) => update({ ...sel, m: e.target.value })}
          className={box}
        >
          <option value="">Mese</option>
          {MONTH_SHORT.map((name, i) => (
            <option key={name} value={i + 1}>
              {name}
            </option>
          ))}
        </select>
        <select
          value={sel.y}
          onChange={(e) => update({ ...sel, y: e.target.value })}
          className={box}
        >
          <option value="">Anno</option>
          {years.map((y) => (
            <option key={y} value={y}>
              {y}
            </option>
          ))}
        </select>
      </div>
      {hint && <span className="block text-[11.5px] text-neutral-500 mt-2">{hint}</span>}
    </div>
  );
}

function SearchField({ value, onChange, placeholder }) {
  return (
    <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5 bg-neutral-900 border border-neutral-800 rounded-2xl px-3.5 py-3">
      <Search size={17} className="text-neutral-500 shrink-0" />
      <input
        value={value}
        onChange={(e) => onChange(e.target.value)}
        placeholder={placeholder}
        className="bg-transparent flex-1 text-[14px] text-neutral-100 placeholder:text-neutral-600 outline-none min-w-0"
      />
      {value && (
        <button onClick={() => onChange("")} className="text-neutral-500 hover:text-neutral-200">
          <X size={16} />
        </button>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

/* ---------- Lista utenti ---------- */

const MEMBER_FILTERS = [
  { key: "tutti", label: "Tutti" },
  { key: "attivo", label: "Attivi" },
  { key: "scadenza", label: "In scadenza" },
  { key: "scaduto", label: "Scaduti" },
  { key: "incompleto", label: "Da completare" },
  { key: "lapsed", label: "Non rinnovati" },
  { key: "sospeso", label: "Sospesi" },
];

function MemberRow({ m, onOpen }) {
  const plans = usePlans();
  const state = memberState(m);
  const certDays = daysTo(m.cert);
  const subDays = m.sub ? daysTo(m.sub.end) : null;

  // La seconda riga dice sempre qual è la cosa più urgente da sistemare.
  let detail;
  if (!m.sub) detail = "Abbonamento da registrare";
  else if (!m.cert) detail = "Certificato da registrare";
  else if (certDays < 0) detail = `Certificato scaduto il ${fmtDay(m.cert)}`;
  else if (subDays < 0) detail = `Abbonamento scaduto il ${fmtDay(m.sub.end)}`;
  else if (certDays <= CERT_ALERT_DAYS && certDays < subDays)
    detail = `Certificato in scadenza ${fmtDay(m.cert)}`;
  else detail = `Scadenza ${fmtDay(m.sub.end)}`;

  return (
    <button
      onClick={() => onOpen(m.id)}
      className="w-full flex items-center gap-3.5 px-4 py-3.5 text-left hover:bg-neutral-900/60"
    >
      <Avatar name={m.name} photo={m.photo} size={46} />
      <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <span className="text-[14.5px] text-neutral-50 truncate">{m.name}</span>
          {m.role && <RoleBadge role={m.role} />}
          <StateChip state={state} />
        </div>
        <div className="text-[12.5px] text-neutral-400 truncate mt-0.5">
          {m.sub ? subLabel(m.sub.type, plans) : "Nessun abbonamento"}
        </div>
        <div className="text-[11.5px] text-neutral-500 truncate">{detail}</div>
      </div>
      <ChevronRight size={17} className="text-neutral-600 shrink-0" />
    </button>
  );
}

function MemberList({ members, pending, onOpen, onPending, onStats }) {
  const [q, setQ] = useState("");
  const [filter, setFilter] = useState("tutti");

  const counts = useMemo(() => {
    const c = { tutti: members.length, lapsed: members.filter(isLapsed).length };
    for (const k of ["attivo", "scadenza", "scaduto", "incompleto", "sospeso"]) {
      c[k] = members.filter((m) => memberState(m) === k).length;
    }
    return c;
  }, [members]);

  const shown = useMemo(() => {
    const term = q.trim().toLowerCase();
    return members
      .filter((m) => {
        if (filter === "lapsed") return isLapsed(m);
        if (filter !== "tutti" && memberState(m) !== filter) return false;
        return true;
      })
      .filter(
        (m) =>
          !term ||
          m.name.toLowerCase().includes(term) ||
          m.email.toLowerCase().includes(term) ||
          m.card.toLowerCase().includes(term)
      )
      .sort((a, b) => a.name.localeCompare(b.name));
  }, [members, filter, q]);

  return (
    <div className="space-y-5">
      <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-4">
        <div>
          <h1
            className="text-neutral-50 text-[28px] leading-tight"
            style={{ fontFamily: "Barlow, sans-serif", fontWeight: 700 }}
          >
            Utenti
          </h1>
          <p className="text-[14px] text-neutral-400 mt-1">Gestisci i membri del box.</p>
        </div>
        <button
          onClick={onStats}
          className="flex items-center gap-2 px-3.5 py-2.5 rounded-2xl border border-neutral-800 bg-neutral-900/60 text-neutral-200 text-[13px] shrink-0 hover:border-neutral-700"
        >
          <BarChart3 size={16} strokeWidth={1.7} />
          Statistiche
        </button>
      </div>

      {pending > 0 && (
        <button
          onClick={onPending}
          className="w-full flex items-center gap-3.5 bg-neutral-100 rounded-2xl p-4 text-left hover:bg-white"
        >
          <UserPlus size={20} className="text-black shrink-0" strokeWidth={1.9} />
          <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
            <div className="text-[14px] text-black font-semibold">
              {pending} {pending === 1 ? "richiesta in attesa" : "richieste in attesa"}
            </div>
            <div className="text-[12px] text-neutral-700">
              Nessuno di loro può ancora accedere all'app.
            </div>
          </div>
          <ChevronRight size={18} className="text-black shrink-0" />
        </button>
      )}

      <SearchField value={q} onChange={setQ} placeholder="Cerca per nome, email o tessera…" />

      <div className="flex gap-2 overflow-x-auto -mx-5 px-5 pb-1 lg:mx-0 lg:px-0 lg:flex-wrap">
        {MEMBER_FILTERS.map((f) => (
          <button
            key={f.key}
            onClick={() => setFilter(f.key)}
            className={`shrink-0 px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl text-[12.5px] border ${
              filter === f.key
                ? "bg-neutral-200 text-black border-neutral-200 font-semibold"
                : "text-neutral-400 border-neutral-800 bg-neutral-900/60"
            }`}
          >
            {f.label} <span className="opacity-60">({counts[f.key] || 0})</span>
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>

      {shown.length ? (
        <Card className="divide-y divide-neutral-800 overflow-hidden">
          {shown.map((m) => (
            <MemberRow key={m.id} m={m} onOpen={onOpen} />
          ))}
        </Card>
      ) : (
        <div className="border border-dashed border-neutral-800 rounded-2xl p-10 text-center">
          <div className="text-neutral-300 text-[14px]">Nessun socio in questo elenco.</div>
          <div className="text-neutral-600 text-[12px] mt-2">
            Prova a cambiare filtro o a svuotare la ricerca.
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      <p className="text-[12px] text-neutral-500 leading-relaxed border-t border-neutral-800 pt-4">
        I soci si registrano dall'app e compaiono qui dopo la tua approvazione. Non si creano
        account da questa schermata.
      </p>
    </div>
  );
}

/* ---------- Richieste da approvare ---------- */

function PendingList({ pending, onBack, onApprove, onReject }) {
  const [rejecting, setRejecting] = useState(null);

  return (
    <div className="space-y-5">
      <BackLink onBack={onBack} label="Utenti" />
      <div>
        <h1
          className="text-neutral-50 text-[24px]"
          style={{ fontFamily: "Barlow, sans-serif", fontWeight: 700 }}
        >
          Richieste di iscrizione
        </h1>
        <p className="text-[14px] text-neutral-400 mt-1">
          Approva chi si è registrato dall'app. Fino ad allora non può entrare.
        </p>
      </div>

      {pending.length ? (
        <div className="space-y-2.5">
          {pending.map((p) => (
            <Card key={p.id} className="p-4">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-3.5">
                <Avatar name={p.name} photo={p.photo} size={46} />
                <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                  <div className="text-[15px] text-neutral-50 truncate">{p.name}</div>
                  <div className="text-[12px] text-neutral-500 truncate">{p.email}</div>
                  <div className="text-[12px] text-neutral-500">{p.phone}</div>
                </div>
              </div>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[11.5px] text-neutral-500 mt-3">
                <Clock size={13} />
                Richiesta {relativeDay(p.at)}
              </div>
              <div className="flex gap-2.5 mt-4">
                <button
                  onClick={() => setRejecting(p)}
                  className="flex-1 py-2.5 rounded-2xl border border-neutral-700 text-neutral-300 text-[13px]"
                >
                  Rifiuta
                </button>
                <button
                  onClick={() => onApprove(p)}
                  className="flex-1 py-2.5 rounded-2xl bg-neutral-200 text-black text-[13px] font-semibold hover:bg-white"
                >
                  Approva
                </button>
              </div>
            </Card>
          ))}
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="border border-dashed border-neutral-800 rounded-2xl p-12 text-center">
          <Check size={22} className="text-neutral-500 mx-auto" />
          <div className="text-neutral-300 text-[14px] mt-3">Nessuna richiesta in attesa.</div>
          <div className="text-neutral-600 text-[12px] mt-1.5">
            Le nuove registrazioni compariranno qui.
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      <p className="text-[12px] text-neutral-500 leading-relaxed border-t border-neutral-800 pt-4">
        Dopo l'approvazione il socio entra nell'app ma non può prenotare finché non registri
        abbonamento e scadenza del certificato medico.
      </p>

      {rejecting && (
        <ConfirmDialog
          title={`Rifiutare ${rejecting.name}?`}
          body="L'account viene eliminato e la persona riceve una email. Potrà registrarsi di nuovo in futuro."
          confirmLabel="Rifiuta la richiesta"
          danger
          onCancel={() => setRejecting(null)}
          onConfirm={() => {
            onReject(rejecting.id);
            setRejecting(null);
          }}
        />
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

/* ---------- Editor abbonamento e certificato ---------- */

function SubSheet({ member, onClose, onSave }) {
  const plans = usePlans();
  const catalog = plans.filter((t) => t.active && isSubPlan(t));
  const [type, setType] = useState(member.sub?.type || catalog[0]?.key || "libero");
  const [start, setStart] = useState(member.sub?.start || isoDay(today()));
  const [end, setEnd] = useState(member.sub?.end || "");
  const chosen = plans.find((t) => t.key === type);

  return (
    <Sheet
      title={member.sub ? "Rinnova o modifica" : "Registra abbonamento"}
      subtitle="I pagamenti si gestiscono in reception. Qui registri solo tipo e validità."
      confirmLabel="Salva abbonamento"
      disabled={!end}
      onClose={onClose}
      onConfirm={() =>
        onSave({
          type,
          start,
          end,
          entriesTotal: chosen?.entries ?? null,
          entriesLeft: chosen?.entries ?? null,
        })
      }
    >
      <div className="space-y-5">
        <div>
          <div className="text-[11px] text-neutral-500 mb-2">Tipo di abbonamento</div>
          <div className="space-y-2">
            {catalog.map((t) => (
              <button
                key={t.key}
                onClick={() => setType(t.key)}
                className={`w-full flex items-center gap-3 px-3.5 py-3 rounded-2xl border text-left ${
                  type === t.key
                    ? "border-neutral-500 bg-neutral-900"
                    : "border-neutral-800 bg-neutral-900/60"
                }`}
              >
                <span className="flex-1 text-[13.5px] text-neutral-100">{t.label}</span>
                {type === t.key && <Check size={16} className="text-neutral-100" />}
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>

        <DateField label="Inizio" value={start} onChange={setStart} />
        <DateField
          label="Scadenza"
          value={end}
          onChange={setEnd}
          hint="Il socio non potrà prenotare classi con data successiva a questa."
        />
        {end && daysTo(end) < 0 && (
          <div className="flex gap-2.5 bg-amber-400/10 border border-amber-400/25 rounded-2xl px-3.5 py-3 -mt-1">
            <AlertTriangle size={15} className="text-amber-300 mt-0.5 shrink-0" strokeWidth={1.8} />
            <div className="text-[12px] text-amber-200/80 leading-relaxed">
              Questa data è già passata: salvando, il socio risulterà subito scaduto e non potrà
              prenotare.
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </Sheet>
  );
}

function CertSheet({ member, onClose, onSave }) {
  const [date, setDate] = useState(member.cert || "");
  return (
    <Sheet
      title="Scadenza certificato medico"
      subtitle="In app si registra solo la data. La copia cartacea resta in reception."
      confirmLabel="Salva scadenza"
      disabled={!date}
      onClose={onClose}
      onConfirm={() => onSave(date)}
    >
      <DateField
        label="Valido fino al"
        value={date}
        onChange={setDate}
        hint="Il socio riceve un avviso nei 30 giorni prima della scadenza."
      />
    </Sheet>
  );
}

/* ---------- Anagrafica ----------
   Nome e data di nascita li tocca solo l'admin: impediscono che un socio
   passi l'account a un'altra persona e le rivenda l'abbonamento. */

function IdentitySheet({ member, onClose, onSave }) {
  const [name, setName] = useState(member.name);
  const [born, setBorn] = useState(member.born || "");
  const [town, setTown] = useState(member.town);
  const [email, setEmail] = useState(member.email);
  const [phone, setPhone] = useState(member.phone);

  return (
    <Sheet
      title="Modifica anagrafica"
      subtitle="Verifica il documento prima di cambiare nome o data di nascita."
      confirmLabel="Salva"
      disabled={!name.trim()}
      onClose={onClose}
      onConfirm={() =>
        onSave({ name: name.trim(), born, town: town.trim(), email: email.trim(), phone: phone.trim() })
      }
    >
      <div className="space-y-4">
        <TextField label="Nome e cognome" value={name} onChange={setName} />
        <BirthDateField
          label="Data di nascita"
          value={born}
          onChange={setBorn}
          hint="Serve per il tesseramento: va registrata sempre nello stesso modo."
        />
        <TextField label="Comune" value={town} onChange={setTown} />
        <div className="border-t border-neutral-800 pt-4 space-y-4">
          <TextField
            label="Email"
            value={email}
            onChange={setEmail}
            type="email"
            hint="Il socio può cambiarla da solo. Quando lo fa, te lo segnaliamo nelle ultime attività."
          />
          <TextField label="Telefono" value={phone} onChange={setPhone} />
        </div>
      </div>
    </Sheet>
  );
}

/* ---------- Scheda socio ---------- */

function DataRow({ label, value, extra, tone = "text-neutral-100" }) {
  return (
    <div className="flex items-baseline justify-between gap-4 px-4 py-3">
      <span className="text-[13px] text-neutral-400 shrink-0">{label}</span>
      <span className="flex items-baseline gap-2 min-w-0">
        {extra && <span className="text-[11.5px] text-neutral-500 shrink-0">{extra}</span>}
        <span className={`text-[13.5px] truncate ${tone}`}>{value}</span>
      </span>
    </div>
  );
}

function InfoRow({ icon: Icon, children }) {
  return (
    <div className="flex items-center gap-3 px-4 py-3 text-[13px] text-neutral-300">
      <Icon size={15} className="text-neutral-500 shrink-0" strokeWidth={1.7} />
      <span className="truncate">{children}</span>
    </div>
  );
}

function MemberStat({ value, label, icon: Icon }) {
  return (
    <Card className="p-3.5">
      <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-neutral-500 mb-2">
        <Icon size={13} strokeWidth={1.8} />
        <span className="text-[11px]">{label}</span>
      </div>
      <Display size="text-[22px]">{value}</Display>
    </Card>
  );
}

function MemberTabs({ value, onChange }) {
  const tabs = [
    { key: "panoramica", label: "Panoramica" },
    { key: "prenotazioni", label: "Prenotazioni" },
    { key: "storico", label: "Storico" },
  ];
  return (
    <div className="bg-neutral-900 border border-neutral-800 rounded-2xl p-1 flex">
      {tabs.map((t) => (
        <button
          key={t.key}
          onClick={() => onChange(t.key)}
          className={`flex-1 py-2.5 rounded-xl text-[13px] transition-colors ${
            value === t.key ? "bg-neutral-200 text-black font-semibold" : "text-neutral-400"
          }`}
        >
          {t.label}
        </button>
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}

function MemberDetail({ member, onBack, onUpdate, onDelete }) {
  const plans = usePlans();
  const [tab, setTab] = useState("panoramica");
  const [sheet, setSheet] = useState(null);
  const [history, setHistory] = useState(() => subHistory(member));
  const [bookings, setBookings] = useState(() => upcomingBookings(member));
  const [cancelling, setCancelling] = useState(null);

  const state = memberState(member);
  const blocked = blockReason(member);
  const certDays = daysTo(member.cert);
  const subDays = member.sub ? daysTo(member.sub.end) : null;
  const attendance = recentAttendance(member);

  return (
    <div className="space-y-5">
      <BackLink onBack={onBack} label="Utenti" />

      <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
        <Avatar name={member.name} photo={member.photo} size={68} />
        <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 flex-wrap">
            <h1
              className="text-neutral-50 text-[22px]"
              style={{ fontFamily: "Barlow, sans-serif", fontWeight: 700 }}
            >
              {member.name}
            </h1>
            {member.role && <RoleBadge role={member.role} />}
            <StateChip state={state} />
          </div>
          <div className="text-[12.5px] text-neutral-500 mt-1">
            Tessera {member.card}
            {member.role && ` · nello staff dal ${member.staffSince}`}
          </div>
          <div className="text-[12.5px] text-neutral-500">Socio dal {member.joined}</div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {blocked && (
        <div className="flex gap-3 bg-red-950/40 border border-red-900/60 rounded-2xl p-4">
          <AlertTriangle size={17} className="text-red-400 mt-0.5 shrink-0" strokeWidth={1.9} />
          <div>
            <div className="text-[13.5px] text-red-300 font-semibold">
              {member.suspended ? "Non può accedere all'app" : "Non può prenotare"}
            </div>
            <div className="text-[12.5px] text-red-200/70 mt-1 leading-relaxed">{blocked}</div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      <MemberTabs value={tab} onChange={setTab} />

      {tab === "panoramica" && (
        <div className="space-y-5">
          <section>
            <SectionHead>Abbonamento</SectionHead>
            <Card className="divide-y divide-neutral-800 overflow-hidden">
              {member.sub ? (
                <>
                  <DataRow label="Tipo" value={subLabel(member.sub.type, plans)} />
                  <DataRow
                    label="Accesso"
                    value={accessLabel(planAccess(member, plans) === "classi")}
                  />
                  <DataRow label="Data inizio" value={fmtDay(member.sub.start)} />
                  <DataRow
                    label="Scadenza"
                    value={fmtDay(member.sub.end)}
                    extra={`(${relativeDay(member.sub.end)})`}
                    tone={subDays < 0 ? "text-red-400" : subDays <= SUB_ALERT_DAYS ? "text-amber-300" : "text-neutral-100"}
                  />
                  <DataRow
                    label="Ingressi residui"
                    value={
                      member.sub.entriesTotal
                        ? `${member.sub.entriesLeft} di ${member.sub.entriesTotal}`
                        : "Illimitati"
                    }
                  />
                  {member.sub.entriesTotal && (
                    <div className="px-4 py-3.5">
                      <EntriesBar left={member.sub.entriesLeft} total={member.sub.entriesTotal} />
                    </div>
                  )}
                </>
              ) : (
                <div className="px-4 py-4 text-[13.5px] text-neutral-400">
                  Nessun abbonamento registrato.
                </div>
              )}
            </Card>
          </section>

          <section>
            <SectionHead
              action={member.personal ? "Cambia" : "Assegna"}
              onAction={() => setSheet("personal")}
            >
              Personal
            </SectionHead>
            <Card className="divide-y divide-neutral-800 overflow-hidden">
              {member.personal ? (
                <>
                  <DataRow label="Pacchetto" value={subLabel(member.personal.type, plans)} />
                  <DataRow
                    label="Sessioni residue"
                    value={`${member.personal.left} di ${member.personal.total}`}
                    tone={member.personal.left === 0 ? "text-neutral-400" : "text-neutral-100"}
                  />
                  <DataRow
                    label="Valido fino al"
                    value={fmtDay(member.personal.end)}
                    extra={`(${relativeDay(member.personal.end)})`}
                    tone={daysTo(member.personal.end) < 0 ? "text-red-400" : "text-neutral-100"}
                  />
                  <div className="px-4 py-3.5">
                    <EntriesBar left={member.personal.left} total={member.personal.total} />
                  </div>
                  <div className="px-4 py-3.5">
                    <button
                      onClick={() =>
                        onUpdate({
                          ...member,
                          personal: {
                            ...member.personal,
                            left: Math.max(0, member.personal.left - 1),
                          },
                        })
                      }
                      disabled={personalState(member) !== "ok"}
                      className="w-full py-2.5 rounded-xl border border-neutral-700 text-neutral-200 text-[13px] hover:border-neutral-600 disabled:opacity-40"
                    >
                      Scala una sessione
                    </button>
                    <div className="text-[11.5px] text-neutral-500 leading-relaxed mt-2.5">
                      Le sessioni si concordano col coach fuori dall'app: il
                      contatore lo aggiorna lo staff dopo l'allenamento.
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </>
              ) : (
                <div className="px-4 py-4 text-[13.5px] text-neutral-400">
                  Nessun pacchetto Personal.
                </div>
              )}
            </Card>
          </section>

          <section>
            <SectionHead>Certificato medico</SectionHead>
            <Card className="divide-y divide-neutral-800 overflow-hidden">
              <DataRow
                label="Scadenza"
                value={member.cert ? fmtDay(member.cert) : "Non registrato"}
                extra={member.cert ? `(${relativeDay(member.cert)})` : ""}
                tone={
                  !member.cert
                    ? "text-sky-300"
                    : certDays < 0
                    ? "text-red-400"
                    : certDays <= CERT_ALERT_DAYS
                    ? "text-amber-300"
                    : "text-neutral-100"
                }
              />
              <div className="px-4 py-3 text-[11.5px] text-neutral-500 leading-relaxed">
                In app si registra solo la data. La copia cartacea resta in reception.
              </div>
            </Card>
          </section>

          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2.5">
            <button
              onClick={() => setSheet("sub")}
              className="py-3 rounded-2xl bg-neutral-200 text-black text-[13px] font-semibold hover:bg-white"
            >
              {member.sub ? "Rinnova abbonamento" : "Registra abbonamento"}
            </button>
            <button
              onClick={() => setSheet("cert")}
              className="py-3 rounded-2xl border border-neutral-700 text-neutral-200 text-[13px] hover:border-neutral-500"
            >
              {member.cert ? "Aggiorna certificato" : "Registra certificato"}
            </button>
          </div>

          <section>
            <SectionHead action="Modifica" onAction={() => setSheet("identity")}>
              Informazioni personali
            </SectionHead>
            <Card className="divide-y divide-neutral-800 overflow-hidden">
              <InfoRow icon={User}>{member.name}</InfoRow>
              <InfoRow icon={CalendarDays}>
                {member.born
                  ? `${fmtDay(member.born)} · ${ageFrom(member.born)} anni`
                  : "Data di nascita da registrare"}
              </InfoRow>
              <InfoRow icon={MapPin}>{member.town}</InfoRow>
              <InfoRow icon={Mail}>{member.email}</InfoRow>
              <InfoRow icon={Phone}>{member.phone}</InfoRow>
            </Card>
            {member.emailChangedAt && (
              <div className="flex gap-2.5 mt-2.5 bg-amber-400/10 border border-amber-400/25 rounded-2xl px-3.5 py-3">
                <Info size={15} className="text-amber-300 mt-0.5 shrink-0" strokeWidth={1.8} />
                <div className="min-w-0">
                  <div className="text-[12px] text-amber-200/80 leading-relaxed">
                    Il socio ha cambiato email {relativeDay(member.emailChangedAt)}. Se non ti
                    risulta, verifica che dietro l'account ci sia sempre la stessa persona.
                  </div>
                  <button
                    onClick={() => onUpdate({ ...member, emailChangedAt: null })}
                    className="text-[12px] text-amber-300 underline underline-offset-2 mt-2"
                  >
                    Segna come verificato
                  </button>
                </div>
              </div>
            )}
            <p className="text-[11.5px] text-neutral-500 mt-2.5 leading-relaxed">
              Il socio può cambiare da solo soltanto email, telefono, password e foto. Nome e data di
              nascita li modifichi tu, dopo aver visto un documento.
            </p>
          </section>

          <section className="border-t border-neutral-800 pt-5 space-y-2.5">
            {member.self ? (
              <p className="text-[12px] text-neutral-500 leading-relaxed">
                Questa è la tua scheda: non puoi sospenderti né eliminarti da solo.
              </p>
            ) : (
            <>
            <button
              onClick={() => setSheet(member.suspended ? "unsuspend" : "suspend")}
              className="w-full flex items-center gap-3 px-4 py-3.5 rounded-2xl border border-neutral-800 text-left hover:border-neutral-700"
            >
              <Ban size={17} className="text-neutral-400 shrink-0" strokeWidth={1.7} />
              <span className="flex-1 text-[13.5px] text-neutral-200">
                {member.suspended ? "Riattiva l'accesso" : "Sospendi l'accesso"}
              </span>
            </button>
            <button
              onClick={() => setSheet("delete")}
              className="w-full flex items-center gap-3 px-4 py-3.5 rounded-2xl border border-neutral-800 text-left hover:border-red-900"
            >
              <Trash2 size={17} className="text-red-400 shrink-0" strokeWidth={1.7} />
              <span className="flex-1 text-[13.5px] text-red-400">Elimina il socio</span>
            </button>
            </>
            )}
          </section>
        </div>
      )}

      {tab === "prenotazioni" && (
        <div className="space-y-5">
          {blocked && bookings.length > 0 && (
            <div className="flex gap-3 bg-neutral-900/60 border border-neutral-800 rounded-2xl p-4">
              <Info size={16} className="text-neutral-400 mt-0.5 shrink-0" strokeWidth={1.8} />
              <div className="text-[12.5px] text-neutral-400 leading-relaxed">
                Non può prenotarne di nuove, ma queste restano valide e le puoi disdire tu.
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          <section>
            <SectionHead>Prossime prenotazioni</SectionHead>
            {bookings.length ? (
              <Card className="divide-y divide-neutral-800 overflow-hidden">
                {bookings.map((b) => (
                  <div key={b.id} className="flex items-center gap-3.5 px-4 py-3.5">
                    <span
                      className="text-neutral-100 text-[14px] w-14 shrink-0"
                      style={{ fontFamily: "Barlow, sans-serif", fontWeight: 600 }}
                    >
                      {b.time}
                    </span>
                    <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                      <div className="text-[13.5px] text-neutral-200">{b.kind}</div>
                      <div className="text-[12px] text-neutral-500">{fmtDayShort(b.date)}</div>
                    </div>
                    <button
                      onClick={() => setCancelling(b)}
                      className="text-[12.5px] text-neutral-400 hover:text-red-400 shrink-0"
                    >
                      Disdici
                    </button>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </Card>
            ) : (
              <div className="border border-dashed border-neutral-800 rounded-2xl p-8 text-center">
                <div className="text-neutral-400 text-[13.5px]">Nessuna prenotazione futura.</div>
              </div>
            )}
          </section>

          <section>
            <SectionHead>Ultimi accessi</SectionHead>
            <Card className="divide-y divide-neutral-800 overflow-hidden">
              {attendance.map((a, i) => (
                <div key={i} className="flex items-center gap-3.5 px-4 py-3.5">
                  <CalendarDays size={15} className="text-neutral-500 shrink-0" strokeWidth={1.7} />
                  <span className="text-[13px] text-neutral-300 w-24 shrink-0">
                    {fmtDayShort(a.date)}
                  </span>
                  <span className="flex-1 text-[13px] text-neutral-400 truncate">{a.kind}</span>
                  <span
                    className={`text-[12px] flex items-center gap-1 shrink-0 ${
                      a.present ? "text-emerald-400" : "text-neutral-500"
                    }`}
                  >
                    {a.present ? <Check size={13} /> : <X size={13} />}
                    {a.present ? "Presente" : "Assente"}
                  </span>
                </div>
              ))}
            </Card>
            <p className="text-[11.5px] text-neutral-500 mt-2.5 leading-relaxed">
              Le presenze contano solo se confermate da uno staff a fine classe.
            </p>
          </section>
        </div>
      )}

      {tab === "storico" && (
        <div className="space-y-5">
          <section>
            <SectionHead>Andamento</SectionHead>
            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-2.5">
              <MemberStat value={member.stats.sessions} label="Sessioni totali" icon={Activity} />
              <MemberStat value={`${member.stats.streak} sett.`} label="Streak" icon={Flame} />
              <MemberStat
                value={member.stats.week.toFixed(1)}
                label="Media settimanale"
                icon={TrendingUp}
              />
              <MemberStat value={`${member.stats.rate}%`} label="Tasso di presenza" icon={Check} />
            </div>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-[12.5px] text-neutral-500 mt-3">
              <Clock size={14} />
              {member.stats.last
                ? `Ultima presenza confermata ${relativeDay(member.stats.last)}`
                : "Nessuna presenza confermata"}
            </div>
          </section>

          <section>
            <SectionHead>Cronologia abbonamenti</SectionHead>
            {!history.length ? (
              <div className="border border-dashed border-neutral-800 rounded-2xl p-8 text-center">
                <div className="text-neutral-400 text-[13.5px]">
                  Nessun abbonamento ancora registrato.
                </div>
              </div>
            ) : (
            <Card className="divide-y divide-neutral-800 overflow-hidden">
              {history.map((h, i) => (
                <div key={i} className="px-4 py-3.5">
                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
                    <span className="text-[13.5px] text-neutral-100">{subLabel(h.type, plans)}</span>
                    {i === 0 && daysTo(h.end) >= 0 && (
                      <span className="text-[11px] text-emerald-400 shrink-0">In corso</span>
                    )}
                  </div>
                  <div className="text-[12px] text-neutral-500 mt-1">
                    {fmtDay(h.start)} → {fmtDay(h.end)} · {h.note}
                  </div>
                </div>
              ))}
            </Card>
            )}
          </section>
        </div>
      )}

      {sheet === "sub" && (
        <SubSheet
          member={member}
          onClose={() => setSheet(null)}
          onSave={(sub) => {
            setHistory((h) => [
              {
                type: sub.type,
                start: sub.start,
                end: sub.end,
                note: h.length ? "Rinnovo in reception" : "Primo abbonamento",
              },
              ...h,
            ]);
            onUpdate({ ...member, sub });
            setSheet(null);
          }}
        />
      )}

      {sheet === "personal" && (
        <PersonalSheet
          member={member}
          plans={plans}
          onClose={() => setSheet(null)}
          onSave={(personal) => {
            onUpdate({ ...member, personal });
            setSheet(null);
          }}
        />
      )}

      {sheet === "identity" && (
        <IdentitySheet
          member={member}
          onClose={() => setSheet(null)}
          onSave={(fields) => {
            onUpdate({ ...member, ...fields });
            setSheet(null);
          }}
        />
      )}

      {sheet === "cert" && (
        <CertSheet
          member={member}
          onClose={() => setSheet(null)}
          onSave={(date) => {
            onUpdate({ ...member, cert: date });
            setSheet(null);
          }}
        />
      )}

      {cancelling && (
        <ConfirmDialog
          title="Disdire la prenotazione?"
          body={`${cancelling.kind} di ${fmtDayShort(cancelling.date)} alle ${cancelling.time}. Il posto torna disponibile e il socio riceve una notifica.`}
          confirmLabel="Disdici"
          danger
          onCancel={() => setCancelling(null)}
          onConfirm={() => {
            setBookings((b) => b.filter((x) => x.id !== cancelling.id));
            setCancelling(null);
          }}
        />
      )}

      {sheet === "suspend" && (
        <ConfirmDialog
          title={`Sospendere ${member.name}?`}
          body="Non potrà accedere all'app né prenotare. Storico, presenze e PR restano salvati. Puoi riattivarlo quando vuoi."
          confirmLabel="Sospendi"
          onCancel={() => setSheet(null)}
          onConfirm={() => {
            onUpdate({ ...member, suspended: true });
            setSheet(null);
          }}
        />
      )}

      {sheet === "unsuspend" && (
        <ConfirmDialog
          title={`Riattivare ${member.name}?`}
          body="Torna ad accedere all'app. Potrà prenotare solo se abbonamento e certificato sono validi."
          confirmLabel="Riattiva"
          onCancel={() => setSheet(null)}
          onConfirm={() => {
            onUpdate({ ...member, suspended: false });
            setSheet(null);
          }}
        />
      )}

      {sheet === "delete" && (
        <ConfirmDialog
          title={`Eliminare ${member.name}?`}
          body={
            member.role
              ? `Fa parte dello staff come ${member.role}: perderà anche l'accesso alla console. Vengono cancellati account, prenotazioni, presenze, PR e punteggi. L'operazione non si può annullare.`
              : "Vengono cancellati account, prenotazioni, presenze, PR e punteggi. L'operazione non si può annullare. Se ti serve solo bloccarlo, usa Sospendi."
          }
          confirmLabel="Elimina definitivamente"
          danger
          onCancel={() => setSheet(null)}
          onConfirm={() => {
            onDelete(member.id);
            setSheet(null);
          }}
        />
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

const DONUT_COLORS = ["#34d399", "#60a5fa", "#fbbf24", "#a78bfa", "#737373"];

const NEW_MEMBERS_BY_PERIOD = {
