function StatCard({ icon: Icon, label, value, delta, tone = "text-neutral-500" }) {
  return (
    <Card className="p-4">
      <Icon size={17} className={tone} strokeWidth={1.7} />
      <div className="text-[12px] text-neutral-400 mt-2.5 leading-snug">{label}</div>
      <div className="flex items-end justify-between gap-2 mt-1.5">
        <Display size="text-[26px]">{value}</Display>
        {delta !== undefined && delta !== null && (
          <span className={`text-[11.5px] ${delta >= 0 ? "text-emerald-400" : "text-red-400"}`}>
            {delta >= 0 ? "↑" : "↓"} {Math.abs(delta)}%
          </span>
        )}
      </div>
    </Card>
  );
}

function Donut({ slices, total }) {
  const r = 52;
  const c = 2 * Math.PI * r;
  let acc = 0;
  return (
    <svg viewBox="0 0 140 140" className="w-[140px] h-[140px] shrink-0">
      <g transform="rotate(-90 70 70)">
        {slices.map((s, i) => {
          const len = total ? (s.value / total) * c : 0;
          const el = (
            <circle
              key={i}
              cx="70"
              cy="70"
              r={r}
              fill="none"
              stroke={DONUT_COLORS[i % DONUT_COLORS.length]}
              strokeWidth="16"
              strokeDasharray={`${len} ${c - len}`}
              strokeDashoffset={-acc}
            />
          );
          acc += len;
          return el;
        })}
      </g>
      <text
        x="70"
        y="66"
        textAnchor="middle"
        fill="#fafafa"
        style={{ fontFamily: "Barlow, sans-serif", fontWeight: 800, fontSize: 24 }}
      >
        {total}
      </text>
      <text x="70" y="84" textAnchor="middle" fill="#a3a3a3" style={{ fontSize: 11 }}>
        soci
      </text>
    </svg>
  );
}

function NewMembersChart({ data }) {
  const max = Math.max(...data.map((d) => d.value), 1);
  return (
    <Card className="p-4">
      <div className="flex items-end gap-2 h-32">
        {data.map((d) => (
          <div key={d.label} className="flex-1 flex flex-col items-center gap-2">
            <div className="w-full flex-1 flex items-end">
              <div
                className="w-full bg-emerald-500/70 rounded-t-md"
                style={{ height: `${(d.value / max) * 100}%` }}
              />
            </div>
            <span className="text-[11px] text-neutral-500">{d.label}</span>
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>
    </Card>
  );
}

function UserStats({ members, onBack }) {
  const plans = usePlans();
  const [period, setPeriod] = useState("30g");

  const s = useMemo(() => {
    const byState = (k) => members.filter((m) => memberState(m) === k).length;
    const certIssues = members.filter((m) => !m.cert || daysTo(m.cert) < 0).length;
    const dist = plans.filter(isSubPlan).map((t) => ({
      label: t.label,
      value: members.filter((m) => m.sub?.type === t.key).length,
    })).filter((d) => d.value > 0);
    const withSub = dist.reduce((a, b) => a + b.value, 0);
    return {
      attivi: members.filter(isActive).length,
      scadenza: byState("scadenza"),
      nonRinnovati: members.filter(isLapsed).length,
      incompleti: byState("incompleto"),
      certIssues,
      dist,
      withSub,
    };
  }, [members, plans]);

  const renewalRate = s.attivi + s.nonRinnovati
    ? Math.round((s.attivi / (s.attivi + s.nonRinnovati)) * 100)
    : 0;

  return (
    <div className="space-y-5">
      <BackLink onBack={onBack} label="Utenti" />
      <div>
        <h1
          className="text-neutral-50 text-[24px]"
          style={{ fontFamily: "Barlow, sans-serif", fontWeight: 700 }}
        >
          Statistiche utenti
        </h1>
        <p className="text-[14px] text-neutral-400 mt-1">Come si muove la base soci.</p>
      </div>

      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-2.5">
        <StatCard icon={Users} label="Soci in regola" value={s.attivi} delta={12} />
        <StatCard icon={Clock} label="In scadenza" value={s.scadenza} delta={4} tone="text-amber-400" />
        <StatCard icon={UserCog} label="Non rinnovati" value={s.nonRinnovati} delta={-2} tone="text-red-400" />
        <StatCard icon={TrendingUp} label="Tasso di rinnovo" value={`${renewalRate}%`} delta={6} />
        <StatCard
          icon={FileText}
          label="Certificati da sistemare"
          value={s.certIssues}
          tone="text-red-400"
        />
        <StatCard icon={UserPlus} label="Schede da completare" value={s.incompleti} tone="text-sky-400" />
      </div>

      <section>
        <SectionHead>Distribuzione abbonamenti</SectionHead>
        <Card className="p-4 flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-center gap-5">
          <div className="flex-1 w-full space-y-2.5">
            {s.dist.map((d, i) => (
              <div key={d.label} className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
                <span
                  className="w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-sm shrink-0"
                  style={{ background: DONUT_COLORS[i % DONUT_COLORS.length] }}
                />
                <span className="flex-1 text-[13px] text-neutral-300 truncate">{d.label}</span>
                <span className="text-[13px] text-neutral-400 shrink-0">
                  {d.value}{" "}
                  <span className="text-neutral-600">
                    ({Math.round((d.value / s.withSub) * 100)}%)
                  </span>
                </span>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
          <Donut slices={s.dist} total={s.withSub} />
        </Card>
      </section>

      <section>
        <SectionHead>Andamento nuovi iscritti</SectionHead>
        <div className="bg-neutral-900 border border-neutral-800 rounded-2xl p-1 flex mb-2.5">
          {STAT_PERIODS.map((p) => (
            <button
              key={p.key}
              onClick={() => setPeriod(p.key)}
              className={`flex-1 py-2 rounded-xl text-[12px] transition-colors ${
                period === p.key ? "bg-neutral-200 text-black font-semibold" : "text-neutral-400"
              }`}
            >
              {p.label}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
        <NewMembersChart data={NEW_MEMBERS_BY_PERIOD[period]} />
      </section>

      <p className="text-[12px] text-neutral-500 leading-relaxed border-t border-neutral-800 pt-4">
        I sei riquadri qui sopra sono una fotografia di adesso, non dipendono dal periodo. Le
        variazioni percentuali e l'andamento degli iscritti sono ancora dati di esempio: diventano
        veri quando colleghiamo il database.
      </p>
    </div>
  );
}

/* ---------- Sezione Utenti ---------- */

function UtentiSection({ members, setMembers, pending, setPending, onToast }) {
  const [view, setView] = useState("list");
  const [openId, setOpenId] = useState(null);

  const member = members.find((m) => m.id === openId);

  function approve(p) {
    const created = {
      id: p.id,
      name: p.name,
      email: p.email,
      phone: p.phone,
      born: "",
      town: "—",
      photo: p.photo,
      joined: fmtDay(isoDay(today())),
      card: `#${String(p.id).padStart(5, "0")}`,
      sub: null,
      cert: null,
      suspended: false,
      stats: { sessions: 0, streak: 0, week: 0, rate: 0, month: 0, last: null },
    };
    setMembers((all) => [...all, created]);
    setPending((q) => q.filter((x) => x.id !== p.id));
    setOpenId(created.id);
    setView("detail");
    onToast(`${p.name} è stato approvato. Ora registra abbonamento e certificato.`);
  }

  if (view === "stats") {
    return <UserStats members={members} onBack={() => setView("list")} />;
  }

  if (view === "pending") {
    return (
      <PendingList
        pending={pending}
        onBack={() => setView("list")}
        onApprove={approve}
        onReject={(id) => {
          setPending((q) => q.filter((x) => x.id !== id));
          onToast("Richiesta rifiutata.");
        }}
      />
    );
  }

  if (view === "detail" && member) {
    return (
      <MemberDetail
        key={member.id}
        member={member}
        onBack={() => {
          setOpenId(null);
          setView("list");
        }}
        onUpdate={(next) => {
          setMembers((all) => all.map((m) => (m.id === next.id ? next : m)));
          onToast("Modifiche salvate.");
        }}
        onDelete={(id) => {
          setMembers((all) => all.filter((m) => m.id !== id));
          setOpenId(null);
          setView("list");
          onToast("Socio eliminato.");
        }}
      />
    );
  }

  return (
    <MemberList
      members={members}
      pending={pending.length}
      onOpen={(id) => {
        setOpenId(id);
        setView("detail");
      }}
      onPending={() => setView("pending")}
      onStats={() => setView("stats")}
    />
  );
}

/* ============================================================
   SEZIONE ABBONAMENTI
   Catalogo pacchetti · Scadenze e rinnovi · Quote associative
   ============================================================ */

/** L'anno sportivo di un'ASD va dal 1 settembre al 31 agosto.
    La quota associativa vale fino a quella data, per tutti insieme. */
function sportsYearEnd(from = today()) {
  const y = from.getMonth() >= 8 ? from.getFullYear() + 1 : from.getFullYear();
  return `${y}-08-31`;
}

/** Scadenza di un abbonamento: il giorno prima dello stesso giorno del mese
    di arrivo. Inizio 1 gennaio + 1 mese = scadenza 31 gennaio. */
function addMonths(iso, months) {
  const d = new Date(`${iso}T00:00:00`);
  const day = d.getDate();
  d.setMonth(d.getMonth() + months);
  if (d.getDate() < day) d.setDate(0);
  d.setDate(d.getDate() - 1);
  return isoDay(d);
}

/** Un pacchetto Personal è esaurito quando finiscono le sessioni,
    scaduto quando passa la data. Sono due cose diverse. */
function personalState(m) {
  if (!m.personal) return "nessuno";
  if (m.personal.left <= 0) return "esaurito";
  if (daysTo(m.personal.end) < 0) return "scaduto";
  return "ok";
}

const PERSONAL_CHIP = {
  ok: { label: "Attivo", cls: "bg-emerald-500/15 text-emerald-300 border-emerald-500/30" },
  esaurito: { label: "Esaurito", cls: "bg-neutral-500/15 text-neutral-400 border-neutral-600/40" },
  scaduto: { label: "Scaduto", cls: "bg-red-500/15 text-red-300 border-red-500/30" },
};

function membershipState(m) {
  if (!m.membership || !m.membership.paid) return "mancante";
  if (daysTo(m.membership.end) < 0) return "scaduta";
  return "ok";
}

const MEMBERSHIP_CHIP = {
  ok: { label: "In regola", cls: "bg-emerald-500/15 text-emerald-300 border-emerald-500/30" },
  scaduta: { label: "Scaduta", cls: "bg-red-500/15 text-red-300 border-red-500/30" },
  mancante: { label: "Da versare", cls: "bg-amber-400/15 text-amber-300 border-amber-400/30" },
};

function planById(plans, id) {
  return plans.find((p) => p.key === id) || null;
}

function euro(n) {
  return `${Number(n || 0).toLocaleString("it-IT")} €`;
}

/* ---------- Tab della sezione ---------- */

function AbbTabs({ value, onChange, counts }) {
  const tabs = [
    { key: "pacchetti", label: "Pacchetti" },
    { key: "scadenze", label: "Scadenze", badge: counts.scadenze },
    { key: "quote", label: "Quote", badge: counts.quote },
  ];
  return (
    <div className="bg-neutral-900 border border-neutral-800 rounded-2xl p-1 flex mb-5">
      {tabs.map((t) => (
        <button
          key={t.key}
          onClick={() => onChange(t.key)}
          className={`flex-1 py-2.5 rounded-xl text-[13.5px] flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 transition-colors ${
            value === t.key
              ? "bg-neutral-300 text-black font-semibold"
              : "text-neutral-400"
          }`}
        >
          {t.label}
          {t.badge > 0 && (
            <span
              className={`text-[10.5px] rounded-full px-1.5 py-0.5 ${
                value === t.key ? "bg-black/15 text-black" : "bg-neutral-800 text-neutral-300"
              }`}
            >
              {t.badge}
            </span>
          )}
        </button>
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}

/* ---------- Catalogo pacchetti ---------- */

function PlanSheet({ plan, kind = "abbonamento", onClose, onSave }) {
  const personal = (plan ? plan.kind : kind) === "personal";
  const [sessions, setSessions] = useState(plan?.sessions ? String(plan.sessions) : "5");
  const [name, setName] = useState(plan?.label || "");
  const [price, setPrice] = useState(plan ? String(plan.price) : "");
  const [months, setMonths] = useState(plan ? String(plan.months) : "1");
  const [limited, setLimited] = useState(Boolean(plan?.entries));
  const [entries, setEntries] = useState(plan?.entries ? String(plan.entries) : "");
  const [classes, setClasses] = useState(plan ? plan.classes !== false : true);

  const valid =
    name.trim() &&
    Number(price) >= 0 &&
    Number(months) >= 1 &&
    (personal ? Number(sessions) >= 1 : !limited || Number(entries) >= 1);

  return (
    <Sheet
      title={plan ? "Modifica pacchetto" : personal ? "Nuovo pacchetto Personal" : "Nuovo abbonamento"}
      subtitle={
        personal
          ? "Il Personal si affianca all'abbonamento. Le sessioni le scala lo staff dalla scheda socio."
          : "I prezzi qui dentro sono quelli che lo staff vede al momento del rinnovo. Il socio non li modifica."
      }
      confirmLabel="Salva pacchetto"
      disabled={!valid}
      onClose={onClose}
      onConfirm={() =>
        onSave(
          personal
            ? {
                key: plan?.key || `pt${Date.now()}`,
                kind: "personal",
                label: name.trim(),
                price: Number(price),
                months: Number(months),
                sessions: Number(sessions),
                active: plan ? plan.active : true,
              }
            : {
                key: plan?.key || `p${Date.now()}`,
                kind: "abbonamento",
                label: name.trim(),
                price: Number(price),
                months: Number(months),
                entries: limited ? Number(entries) : null,
                classes,
                active: plan ? plan.active : true,
              }
        )
      }
    >
      <div className="space-y-5">
        <TextField
          label="Nome del pacchetto"
          value={name}
          onChange={setName}
          placeholder="Mensile libero"
        />
        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
          <TextField
            label="Prezzo in euro"
            value={price}
            onChange={setPrice}
            type="number"
            placeholder="70"
          />
          <TextField
            label={personal ? "Validità in mesi" : "Durata in mesi"}
            value={months}
            onChange={setMonths}
            type="number"
            placeholder="1"
          />
        </div>

        {personal && (
          <TextField
            label="Sessioni incluse"
            value={sessions}
            onChange={setSessions}
            type="number"
            placeholder="5"
            hint="Metti 1 per la sessione singola."
          />
        )}

        {!personal && (
        <>
        <div className="bg-neutral-900 border border-neutral-800 rounded-2xl px-4 py-3.5">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
            <div className="min-w-0">
              <div className="text-[13.5px] text-neutral-100">Include le classi</div>
              <div className="text-[12px] text-neutral-500 mt-0.5 leading-relaxed">
                Spento significa solo Open Box. Chi ha le classi può prenotare
                comunque anche l'Open Box.
              </div>
            </div>
            <Toggle on={classes} onChange={() => setClasses((v) => !v)} />
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="bg-neutral-900 border border-neutral-800 rounded-2xl px-4 py-3.5">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
            <div className="min-w-0">
              <div className="text-[13.5px] text-neutral-100">Numero di ingressi limitato</div>
              <div className="text-[12px] text-neutral-500 mt-0.5 leading-relaxed">
                Spento significa accesso libero per tutta la durata.
              </div>
            </div>
            <Toggle on={limited} onChange={() => setLimited((v) => !v)} />
          </div>
          {limited && (
            <div className="mt-4">
              <TextField
                label="Ingressi inclusi"
                value={entries}
                onChange={setEntries}
                type="number"
                placeholder="12"
              />
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
        </>
        )}
      </div>
    </Sheet>
  );
}

function PlanCard({ plan, inUse, onEdit, onToggle, onDelete }) {
  return (
    <div className="px-4 py-3.5">
      <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
        <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <span className="text-[14.5px] text-neutral-50 truncate">{plan.label}</span>
            {!isPersonalPlan(plan) && !plan.classes && (
              <span className="text-[11px] rounded-lg px-2 py-0.5 border shrink-0 bg-sky-400/15 text-sky-300 border-sky-400/30">
                Solo Open Box
              </span>
            )}
            {!plan.active && (
              <span className="text-[11px] rounded-lg px-2 py-0.5 border shrink-0 bg-neutral-500/15 text-neutral-400 border-neutral-600/40">
                Sospeso
              </span>
            )}
          </div>
          <div className="text-[12.5px] text-neutral-400 mt-0.5">
            {euro(plan.price)} ·{" "}
            {isPersonalPlan(plan)
              ? `${plan.sessions} ${plan.sessions === 1 ? "sessione" : "sessioni"} · valido ${plan.months} mesi`
              : `${plan.months === 1 ? "1 mese" : `${plan.months} mesi`} · ${
                  plan.entries ? `${plan.entries} ingressi` : "accesso libero"
                }`}
          </div>
          <div className="text-[11.5px] text-neutral-500 mt-0.5">
            {inUse === 0
              ? "Nessun socio attivo su questo pacchetto"
              : inUse === 1
              ? "1 socio attivo"
              : `${inUse} soci attivi`}
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-1 shrink-0">
          <button
            onClick={onEdit}
            className="p-2 rounded-xl text-neutral-400 hover:text-neutral-100 hover:bg-neutral-800"
            aria-label="Modifica"
          >
            <Pencil size={16} />
          </button>
          <button
            onClick={onDelete}
            disabled={inUse > 0}
            className="p-2 rounded-xl text-neutral-400 hover:text-red-300 hover:bg-neutral-800 disabled:opacity-30 disabled:hover:text-neutral-400 disabled:hover:bg-transparent"
            aria-label="Elimina"
          >
            <Trash2 size={16} />
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3 mt-3 pt-3 border-t border-neutral-800">
        <span className="text-[12.5px] text-neutral-400">
          Proponibile in reception
        </span>
        <Toggle on={plan.active} onChange={onToggle} />
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function PlansTab({ plans, setPlans, members, onToast }) {
  const [editing, setEditing] = useState(null); // oggetto pacchetto | "new" | null
  const [confirmDelete, setConfirmDelete] = useState(null);

  const usage = useMemo(() => {
    const map = {};
    members.forEach((m) => {
      if (m.sub && daysTo(m.sub.end) >= 0) {
        map[m.sub.type] = (map[m.sub.type] || 0) + 1;
      }
      if (m.personal && personalState(m) === "ok") {
        map[m.personal.type] = (map[m.personal.type] || 0) + 1;
      }
    });
    return map;
  }, [members]);

  function save(plan) {
    setPlans((prev) => {
      const exists = prev.some((p) => p.key === plan.key);
      return exists ? prev.map((p) => (p.key === plan.key ? plan : p)) : [...prev, plan];
    });
    setEditing(null);
    onToast(`Pacchetto "${plan.label}" salvato.`);
  }

  const renderGroup = (list) => (
    <Card className="divide-y divide-neutral-800 overflow-hidden">
      {!list.length ? (
        <div className="px-4 py-5 text-[13px] text-neutral-500">
          Nessuna voce in questo gruppo.
        </div>
      ) : (
        list.map((p) => (
          <PlanCard
            key={p.key}
            plan={p}
            inUse={usage[p.key] || 0}
            onEdit={() => setEditing(p)}
            onToggle={() =>
              setPlans((prev) =>
                prev.map((x) => (x.key === p.key ? { ...x, active: !x.active } : x))
              )
            }
            onDelete={() => setConfirmDelete(p)}
          />
        ))
      )}
    </Card>
  );

  return (
    <div className="space-y-5">
      <SectionHead action="Nuovo abbonamento" onAction={() => setEditing("new")}>
        Abbonamenti
      </SectionHead>
      {renderGroup(plans.filter(isSubPlan))}

      <SectionHead action="Nuovo pacchetto" onAction={() => setEditing("newPersonal")}>
        Personal
      </SectionHead>
      {renderGroup(plans.filter(isPersonalPlan))}

      <Card className="flex items-start gap-3 p-4">
        <Info size={16} className="text-neutral-500 shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
        <div className="text-[12.5px] text-neutral-400 leading-relaxed">
          Un pacchetto con soci attivi non si può eliminare: sospendilo e sparisce
          dalle scelte al rinnovo, senza toccare chi ce l'ha già.
        </div>
      </Card>

      {editing && (
        <PlanSheet
          plan={typeof editing === "string" ? null : editing}
          kind={editing === "newPersonal" ? "personal" : "abbonamento"}
          onClose={() => setEditing(null)}
          onSave={save}
        />
      )}

      {confirmDelete && (
        <ConfirmDialog
          title={`Eliminare "${confirmDelete.label}"?`}
          body="Il pacchetto sparisce dal catalogo. La cronologia dei soci che lo hanno avuto in passato resta invariata."
          confirmLabel="Elimina"
          danger
          onCancel={() => setConfirmDelete(null)}
          onConfirm={() => {
            setPlans((prev) => prev.filter((p) => p.key !== confirmDelete.key));
            onToast(`Pacchetto "${confirmDelete.label}" eliminato.`);
            setConfirmDelete(null);
          }}
        />
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

/* ---------- Rinnovi ---------- */

function RenewSheet({ member, plans, onClose, onSave }) {
  const active = plans.filter((p) => p.active && isSubPlan(p));
  const current = member.sub?.type;
  const [type, setType] = useState(
    active.some((p) => p.key === current) ? current : active[0]?.key || ""
  );

  // Se l'abbonamento è ancora valido il rinnovo parte dal giorno dopo,
  // altrimenti da oggi: non regaliamo giorni e non ne togliamo.
  const defaultStart = useMemo(() => {
    if (member.sub && daysTo(member.sub.end) >= 0) {
      const d = new Date(`${member.sub.end}T00:00:00`);
      d.setDate(d.getDate() + 1);
      return isoDay(d);
    }
    return isoDay(today());
  }, [member]);

  const [start, setStart] = useState(defaultStart);
  const [paid, setPaid] = useState(true);
  const chosen = planById(plans, type);
  const end = chosen ? addMonths(start, chosen.months) : "";

  return (
    <Sheet
      title={member.sub ? "Rinnova abbonamento" : "Registra abbonamento"}
      subtitle={member.name}
      confirmLabel="Salva"
      disabled={!chosen}
      onClose={onClose}
      onConfirm={() =>
        onSave({
          type,
          start,
          end,
          paid,
          entriesTotal: chosen?.entries ?? null,
          entriesLeft: chosen?.entries ?? null,
        })
      }
    >
      <div className="space-y-5">
        <div>
          <div className="text-[11px] text-neutral-500 mb-2">Pacchetto</div>
          <div className="space-y-2">
            {active.map((p) => (
              <button
                key={p.key}
                onClick={() => setType(p.key)}
                className={`w-full flex items-center gap-3 px-3.5 py-3 rounded-2xl border text-left ${
                  type === p.key
                    ? "border-neutral-500 bg-neutral-900"
                    : "border-neutral-800 bg-neutral-900/60"
                }`}
              >
                <span className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                  <span className="block text-[13.5px] text-neutral-100 truncate">
                    {p.label}
                  </span>
                  <span className="block text-[11.5px] text-neutral-500 mt-0.5">
                    {euro(p.price)} · {p.months === 1 ? "1 mese" : `${p.months} mesi`} ·{" "}
                    {accessLabel(p.classes)}
                  </span>
                </span>
                {type === p.key && <Check size={16} className="text-neutral-100 shrink-0" />}
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>

        <DateField label="Inizio" value={start} onChange={setStart} />

        <div className="bg-neutral-900 border border-neutral-800 rounded-2xl px-4 py-3.5">
          <div className="text-[11px] text-neutral-500 mb-1">Scadenza calcolata</div>
          <div className="text-[14px] text-neutral-100">
            {end ? fmtDay(end) : "—"}
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="bg-neutral-900 border border-neutral-800 rounded-2xl px-4 py-3.5 flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
          <div className="min-w-0">
            <div className="text-[13.5px] text-neutral-100">Pagamento ricevuto</div>
            <div className="text-[12px] text-neutral-500 mt-0.5 leading-relaxed">
              Spegnilo se il socio deve ancora saldare.
            </div>
          </div>
          <Toggle on={paid} onChange={() => setPaid((v) => !v)} />
        </div>
      </div>
    </Sheet>
  );
}

function PersonalSheet({ member, plans, onClose, onSave }) {
  const catalog = plans.filter((p) => p.active && isPersonalPlan(p));
  const [type, setType] = useState(catalog[0]?.key || "");
  const [start, setStart] = useState(isoDay(today()));
  const [paid, setPaid] = useState(true);
  const chosen = catalog.find((p) => p.key === type);
  const end = chosen ? addMonths(start, chosen.months) : "";

  return (
    <Sheet
      title="Pacchetto Personal"
      subtitle={member.name}
      confirmLabel="Assegna pacchetto"
      disabled={!chosen}
      onClose={onClose}
      onConfirm={() =>
        onSave({
          type,
          total: chosen.sessions,
          left: chosen.sessions,
          start,
          end,
          paid,
        })
      }
    >
      <div className="space-y-5">
        {!catalog.length ? (
          <div className="text-[13.5px] text-neutral-400 leading-relaxed">
            Nessun pacchetto Personal attivo nel catalogo. Creane uno dalla
            scheda Pacchetti.
          </div>
        ) : (
          <>
            <div>
              <div className="text-[11px] text-neutral-500 mb-2">Pacchetto</div>
              <div className="space-y-2">
                {catalog.map((p) => (
                  <button
                    key={p.key}
                    onClick={() => setType(p.key)}
                    className={`w-full flex items-center gap-3 px-3.5 py-3 rounded-2xl border text-left ${
                      type === p.key
                        ? "border-neutral-500 bg-neutral-900"
                        : "border-neutral-800 bg-neutral-900/60"
                    }`}
                  >
                    <span className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                      <span className="block text-[13.5px] text-neutral-100 truncate">
                        {p.label}
                      </span>
                      <span className="block text-[11.5px] text-neutral-500 mt-0.5">
                        {euro(p.price)} · valido {p.months} mesi
                      </span>
                    </span>
                    {type === p.key && <Check size={16} className="text-neutral-100 shrink-0" />}
                  </button>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>

            <DateField label="Inizio" value={start} onChange={setStart} />

            <div className="bg-neutral-900 border border-neutral-800 rounded-2xl px-4 py-3.5">
              <div className="text-[11px] text-neutral-500 mb-1">Valido fino al</div>
              <div className="text-[14px] text-neutral-100">{end ? fmtDay(end) : "—"}</div>
            </div>

            <div className="bg-neutral-900 border border-neutral-800 rounded-2xl px-4 py-3.5 flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
              <div className="min-w-0">
                <div className="text-[13.5px] text-neutral-100">Pagamento ricevuto</div>
                <div className="text-[12px] text-neutral-500 mt-0.5 leading-relaxed">
                  Spegnilo se il socio deve ancora saldare.
                </div>
              </div>
              <Toggle on={paid} onChange={() => setPaid((v) => !v)} />
            </div>

            {member.personal && (
              <div className="flex items-start gap-2.5 border border-amber-400/25 bg-amber-400/5 rounded-2xl px-3.5 py-3">
                <AlertTriangle size={15} className="text-amber-300 shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
                <div className="text-[12px] text-amber-200/80 leading-relaxed">
                  Il socio ha già un pacchetto con {member.personal.left} sessioni
                  residue. Assegnandone uno nuovo, quelle vengono sostituite.
                </div>
              </div>
            )}
          </>
        )}
      </div>
    </Sheet>
  );
}

function ExpiryRow({ m, plans, onRenew, onMarkPaid }) {
  const days = m.sub ? daysTo(m.sub.end) : null;
  const unpaid = m.sub && m.sub.paid === false;

  let chip;
  if (!m.sub) chip = { label: "Nessuno", cls: "bg-sky-400/15 text-sky-300 border-sky-400/30" };
  else if (days < 0) chip = { label: "Scaduto", cls: "bg-red-500/15 text-red-300 border-red-500/30" };
  else chip = { label: `Fra ${days} gg`, cls: "bg-amber-400/15 text-amber-300 border-amber-400/30" };

  return (
    <div className="px-4 py-3.5">
      <div className="flex items-center gap-3.5">
        <Avatar name={m.name} photo={m.photo} size={42} />
        <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <span className="text-[14.5px] text-neutral-50 truncate">{m.name}</span>
            <span className={`text-[11px] rounded-lg px-2 py-0.5 border shrink-0 ${chip.cls}`}>
              {chip.label}
            </span>
          </div>
          <div className="text-[12.5px] text-neutral-400 truncate mt-0.5">
            {m.sub ? subLabel(m.sub.type, plans) : "Abbonamento da registrare"}
          </div>
          <div className="text-[11.5px] text-neutral-500 truncate">
            {m.sub ? `Scadenza ${fmtDay(m.sub.end)}` : "—"}
            {unpaid && " · da saldare"}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div className="flex gap-2 mt-3">
        <button
          onClick={onRenew}
          className="flex-1 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-neutral-200 text-black text-[12.5px] font-semibold hover:bg-white"
        >
          {m.sub ? "Rinnova" : "Registra"}
        </button>
        {unpaid && (
          <button
            onClick={onMarkPaid}
            className="flex-1 py-2.5 rounded-xl border border-neutral-700 text-neutral-300 text-[12.5px] hover:border-neutral-600"
          >
            Segna pagato
          </button>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function ScadenzeTab({ members, setMembers, plans, onToast }) {
  const [filter, setFilter] = useState("tutti");
  const [q, setQ] = useState("");
  const [renewing, setRenewing] = useState(null);

  const buckets = useMemo(() => {
    const scaduti = [];
    const inScadenza = [];
    const daSaldare = [];
    const senza = [];
    members.forEach((m) => {
      if (m.suspended) return;
      if (!m.sub) {
        senza.push(m);
        return;
      }
      const d = daysTo(m.sub.end);
      if (d < 0) scaduti.push(m);
      else if (d <= 14) inScadenza.push(m);
      if (m.sub.paid === false) daSaldare.push(m);
    });
    return { scaduti, inScadenza, daSaldare, senza };
  }, [members]);

  const filters = [
    { key: "tutti", label: "Tutti", n: null },
    { key: "scaduti", label: "Scaduti", n: buckets.scaduti.length },
    { key: "scadenza", label: "In scadenza", n: buckets.inScadenza.length },
    { key: "saldare", label: "Da saldare", n: buckets.daSaldare.length },
    { key: "senza", label: "Senza", n: buckets.senza.length },
  ];

  const list = useMemo(() => {
    let base;
    if (filter === "scaduti") base = buckets.scaduti;
    else if (filter === "scadenza") base = buckets.inScadenza;
    else if (filter === "saldare") base = buckets.daSaldare;
    else if (filter === "senza") base = buckets.senza;
    else
      base = [
        ...buckets.scaduti,
        ...buckets.inScadenza,
        ...buckets.senza,
        ...buckets.daSaldare.filter(
          (m) => daysTo(m.sub.end) > 14
        ),
      ];
    const seen = new Set();
    const uniq = base.filter((m) => (seen.has(m.id) ? false : seen.add(m.id)));
    const needle = q.trim().toLowerCase();
    return needle ? uniq.filter((m) => m.name.toLowerCase().includes(needle)) : uniq;
  }, [filter, buckets, q]);

  function applyRenewal(id, sub) {
    setMembers((prev) =>
      prev.map((m) =>
        m.id === id
          ? { ...m, sub: { ...sub }, subHistoryExtra: undefined }
          : m
      )
    );
    setRenewing(null);
    onToast("Abbonamento aggiornato.");
  }

  function markPaid(id) {
    setMembers((prev) =>
      prev.map((m) => (m.id === id ? { ...m, sub: { ...m.sub, paid: true } } : m))
    );
    onToast("Pagamento registrato.");
  }

  return (
    <div className="space-y-4">
      <SearchField value={q} onChange={setQ} placeholder="Cerca un socio" />

      <div className="flex gap-2 overflow-x-auto pb-1">
        {filters.map((f) => (
          <button
            key={f.key}
            onClick={() => setFilter(f.key)}
            className={`shrink-0 px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl text-[12.5px] border ${
              filter === f.key
                ? "bg-neutral-200 text-black border-neutral-200 font-semibold"
                : "border-neutral-800 text-neutral-400 bg-neutral-900/60"
            }`}
          >
            {f.label}
            {f.n !== null && f.n > 0 && ` · ${f.n}`}
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>

      {!list.length ? (
        <div className="border border-dashed border-neutral-800 rounded-2xl p-10 text-center">
          <div className="text-neutral-300 text-[14px]">Niente da sistemare</div>
          <div className="text-neutral-600 text-[12px] mt-1.5">
            Nessun abbonamento in scadenza o da saldare.
          </div>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <Card className="divide-y divide-neutral-800 overflow-hidden">
          {list.map((m) => (
            <ExpiryRow
              key={m.id}
              m={m}
              plans={plans}
              onRenew={() => setRenewing(m)}
              onMarkPaid={() => markPaid(m.id)}
            />
          ))}
        </Card>
      )}

      {renewing && (
        <RenewSheet
          member={renewing}
          plans={plans}
          onClose={() => setRenewing(null)}
          onSave={(sub) => applyRenewal(renewing.id, sub)}
        />
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

/* ---------- Quote associative ---------- */

function QuoteTab({ members, setMembers, onToast }) {
  const [q, setQ] = useState("");
  const [onlyOpen, setOnlyOpen] = useState(true);

  const rows = useMemo(() => {
    const needle = q.trim().toLowerCase();
    return members
      .filter((m) => (onlyOpen ? membershipState(m) !== "ok" : true))
      .filter((m) => (needle ? m.name.toLowerCase().includes(needle) : true));
  }, [members, q, onlyOpen]);

  const inRegola = members.filter((m) => membershipState(m) === "ok").length;
  const end = sportsYearEnd();

  function register(id) {
    setMembers((prev) =>
      prev.map((m) => (m.id === id ? { ...m, membership: { paid: true, end } } : m))
    );
    onToast("Quota associativa registrata.");
  }

  return (
    <div className="space-y-4">
      <Card className="p-4">
        <div className="flex items-end justify-between gap-3">
          <div>
            <Display size="text-3xl">
              {inRegola}
              <span className="text-[16px] text-neutral-500"> / {members.length}</span>
            </Display>
            <div className="text-[13px] text-neutral-400 mt-1.5">
              Soci con la quota in regola
            </div>
          </div>
          <div className="text-right shrink-0">
            <div className="text-[11px] text-neutral-500">Anno sportivo fino al</div>
            <div className="text-[13.5px] text-neutral-100 mt-0.5">{fmtDay(end)}</div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </Card>

      <SearchField value={q} onChange={setQ} placeholder="Cerca un socio" />

      <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3 bg-neutral-900 border border-neutral-800 rounded-2xl px-4 py-3">
        <span className="text-[13px] text-neutral-300">Mostra solo chi deve versare</span>
        <Toggle on={onlyOpen} onChange={() => setOnlyOpen((v) => !v)} />
      </div>

      {!rows.length ? (
        <div className="border border-dashed border-neutral-800 rounded-2xl p-10 text-center">
          <div className="text-neutral-300 text-[14px]">Tutti in regola</div>
          <div className="text-neutral-600 text-[12px] mt-1.5">
            Nessuna quota associativa da incassare.
          </div>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <Card className="divide-y divide-neutral-800 overflow-hidden">
          {rows.map((m) => {
            const st = membershipState(m);
            const chip = MEMBERSHIP_CHIP[st];
            return (
              <div key={m.id} className="px-4 py-3.5 flex items-center gap-3.5">
                <Avatar name={m.name} photo={m.photo} size={42} />
                <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                    <span className="text-[14.5px] text-neutral-50 truncate">{m.name}</span>
                    <span className={`text-[11px] rounded-lg px-2 py-0.5 border shrink-0 ${chip.cls}`}>
                      {chip.label}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                  <div className="text-[12px] text-neutral-500 mt-0.5">
                    {st === "ok"
                      ? `Valida fino al ${fmtDay(m.membership.end)}`
                      : st === "scaduta"
                      ? `Scaduta il ${fmtDay(m.membership.end)}`
                      : "Mai versata"}
                  </div>
                </div>
                {st !== "ok" && (
                  <button
                    onClick={() => register(m.id)}
                    className="shrink-0 px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl bg-neutral-200 text-black text-[12.5px] font-semibold hover:bg-white"
                  >
                    Registra
                  </button>
                )}
              </div>
            );
          })}
        </Card>
      )}

      <Card className="flex items-start gap-3 p-4">
        <Info size={16} className="text-neutral-500 shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
        <div className="text-[12.5px] text-neutral-400 leading-relaxed">
          La quota associativa è cosa distinta dall'abbonamento: rende il socio
          tesserato all'ASD e scade il 31 agosto per tutti, indipendentemente da
          quando è stata versata.
        </div>
      </Card>
    </div>
  );
}

/* ---------- Sezione ---------- */

function AbbonamentiSection({ members, setMembers, plans, setPlans, onToast }) {
  const [tab, setTab] = useState("scadenze");

  const counts = useMemo(() => {
    let scadenze = 0;
    members.forEach((m) => {
      if (m.suspended) return;
      if (!m.sub) scadenze += 1;
      else if (daysTo(m.sub.end) <= 14 || m.sub.paid === false) scadenze += 1;
    });
    const quote = members.filter((m) => membershipState(m) !== "ok").length;
    return { scadenze, quote };
  }, [members]);

  return (
    <div>
      <div className="mb-5">
        <h1 className="text-neutral-50 text-[26px] font-display font-extrabold leading-tight">
          Abbonamenti
        </h1>
        <p className="text-[13px] text-neutral-500 mt-1">
          Catalogo, rinnovi e quote associative.
        </p>
      </div>

      <AbbTabs value={tab} onChange={setTab} counts={counts} />

      {tab === "pacchetti" && (
        <PlansTab plans={plans} setPlans={setPlans} members={members} onToast={onToast} />
      )}
      {tab === "scadenze" && (
        <ScadenzeTab
          members={members}
          setMembers={setMembers}
          plans={plans}
          onToast={onToast}
        />
      )}
      {tab === "quote" && (
        <QuoteTab members={members} setMembers={setMembers} onToast={onToast} />
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

/* ---------- WOD ----------
   Lo staff scrive il WOD del giorno: una riga per movimento, tre versioni
   (RX obbligatoria, Scaled e Open facoltative). Appena salvato è visibile
   nell'app dei soci, anche se riguarda i giorni successivi. */

const WOD_VARIANTS = ["RX", "SCALED", "OPEN"];
const VARIANT_LABEL = { RX: "RX", SCALED: "Scaled", OPEN: "Open" };
const VARIANT_TONE = {
  RX: "bg-neutral-100 text-black",
  SCALED: "bg-neutral-700 text-neutral-100",
  OPEN: "border border-neutral-600 text-neutral-300",
};

const SCORE_TYPES = [
  { key: "time", label: "Tempo", hint: "Si scrive mm:ss. In classifica vince il più veloce.", icon: Clock },
  { key: "reps", label: "Ripetizioni", hint: "Solo numeri interi. In classifica vince chi ne fa di più.", icon: Hash },
  { key: "load", label: "Carico", hint: "Kg con un decimale. In classifica vince il carico più alto.", icon: Dumbbell },
];

const WOD_FORMATS = ["FOR TIME", "AMRAP", "EMOM", "STRENGTH", "CHIPPER"];

// Stessa libreria esercizi dell'app socio: il carico personale si calcola
// solo se il nome del movimento coincide con quello salvato nei PR.
const EXERCISE_NAMES = [
  "Snatch", "Power Snatch", "Hang Snatch", "Squat Snatch", "Clean", "Power Clean", "Hang Clean",
  "Squat Clean", "Clean & Jerk", "Jerk", "Push Jerk", "Split Jerk", "Muscle Snatch", "Muscle Clean",
  "Back Squat", "Front Squat", "Overhead Squat", "Box Squat", "Pause Squat", "Bulgarian Split Squat",
  "Pistol Squat", "Lunge con bilanciere", "Deadlift", "Sumo Deadlift", "Romanian Deadlift",
  "Deficit Deadlift", "Snatch Pull", "Clean Pull", "Rack Pull", "Bench Press", "Incline Bench Press",
  "Close Grip Bench", "Strict Press", "Push Press", "Thruster", "Dip zavorrato", "Pull Up zavorrato",
  "Chin Up zavorrato", "Muscle Up", "Handstand Push Up", "Ring Dip zavorrato", "Rope Climb",
  "Toes to Bar", "Barbell Row", "Pendlay Row", "Good Morning", "Hip Thrust", "Farmer Walk",
  "Turkish Get Up",
];

// Nel prototipo la console è usata dall'owner.
const CURRENT_STAFF = "Simone Zen";

function isClosedDay(iso) {
  return new Date(`${iso}T00:00:00`).getDay() === 0;
}

function fmtDayLong(iso) {
  const d = new Date(`${iso}T00:00:00`);
  return `${DAY_FULL[d.getDay()]} ${d.getDate()} ${MONTH_FULL[d.getMonth()].toLowerCase()}`;
}

function wodLines(text) {
  return (text || "").split("\n").map((l) => l.trim()).filter(Boolean);
}

/** "5 ROUNDS" diventa un'intestazione; "400m Run" resta un movimento. */
function isHeaderLine(line) {
  return /[A-Z]/.test(line) && !/[a-zà-ù]/.test(line);
}

function matchExercise(name) {
  const n = name.trim().toLowerCase();
  return EXERCISE_NAMES.find((e) => e.toLowerCase() === n) || null;
}

/** Riconosce "Back Squat 5x5 @ 75%" e "Back Squat @ 75%". */
function parseLoadLine(line) {
  let m = line.match(/^(.+?)\s+(\d+)\s*[x×]\s*(\d+)\s*@\s*(\d+(?:[.,]\d+)?)\s*%/i);
  if (m) {
    return { movement: m[1].trim(), sets: +m[2], reps: +m[3], percent: parseFloat(m[4].replace(",", ".")) };
  }
  m = line.match(/^(.+?)\s*@\s*(\d+(?:[.,]\d+)?)\s*%/);
  if (m) return { movement: m[1].trim(), percent: parseFloat(m[2].replace(",", ".")) };
  return null;
}

function loadBlocksOf(lines) {
  return lines
    .map(parseLoadLine)
    .filter(Boolean)
    .map((b) => ({ ...b, known: matchExercise(b.movement) }));
}

/* Score: inserimento guidato come nell'app socio */

function maskTime(raw) {
  const d = raw.replace(/\D/g, "").slice(0, 4);
  return d.length > 2 ? `${d.slice(0, d.length - 2)}:${d.slice(-2)}` : d;
}

function maskScore(type, raw) {
  if (type === "time") return maskTime(raw);
  if (type === "reps") return raw.replace(/\D/g, "").slice(0, 4);
  const m = raw.replace(".", ",").replace(/[^\d,]/g, "").match(/^(\d{0,3})(,\d?)?/);
  return m ? m[1] + (m[2] || "") : "";
}

function scoreToNumber(type, raw) {
  const s = String(raw).trim();
  if (type === "time") {
    const m = s.match(/^(\d{1,2}):([0-5]\d)$/);
    return m ? +m[1] * 60 + +m[2] : null;
  }
  if (type === "reps") return /^\d+$/.test(s) ? +s : null;
  const v = s.replace(",", ".");
  return /^\d+(\.\d)?$/.test(v) ? +v : null;
}

function scoreToInput(type, v) {
  if (type === "time") return `${Math.floor(v / 60)}:${String(v % 60).padStart(2, "0")}`;
  if (type === "reps") return String(v);
  return String(v).replace(".", ",");
}

function fmtScore(type, v) {
  if (type === "time") return scoreToInput(type, v);
  if (type === "reps") return `${v} rip.`;
  return `${scoreToInput(type, v)} kg`;
}

function sortScores(type, list) {
  return [...list].sort((a, b) => (type === "time" ? a.value - b.value : b.value - a.value));
}

function countByVariant(list = []) {
  return list.reduce((acc, s) => ({ ...acc, [s.variant]: (acc[s.variant] || 0) + 1 }), {});
}

/* Dati di esempio */

  },
  {
    title: "AMRAP 20'",
    scoreType: "reps",
    cap: "20:00",
    warmUp: ["2 ROUNDS", "250m Row", "10 Scapular Pull Up", "10 Ring Row", "SKILL", "Kipping swing 3x8", "Progressioni Muscle Up 5'"],

    variants: {

      SCALED: ["Back Squat 5x5 @ 65%", "Strict Press 3x8 @ 60%", "Riposo 2' tra le serie"],

    },

    title: "EMOM 12'",
    scoreType: "reps",
    cap: "12:00",
    warmUp: ["3 ROUNDS", "1' Row", "10 Hollow Rock", "5 Power Clean bilanciere vuoto"],
    variants: {
      RX: ["MIN 1 · 12 Cal Row", "MIN 2 · 10 Toes to Bar", "MIN 3 · 8 Power Clean 60 kg"],
      SCALED: ["MIN 1 · 10 Cal Row", "MIN 2 · 10 Knee Raise", "MIN 3 · 8 Power Clean 40 kg"],
    },
  },

function buildWodSeed(members) {
  const wods = {};
  const scores = {};
  const pool = members.filter((m) => !m.suspended);
  let t = 0;

  for (let off = -6; off <= 2; off++) {
    const iso = offsetDay(off);
    if (isClosedDay(iso)) continue;
    const tpl = WOD_TEMPLATES[t % WOD_TEMPLATES.length];
    t += 1;

    wods[iso] = {
      title: tpl.title,
      scoreType: tpl.scoreType,
      cap: tpl.cap,
      warmUp: (tpl.warmUp || []).join("\n"),
      variants: Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(tpl.variants).map(([k, v]) => [k, v.join("\n")])),
      author: t % 3 === 0 ? "Alberto R." : CURRENT_STAFF,
      publishedAt: offsetDay(Math.min(off, 0) - 2),
      editedAt: null,
      editedBy: null,
    };

    if (off > 0) continue;
    const variants = WOD_VARIANTS.filter((v) => tpl.variants[v]);
    scores[iso] = pool
      .filter((m) => seeded(iso + "s" + m.id, 3) !== 0)
      .map((m) => {
        const r = seeded(m.name + iso, 99);
        const idx = Math.min(variants.length - 1, r < 35 ? 0 : r < 80 ? 1 : 2);
        const jitter = seeded(iso + m.name, 60);
        let value;
        if (tpl.scoreType === "time") value = Math.min(1190, 560 + jitter * 9 + idx * 70);
        else if (tpl.scoreType === "reps") value = (tpl.cap === "12:00" ? 110 : 310) - jitter * (tpl.cap === "12:00" ? 1 : 2) - idx * 25;
        else value = 125 - jitter - idx * 15 + (jitter % 2 ? 0.5 : 0);
        return {
          id: `${iso}-${m.id}`,
          memberId: m.id,
          variant: variants[idx],
          value,
          private: seeded("p" + m.id + iso, 6) === 0,
          editedBy: null,
        };
      });
  }
  return { wods, scores };
}

/* Componenti */

function VariantTag({ variant }) {
  return (
    <span className={`px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg text-[11px] font-bold tracking-wide ${VARIANT_TONE[variant]}`}>
      {variant}
    </span>
  );
}

function WodChip({ icon: Icon, children }) {
  return (
    <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg bg-neutral-900 border border-neutral-800 text-[12px] text-neutral-300">
      <Icon size={13} strokeWidth={1.8} />
      {children}
    </span>
  );
}

function WeekStrip({ days, selected, onSelect, wods }) {
  const activeRef = React.useRef(null);
  const todayIso = offsetDay(0);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (activeRef.current) activeRef.current.scrollIntoView({ inline: "center", block: "nearest" });
  }, [selected]);

  return (
    <div className="flex gap-1.5 overflow-x-auto pb-1 -mx-5 px-5 lg:mx-0 lg:px-0" style={{ scrollbarWidth: "none" }}>
      {days.map((iso) => {
        const d = new Date(`${iso}T00:00:00`);
        const closed = isClosedDay(iso);
        const active = iso === selected;
        const has = Boolean(wods[iso]);
        const isToday = iso === todayIso;
        return (
          <button
            key={iso}
            ref={active ? activeRef : null}
            onClick={() => onSelect(iso)}
            className={`shrink-0 w-[54px] rounded-2xl py-2.5 flex flex-col items-center border transition-colors ${
              active
                ? "bg-neutral-200 border-neutral-200 text-black"
                : closed
                ? "border-neutral-900 text-neutral-600"
                : "bg-neutral-900/60 border-neutral-800 text-neutral-300"
            }`}
          >
            <span
              className={`text-[10px] uppercase tracking-wide ${
                active ? "text-black/60" : isToday ? "text-neutral-50 font-semibold" : ""
              }`}
            >
              {isToday ? "Oggi" : DAY_FULL[d.getDay()].slice(0, 3)}
            </span>
            <span className="text-[18px] mt-0.5" style={{ fontFamily: "Barlow, sans-serif", fontWeight: 700 }}>
              {d.getDate()}
            </span>
            <span
              className={`w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full mt-1 ${
                has ? (active ? "bg-black" : "bg-emerald-400") : "bg-transparent"
              }`}
            />
          </button>
        );
      })}
    </div>
  );
}

function LoadPreview({ loads }) {
  if (!loads.length) return null;
  return (
    <div className="mt-4 rounded-2xl border border-neutral-800 bg-neutral-950/60 p-3.5">
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-[12.5px] text-neutral-200 font-semibold">
        <TrendingUp size={14} />
        Carichi personali
      </div>
      <p className="text-[11.5px] text-neutral-500 mt-1 mb-3 leading-relaxed">
        Ogni socio vede il suo carico calcolato dal proprio PR.
      </p>
      <ul className="space-y-2.5">
        {loads.map((b, i) => (
          <li key={i} className="flex items-start gap-2 text-[12.5px]">
            {b.known ? (
              <Check size={14} className="text-emerald-400 mt-0.5 shrink-0" />
            ) : (
              <AlertTriangle size={14} className="text-amber-400 mt-0.5 shrink-0" />
            )}
            <span className="flex-1 min-w-0">
              <span className="text-neutral-100">{b.known || b.movement}</span>
              <span className="text-neutral-500">
                {" "}· {String(b.percent).replace(".", ",")}%{b.sets ? ` · ${b.sets}x${b.reps}` : ""}
              </span>
              {!b.known && (
                <span className="block text-[11.5px] text-amber-300/80 mt-0.5 leading-relaxed">
                  Non è nella libreria esercizi: i soci non vedranno il carico calcolato.
                  Controlla come è scritto il nome.
                </span>
              )}
            </span>
          </li>
        ))}
      </ul>
    </div>
  );
}

function WodCard({ wod, onEdit, onDuplicate, onDelete }) {
  const [warmOpen, setWarmOpen] = useState(false);
  const warm = wodLines(wod.warmUp);
  const available = WOD_VARIANTS.filter((v) => wod.variants[v]);
  const [variant, setVariant] = useState(available[0]);
  const current = wod.variants[variant] ? variant : available[0];
  const type = SCORE_TYPES.find((t) => t.key === wod.scoreType);
  const lines = wodLines(wod.variants[current]);
  const missing = WOD_VARIANTS.filter((v) => !wod.variants[v]);

  return (
    <Card className="overflow-hidden">
      <div className="p-5">
        <Display size="text-[30px]">{wod.title}</Display>
        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2 mt-3">
          <WodChip icon={type.icon}>Score a {type.label.toLowerCase()}</WodChip>
          {wod.cap && <WodChip icon={Timer}>Cap {wod.cap}</WodChip>}
        </div>

        <div className="bg-neutral-900 border border-neutral-800 rounded-2xl p-1 flex mt-5">
          {WOD_VARIANTS.map((v) => (
            <button
              key={v}
              disabled={!wod.variants[v]}
              onClick={() => setVariant(v)}
              className={`flex-1 py-2 rounded-xl text-[12.5px] transition-colors ${
                current === v ? "bg-neutral-300 text-black font-semibold" : "text-neutral-400 disabled:text-neutral-700"
              }`}
            >
              {v}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>

        <ul className="mt-4 space-y-2">
          {lines.map((l, i) =>
            isHeaderLine(l) ? (
              <li key={i} className="text-[11.5px] tracking-widest text-neutral-500 pt-1">
                {l}
              </li>
            ) : (
              <li key={i} className="text-[15px] text-neutral-100 leading-snug">
                {l}
              </li>
            )
          )}
        </ul>

        <LoadPreview loads={loadBlocksOf(lines)} />

        {warm.length > 0 && (
          <div className="mt-5 rounded-2xl border border-neutral-800 overflow-hidden">
            <button
              onClick={() => setWarmOpen(!warmOpen)}
              className="w-full flex items-center gap-2.5 px-4 py-3 text-left hover:bg-neutral-900"
            >
              <Tv size={15} className="text-neutral-400 shrink-0" />
              <span className="flex-1 text-[13px] text-neutral-200">
                Warm up <span className="text-neutral-500">· {warm.length} righe · solo TV</span>
              </span>
              <ChevronDown size={16} className={`text-neutral-500 transition-transform ${warmOpen ? "rotate-180" : ""}`} />
            </button>
            {warmOpen && (
              <ul className="px-4 pb-4 space-y-1.5">
                {warm.map((l, i) =>
                  isHeaderLine(l) ? (
                    <li key={i} className="text-[11px] tracking-widest text-neutral-500 pt-1.5">{l}</li>
                  ) : (
                    <li key={i} className="text-[13.5px] text-neutral-300">{l}</li>
                  )
                )}
              </ul>
            )}
          </div>
        )}

        {missing.length > 0 && (
          <p className="text-[11.5px] text-neutral-600 mt-4">
            {missing.map((v) => VARIANT_LABEL[v]).join(" e ")} non previst{missing.length > 1 ? "e" : "a"} per questo WOD.
          </p>
        )}
      </div>

      <div className="px-5 py-3 border-t border-neutral-800 flex items-center gap-3">
        <span className="text-[11.5px] text-neutral-500 flex-1 min-w-0 leading-snug">
          {wod.editedAt
            ? `Modificato da ${wod.editedBy} · ${fmtDayShort(wod.editedAt)}`
            : `Pubblicato da ${wod.author} · ${fmtDayShort(wod.publishedAt)}`}
        </span>
        <button
          onClick={onDuplicate}
          aria-label="Copia su un altro giorno"
          className="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl border border-neutral-800 flex items-center justify-center text-neutral-300 hover:border-neutral-600 shrink-0"
        >
          <Copy size={16} />
        </button>
        <button
          onClick={onDelete}
          aria-label="Elimina WOD"
          className="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl border border-neutral-800 flex items-center justify-center text-neutral-400 hover:text-red-400 hover:border-red-900 shrink-0"
        >
          <Trash2 size={16} />
        </button>
        <button
          onClick={onEdit}
          className="h-10 px-4 rounded-xl bg-neutral-200 text-black text-[13px] font-semibold flex items-center gap-1.5 hover:bg-white shrink-0"
        >
          <Pencil size={14} />
          Modifica
        </button>
      </div>
    </Card>
  );
}

function WodScores({ wod, scores, members, onOpen }) {
  const available = WOD_VARIANTS.filter((v) => wod.variants[v]);
  const [variant, setVariant] = useState(available[0]);
  const current = wod.variants[variant] ? variant : available[0];
  const counts = countByVariant(scores);
  const nameOf = (id) => (members.find((m) => m.id === id) || {}).name || "Socio";
  const list = sortScores(wod.scoreType, scores.filter((s) => s.variant === current));
  let rank = 0;

  return (
    <section>
      <div className="flex items-end justify-between mb-3">
        <h2 className="text-neutral-50 text-[19px]" style={{ fontFamily: "Barlow, sans-serif", fontWeight: 700 }}>
          Score dei soci
        </h2>
        <span className="text-[12px] text-neutral-500">{scores.length} salvati</span>
      </div>

      <div className="bg-neutral-900 border border-neutral-800 rounded-2xl p-1 flex mb-3">
        {available.map((v) => (
          <button
            key={v}
            onClick={() => setVariant(v)}
            className={`flex-1 py-2 rounded-xl text-[12.5px] flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 transition-colors ${
              current === v ? "bg-neutral-300 text-black font-semibold" : "text-neutral-400"
            }`}
          >
            {v}
            <span className={`text-[10.5px] ${current === v ? "text-black/55" : "text-neutral-600"}`}>
              {counts[v] || 0}
            </span>
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>

      {list.length ? (
        <Card className="divide-y divide-neutral-800 overflow-hidden">
          {list.map((s) => {
            if (!s.private) rank += 1;
            const name = nameOf(s.memberId);
            return (
              <button
                key={s.id}
                onClick={() => onOpen(s)}
                className="w-full flex items-center gap-3 px-4 py-3 text-left hover:bg-neutral-900"
              >
                <span className="w-6 flex justify-center text-[13px] text-neutral-500 tabular-nums shrink-0">
                  {s.private ? <Lock size={13} /> : rank}
                </span>
                <Avatar name={name} size={34} />
                <span className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                  <span className="block text-[14px] text-neutral-100 truncate">{name}</span>
                  {(s.private || s.editedBy) && (
                    <span className="block text-[11px] text-neutral-500 truncate">
                      {[s.private && "Privato, non in classifica", s.editedBy && `Corretto da ${s.editedBy}`]
                        .filter(Boolean)
                        .join(" · ")}
                    </span>
                  )}
                </span>
                <span
                  className="text-[16px] text-neutral-50 tabular-nums shrink-0"
                  style={{ fontFamily: "Barlow, sans-serif", fontWeight: 700 }}
                >
                  {fmtScore(wod.scoreType, s.value)}
                </span>
                <ChevronRight size={16} className="text-neutral-600 shrink-0" />
              </button>
            );
          })}
        </Card>
      ) : (
        <div className="border border-dashed border-neutral-800 rounded-2xl px-5 py-7 text-center text-[13px] text-neutral-500">
          Nessuno score in {VARIANT_LABEL[current]} per ora.
        </div>
      )}

      <p className="text-[11.5px] text-neutral-500 mt-2.5 leading-relaxed">
        Gli score privati li vede solo lo staff: nell'app non compaiono in classifica.
      </p>
    </section>
  );
}

function ScoreSheet({ score, wod, name, onClose, onSave, onDelete }) {
  const available = WOD_VARIANTS.filter((v) => wod.variants[v]);
  const type = SCORE_TYPES.find((t) => t.key === wod.scoreType);
  const [variant, setVariant] = useState(score.variant);
  const [raw, setRaw] = useState(scoreToInput(wod.scoreType, score.value));
  const [asking, setAsking] = useState(false);
  const value = scoreToNumber(wod.scoreType, raw);
  const unchanged = value === score.value && variant === score.variant;

  if (asking) {
    return (
      <ConfirmDialog
        title="Eliminare lo score?"
        body={`Lo score di ${name} sparisce dalla classifica e dai suoi progressi.`}
        confirmLabel="Elimina"
        danger
        onCancel={() => setAsking(false)}
        onConfirm={onDelete}
      />
    );
  }

  return (
    <Sheet
      title="Correggi score"
      subtitle={`${name} · ${wod.title}`}
      onClose={onClose}
      onConfirm={() => onSave({ ...score, variant, value })}
      confirmLabel="Salva"
      disabled={value === null || unchanged}
    >
      <div className="space-y-5">
        <div>
          <span className="block text-[11px] text-neutral-500 mb-2">Versione</span>
          <div className="bg-neutral-900 border border-neutral-800 rounded-2xl p-1 flex">
            {available.map((v) => (
              <button
                key={v}
                onClick={() => setVariant(v)}
                className={`flex-1 py-2.5 rounded-xl text-[13px] transition-colors ${
                  variant === v ? "bg-neutral-300 text-black font-semibold" : "text-neutral-400"
                }`}
              >
                {v}
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>

        <label className="block">
          <span className="block text-[11px] text-neutral-500 mb-2">Score ({type.label.toLowerCase()})</span>
          <input
            value={raw}
            inputMode={wod.scoreType === "load" ? "decimal" : "numeric"}
            onChange={(e) => setRaw(maskScore(wod.scoreType, e.target.value))}
            placeholder={wod.scoreType === "time" ? "12:30" : wod.scoreType === "reps" ? "250" : "92,5"}
            className="w-full bg-neutral-900 border border-neutral-800 rounded-2xl px-3.5 py-3 text-[20px] text-neutral-100 tabular-nums placeholder:text-neutral-700 outline-none focus:border-neutral-600"
            style={{ fontFamily: "Barlow, sans-serif", fontWeight: 700 }}
          />
          <span className={`block text-[11.5px] mt-2 ${raw && value === null ? "text-amber-400" : "text-neutral-500"}`}>
            {raw && value === null ? "Formato non valido. " : ""}
            {type.hint}
          </span>
        </label>

        <p className="text-[11.5px] text-neutral-500 leading-relaxed">
          La correzione resta registrata a tuo nome, così il socio sa chi l'ha fatta.
        </p>

        <button
          onClick={() => setAsking(true)}
          className="w-full py-3 rounded-2xl border border-red-900/60 text-red-400 text-[13.5px] flex items-center justify-center gap-2 hover:bg-red-950/30"
        >
          <Trash2 size={15} />
          Elimina score
        </button>
      </div>
    </Sheet>
  );
}

function PickDaySheet({ title, subtitle, options, confirmLabel, emptyText, onClose, onConfirm }) {
  const [picked, setPicked] = useState(null);
  return (
    <Sheet
      title={title}
      subtitle={subtitle}
      onClose={onClose}
      onConfirm={() => onConfirm(picked)}
      confirmLabel={confirmLabel}
      disabled={!picked}
    >
      {options.length ? (
        <div className="space-y-2">
          {options.map((o) => (
            <button
              key={o.key}
              onClick={() => setPicked(o.key)}
              className={`w-full flex items-center gap-3 rounded-2xl border px-4 py-3 text-left transition-colors ${
                picked === o.key ? "border-neutral-300 bg-neutral-900" : "border-neutral-800 hover:border-neutral-600"
              }`}
            >
              <span className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                <span className="block text-[14px] text-neutral-100">{o.label}</span>
                {o.detail && <span className="block text-[12px] text-neutral-500 truncate">{o.detail}</span>}
              </span>
              <span
                className={`w-5 h-5 rounded-full border flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 ${
                  picked === o.key ? "bg-neutral-100 border-neutral-100 text-black" : "border-neutral-600"
                }`}
              >
                {picked === o.key && <Check size={12} strokeWidth={3} />}
              </span>
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
      ) : (
        <p className="text-[13px] text-neutral-500 text-center py-6">{emptyText}</p>
      )}
    </Sheet>
  );
}

function VariantEditor({ variant, value, onChange, onRemove, removeLocked }) {
  const lines = wodLines(value);
  const loads = loadBlocksOf(lines);
  const rows = Math.max(4, value.split("\n").length + 1);

  return (
    <Card className="p-4">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-3">
        <VariantTag variant={variant} />
        {variant === "RX" ? (
          <span className="text-[11px] text-neutral-500">Obbligatoria</span>
        ) : (
          <button
            onClick={onRemove}
            disabled={removeLocked}
            className="text-[12px] text-neutral-400 hover:text-red-400 disabled:opacity-40 disabled:hover:text-neutral-400"
          >
            Rimuovi
          </button>
        )}
      </div>
      <textarea
        value={value}
        rows={rows}
        onChange={(e) => onChange(e.target.value)}
        placeholder={variant === "RX" ? "5 ROUNDS\n400m Run\n15 Wall Ball 9 kg\n10 Burpees" : "Una riga per movimento"}
        className="w-full bg-neutral-900 border border-neutral-800 rounded-2xl px-3.5 py-3 text-[14px] leading-relaxed text-neutral-100 placeholder:text-neutral-600 outline-none focus:border-neutral-600 resize-none"
      />
      {removeLocked && (
        <p className="text-[11.5px] text-neutral-500 mt-2">
          Ci sono score salvati in questa versione: non si può rimuovere.
        </p>
      )}
      {variant === "RX" && !loads.length && (
        <p className="text-[11.5px] text-neutral-500 mt-2 leading-relaxed">
          Una riga per movimento. Le righe in maiuscolo diventano intestazioni. Per i carichi personali
          scrivi la percentuale, per esempio Back Squat 5x5 @ 75%.
        </p>
      )}
      <LoadPreview loads={loads} />
    </Card>
  );
}

function WodEditor({ iso, isNew, initial, scoreCounts, onCancel, onSave }) {
  const [d, setD] = useState(initial);
  const total = Object.values(scoreCounts).reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0);
  const typeLocked = !isNew && total > 0;

  const set = (patch) => setD((p) => ({ ...p, ...patch }));
  const setVariant = (v, text) => setD((p) => ({ ...p, variants: { ...p.variants, [v]: text } }));
  const removeVariant = (v) =>
    setD((p) => {
      const next = { ...p.variants };
      delete next[v];
      return { ...p, variants: next };
    });
  const sourceFor = (v) => (v === "OPEN" && d.variants.SCALED !== undefined ? "SCALED" : "RX");

  const capOk = !d.cap || /^\d{1,2}:[0-5]\d$/.test(d.cap);
  const missing = !d.title.trim()
    ? "Manca il formato del WOD."
    : !wodLines(d.variants.RX).length
    ? "Scrivi almeno una riga nella versione RX."
    : !capOk
    ? "Il time cap va scritto come mm:ss."
    : null;

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      <BackLink onBack={onCancel} label="WOD" />

      <div>
        <h1 className="text-neutral-50 text-[26px]" style={{ fontFamily: "Barlow, sans-serif", fontWeight: 700 }}>
          {isNew ? "Nuovo WOD" : "Modifica WOD"}
        </h1>
        <p className="text-[13px] text-neutral-500 mt-1">
          {fmtDayLong(iso)} · {isNew ? "i soci lo vedono appena lo pubblichi" : "le modifiche arrivano subito ai soci"}
        </p>
      </div>

      <Card className="p-4">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-3">
          <span className="text-[13.5px] text-neutral-100 font-semibold">Warm up</span>
          <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[11px] text-neutral-400">
            <Tv size={13} />
            Solo TV e staff
          </span>
        </div>
        <textarea
          value={d.warmUp}
          rows={Math.max(4, d.warmUp.split("\n").length + 1)}
          onChange={(e) => set({ warmUp: e.target.value })}
          placeholder={"3 ROUNDS\n200m Run facile\n10 Air Squat\n10 Push Up"}
          className="w-full bg-neutral-900 border border-neutral-800 rounded-2xl px-3.5 py-3 text-[14px] leading-relaxed text-neutral-100 placeholder:text-neutral-600 outline-none focus:border-neutral-600 resize-none"
        />
        <p className="text-[11.5px] text-neutral-500 mt-2 leading-relaxed">
          Facoltativo. Compare sullo schermo del box accanto al WOD; nell'app dei soci non si vede.
        </p>
      </Card>

      <Card className="p-4">
        <TextField
          label="Formato"
          value={d.title}
          onChange={(v) => set({ title: v })}
          placeholder="Es. FOR TIME, AMRAP 20', EMOM 12'"
        />
        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2 mt-3">
          {WOD_FORMATS.map((f) => (
            <button
              key={f}
              onClick={() => set({ title: f === "AMRAP" || f === "EMOM" ? `${f} ` : f })}
              className="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-full border border-neutral-800 text-[12px] text-neutral-300 hover:border-neutral-600"
            >
              {f}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
      </Card>

      <section>
        <span className="block text-[11px] text-neutral-500 mb-2">Tipo di score</span>
        <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2">
          {SCORE_TYPES.map((t) => {
            const Icon = t.icon;
            const active = d.scoreType === t.key;
            return (
              <button
                key={t.key}
                disabled={typeLocked && !active}
                onClick={() => set({ scoreType: t.key })}
                className={`rounded-2xl border p-3 text-left transition-colors disabled:opacity-35 ${
                  active ? "bg-neutral-200 border-neutral-200 text-black" : "bg-neutral-900/60 border-neutral-800 text-neutral-300"
                }`}
              >
                <Icon size={18} strokeWidth={1.8} />
                <div className="text-[13px] font-semibold mt-2">{t.label}</div>
              </button>
            );
          })}
        </div>
        <p className="text-[11.5px] text-neutral-500 mt-2 leading-relaxed">
          {typeLocked
            ? `Ci sono già ${total} score salvati: il tipo di score non si può più cambiare.`
            : SCORE_TYPES.find((t) => t.key === d.scoreType).hint}
        </p>
      </section>

      <label className="block">
        <span className="block text-[11px] text-neutral-500 mb-2">Time cap</span>
        <input
          value={d.cap}
          inputMode="numeric"
          onChange={(e) => set({ cap: maskTime(e.target.value) })}
          placeholder="Nessuno"
          className="w-36 bg-neutral-900 border border-neutral-800 rounded-2xl px-3.5 py-3 text-[14px] text-neutral-100 tabular-nums placeholder:text-neutral-600 outline-none focus:border-neutral-600"
        />
        <span className={`block text-[11.5px] mt-2 ${capOk ? "text-neutral-500" : "text-amber-400"}`}>
          Facoltativo. Scrivi solo le cifre: i due punti si inseriscono da soli.
        </span>
      </label>

      <section className="space-y-3">
        <span className="block text-[11px] text-neutral-500">Versioni</span>
        {WOD_VARIANTS.map((v) =>
          d.variants[v] !== undefined ? (
            <VariantEditor
              key={v}
              variant={v}
              value={d.variants[v]}
              onChange={(text) => setVariant(v, text)}
              onRemove={() => removeVariant(v)}
              removeLocked={(scoreCounts[v] || 0) > 0}
            />
          ) : (
            <button
              key={v}
              onClick={() => setVariant(v, d.variants[sourceFor(v)] || "")}
              className="w-full border border-dashed border-neutral-800 rounded-2xl px-4 py-3.5 flex items-center gap-3 text-left hover:border-neutral-600"
            >
              <span className="w-9 h-9 rounded-xl bg-neutral-900 border border-neutral-800 flex items-center justify-center text-neutral-300 shrink-0">
                <Plus size={16} />
              </span>
              <span className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                <span className="block text-[13.5px] text-neutral-100">Aggiungi {VARIANT_LABEL[v]}</span>
                <span className="block text-[11.5px] text-neutral-500">
                  Parte da una copia di {VARIANT_LABEL[sourceFor(v)]}: cambi solo quello che serve.
                </span>
              </span>
            </button>
          )
        )}
      </section>

      <div>
        {missing && <p className="text-[12px] text-neutral-500 mb-3">{missing}</p>}
        <div className="flex gap-2.5">
          <button
            onClick={onCancel}
            className="flex-1 py-3.5 rounded-2xl border border-neutral-700 text-neutral-300 text-[14px]"
          >
            Annulla
          </button>
          <button
            onClick={() => onSave(d)}
            disabled={Boolean(missing)}
            className="flex-1 py-3.5 rounded-2xl bg-neutral-200 text-black font-semibold text-[14px] hover:bg-white disabled:opacity-40"
          >
            {isNew ? "Pubblica" : "Salva modifiche"}
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function WodSection({ members, wods, setWods, scores, setScores, onToast }) {
  const [selected, setSelected] = useState(() => {
    const t = offsetDay(0);
    return isClosedDay(t) ? offsetDay(1) : t;
  });
  const [editor, setEditor] = useState(null);
  const [sheet, setSheet] = useState(null); // "copyFrom" | "duplicate" | "delete"
  const [scoreOpen, setScoreOpen] = useState(null);
  const [tvOpen, setTvOpen] = useState(false);

  const days = useMemo(() => Array.from({ length: 21 }, (_, i) => offsetDay(i - 7)), []);

  const wod = wods[selected];
  const dayScores = scores[selected] || [];
  const closed = isClosedDay(selected);
  const future = daysTo(selected) > 0;
  const nameOf = (id) => (members.find((m) => m.id === id) || {}).name || "Socio";

  function draftFrom(w) {
    return { title: w.title, scoreType: w.scoreType, cap: w.cap || "", warmUp: w.warmUp || "", variants: { ...w.variants } };
  }

  function saveWod(draft) {
    const variants = {};
    WOD_VARIANTS.forEach((v) => {
      const text = wodLines(draft.variants[v]).join("\n");
      if (text) variants[v] = text;
    });
    const prev = editor.isNew ? null : wods[editor.iso];
    setWods({
      ...wods,
      [editor.iso]: {
        title: draft.title.trim(),
        scoreType: draft.scoreType,
        cap: draft.cap,
        warmUp: wodLines(draft.warmUp).join("\n"),
        variants,
        author: prev ? prev.author : CURRENT_STAFF,
        publishedAt: prev ? prev.publishedAt : offsetDay(0),
        editedAt: prev ? offsetDay(0) : null,
        editedBy: prev ? CURRENT_STAFF : null,
      },
    });
    onToast(prev ? "Modifiche salvate: i soci le vedono già." : `WOD pubblicato per ${fmtDayShort(editor.iso)}.`);
    setSelected(editor.iso);
    setEditor(null);
  }

  function duplicateTo(iso) {
    setWods({
      ...wods,
      [iso]: { ...draftFrom(wod), author: CURRENT_STAFF, publishedAt: offsetDay(0), editedAt: null, editedBy: null },
    });
    setSheet(null);
    setSelected(iso);
    onToast(`WOD copiato su ${fmtDayShort(iso)} e già visibile ai soci.`);
  }

  function deleteWod() {
    const nextW = { ...wods };
    const nextS = { ...scores };
    delete nextW[selected];
    delete nextS[selected];
    setWods(nextW);
    setScores(nextS);
    setSheet(null);
    onToast("WOD eliminato.");
  }

  function saveScore(s) {
    setScores({
      ...scores,
      [selected]: dayScores.map((x) => (x.id === s.id ? { ...s, editedBy: CURRENT_STAFF } : x)),
    });
    setScoreOpen(null);
    onToast("Score corretto.");
  }

  function deleteScore(s) {
    setScores({ ...scores, [selected]: dayScores.filter((x) => x.id !== s.id) });
    setScoreOpen(null);
    onToast("Score eliminato.");
  }

  if (editor) {
    return (
      <WodEditor
        key={`${editor.iso}-${editor.isNew}`}
        iso={editor.iso}
        isNew={editor.isNew}
        initial={editor.draft}
        scoreCounts={editor.isNew ? {} : countByVariant(scores[editor.iso])}
        onCancel={() => setEditor(null)}
        onSave={saveWod}
      />
    );
  }

  const otherWods = Object.keys(wods)
    .filter((iso) => iso !== selected)
    .sort((a, b) => (a < b ? 1 : -1));

  const freeDays = Array.from({ length: 15 }, (_, i) => offsetDay(i)).filter(
    (iso) => iso !== selected && !isClosedDay(iso) && !wods[iso]
  );

  return (
    <div className="space-y-5">
      <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-3">
        <div className="min-w-0">
          <h1 className="text-neutral-50 text-[26px]" style={{ fontFamily: "Barlow, sans-serif", fontWeight: 700 }}>
            WOD
          </h1>
          <p className="text-[13px] text-neutral-500 mt-1">Quello che pubblichi compare subito nell'app e sulla TV.</p>
        </div>
        <button
          onClick={() => setTvOpen(true)}
          className="h-11 px-3.5 rounded-2xl border border-neutral-800 bg-neutral-900/60 text-neutral-200 text-[13px] flex items-center gap-2 hover:border-neutral-600 shrink-0"
        >
          <Tv size={17} />
          Schermo TV
        </button>
      </div>

      <WeekStrip days={days} selected={selected} onSelect={setSelected} wods={wods} />

      <div className="flex items-baseline justify-between gap-3">
        <h2 className="text-neutral-50 text-[19px]" style={{ fontFamily: "Barlow, sans-serif", fontWeight: 700 }}>
          {fmtDayLong(selected)}
        </h2>
        <span className="text-[12px] text-neutral-500 shrink-0">{relativeDay(selected)}</span>
      </div>

      {wod ? (
        <>
          <WodCard
            key={selected}
            wod={wod}
            onEdit={() => setEditor({ iso: selected, isNew: false, draft: draftFrom(wod) })}
            onDuplicate={() => setSheet("duplicate")}
            onDelete={() => setSheet("delete")}
          />
          {future ? (
            <p className="text-[12px] text-neutral-500">Gli score si aprono il giorno del WOD.</p>
          ) : (
            <WodScores
              key={`s-${selected}`}
              wod={wod}
              scores={dayScores}
              members={members}
              onOpen={setScoreOpen}
            />
          )}
        </>
      ) : closed ? (
        <div className="border border-dashed border-neutral-800 rounded-2xl px-6 py-10 text-center">
          <Ban size={26} strokeWidth={1.4} className="mx-auto text-neutral-600" />
          <div className="text-neutral-300 text-[15px] mt-3">Box chiuso</div>
          <p className="text-neutral-500 text-[12.5px] mt-1.5">Nei giorni di chiusura non c'è WOD.</p>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="border border-dashed border-neutral-800 rounded-2xl px-6 py-10 text-center">
          <Dumbbell size={28} strokeWidth={1.4} className="mx-auto text-neutral-600" />
          <div className="text-neutral-200 text-[15px] mt-3">
            {daysTo(selected) < 0 ? "Nessun WOD pubblicato" : "WOD ancora da scrivere"}
          </div>
          <p className="text-neutral-500 text-[12.5px] mt-1.5 max-w-xs mx-auto leading-relaxed">
