            {daysTo(selected) < 0
              ? "Puoi aggiungerlo anche adesso: i soci potranno salvare il loro score."
              : "Appena lo pubblichi compare nell'app dei soci."}
          </p>
          <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row gap-2.5 justify-center mt-5">
            <button
              onClick={() =>
                setEditor({ iso: selected, isNew: true, draft: { title: "", scoreType: "time", cap: "", warmUp: "", variants: { RX: "" } } })
              }
              className="px-5 py-3 rounded-2xl bg-neutral-200 text-black text-[13.5px] font-semibold hover:bg-white flex items-center justify-center gap-2"
            >
              <Plus size={16} />
              Scrivi il WOD
            </button>
            {otherWods.length > 0 && (
              <button
                onClick={() => setSheet("copyFrom")}
                className="px-5 py-3 rounded-2xl border border-neutral-700 text-neutral-200 text-[13.5px] hover:border-neutral-500 flex items-center justify-center gap-2"
              >
                <Copy size={15} />
                Parti da un WOD già fatto
              </button>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {sheet === "copyFrom" && (
        <PickDaySheet
          title="Parti da un WOD già fatto"
          subtitle="Si apre già compilato: lo sistemi e poi lo pubblichi."
          options={otherWods.map((iso) => ({ key: iso, label: wods[iso].title, detail: fmtDayLong(iso) }))}
          confirmLabel="Usa questo"
          emptyText="Non ci sono altri WOD."
          onClose={() => setSheet(null)}
          onConfirm={(iso) => {
            setSheet(null);
            setEditor({ iso: selected, isNew: true, draft: draftFrom(wods[iso]) });
          }}
        />
      )}

      {sheet === "duplicate" && wod && (
        <PickDaySheet
          title="Copia su un altro giorno"
          subtitle="Il WOD viene pubblicato subito sul giorno che scegli. Gli score non vengono copiati."
          options={freeDays.map((iso) => ({ key: iso, label: fmtDayLong(iso), detail: relativeDay(iso) }))}
          confirmLabel="Copia"
          emptyText="Nei prossimi 14 giorni non ci sono giorni senza WOD."
          onClose={() => setSheet(null)}
          onConfirm={duplicateTo}
        />
      )}

      {sheet === "delete" && wod && (
        <ConfirmDialog
          title="Eliminare il WOD?"
          body={
            dayScores.length
              ? `Sparisce dall'app dei soci insieme ai ${dayScores.length} score già salvati. Non si può annullare.`
              : "Sparisce dall'app dei soci. Non si può annullare."
          }
          confirmLabel="Elimina"
          danger
          onCancel={() => setSheet(null)}
          onConfirm={deleteWod}
        />
      )}

      {tvOpen && <TvPreview iso={selected} wod={wod} onClose={() => setTvOpen(false)} />}

      {scoreOpen && wod && (
        <ScoreSheet
          score={scoreOpen}
          wod={wod}
          name={nameOf(scoreOpen.memberId)}
          onClose={() => setScoreOpen(null)}
          onSave={saveScore}
          onDelete={() => deleteScore(scoreOpen)}
        />
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

/* ---------- Schermo TV ----------
   In produzione è la pagina /tv/<codice>, aperta dalla chiavetta collegata
   alla TV. Mostra warm up e WOD del giorno; il warm up c'è solo qui e nel
   gestionale, nell'app dei soci non compare. */

const TV_W = 1920;
const TV_H = 1080;

/** Il testo deve stare nello schermo senza scorrere: la dimensione dipende
    sia da quante righe ci sono sia da quanto è larga la colonna. */
function tvLineSize(columnsLines, columnWidth, maxHeight, maxSize) {
  // Parte dal carattere più grande e scende finché ogni colonna, righe
  // spezzate comprese, sta nell'altezza disponibile.
  for (let size = maxSize; size > 24; size -= 2) {
    const fits = columnsLines.every((lines) => {
      const height = lines.reduce((h, l) => {
        const rows = Math.max(1, Math.ceil((l.length * 0.55 * size) / columnWidth));
        return h + (isHeaderLine(l) ? size * 1.3 : rows * size * 1.25 + size * 0.32);
      }, 0);
      return height <= maxHeight;
    });
    if (fits) return size;
  }
  return 24;
}

function TvLines({ lines, size, color = "#f5f5f5" }) {
  return (
    <div>
      {lines.map((l, i) =>
        isHeaderLine(l) ? (
          <div
            key={i}
            style={{
              fontSize: size * 0.62,
              letterSpacing: "0.14em",
              color: "#8a8a8a",
              fontWeight: 600,
              marginTop: i === 0 ? 0 : size * 0.7,
              marginBottom: size * 0.25,
            }}
          >
            {l}
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div key={i} style={{ fontSize: size, lineHeight: 1.25, color, fontWeight: 500, marginBottom: size * 0.32 }}>
            {l}
          </div>
        )
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

function TvCanvas({ iso, wod, now }) {
  const d = new Date(`${iso}T00:00:00`);
  const dateLabel = `${DAY_FULL[d.getDay()]} ${d.getDate()} ${MONTH_FULL[d.getMonth()]}`.toUpperCase();
  const clock = `${String(now.getHours()).padStart(2, "0")}:${String(now.getMinutes()).padStart(2, "0")}`;

  const isToday = iso === offsetDay(0);
  const nowHm = clock;
  const classes = SCHEDULE_BY_WEEKDAY[d.getDay()] || [];
  const next = isToday ? classes.find((t) => t > nowHm) : classes[0];

  const barlow = { fontFamily: "Barlow, sans-serif" };
  const warm = wod ? wodLines(wod.warmUp) : [];
  const variants = wod ? WOD_VARIANTS.filter((v) => wod.variants[v]) : [];
  const variantLines = variants.map((v) => wodLines(wod.variants[v]));
  const warmWidth = 440;
  const wodWidth = TV_W - 144 - (warm.length ? warmWidth + 56 + 64 : 0);
  const columnWidth = (wodWidth - 40 * Math.max(0, variants.length - 1)) / Math.max(1, variants.length);
  const lineSize = tvLineSize(variantLines, columnWidth, 600, 64);
  const warmSize = tvLineSize([warm], warmWidth, 720, 38);
  const type = wod ? SCORE_TYPES.find((t) => t.key === wod.scoreType) : null;

  const tagStyle = {
    RX: { background: "#f5f5f5", color: "#000", border: "3px solid #f5f5f5" },
    SCALED: { background: "#404040", color: "#f5f5f5", border: "3px solid #404040" },
    OPEN: { border: "3px solid #737373", color: "#d4d4d4" },
  };

  return (
    <div
      style={{
        width: TV_W,
        height: TV_H,
        background: "#000",
        color: "#f5f5f5",
        fontFamily: "Montserrat, sans-serif",
        padding: "56px 72px",
        display: "flex",
        flexDirection: "column",
        boxSizing: "border-box",
      }}
    >
      {/* Testata */}
      <div style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "space-between", paddingBottom: 32, borderBottom: "2px solid #262626" }}>
        <div style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "center", gap: 36 }}>
          <img src={LOGO_SRC} alt="PEAK" style={{ height: 84, width: "auto" }} />
          <div style={{ ...barlow, fontWeight: 700, fontSize: 38, letterSpacing: "0.06em", color: "#a3a3a3" }}>{dateLabel}</div>
        </div>
        <div style={{ textAlign: "right" }}>
          {isToday && <div style={{ ...barlow, fontWeight: 800, fontSize: 76, lineHeight: 1 }}>{clock}</div>}
          <div style={{ fontSize: 24, color: "#a3a3a3", marginTop: isToday ? 8 : 0 }}>
            {next ? `${isToday ? "Prossima classe" : "Prima classe"} ${next}` : isToday ? "Classi finite per oggi" : ""}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {!wod ? (
        <div style={{ flex: 1, display: "flex", flexDirection: "column", alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "center" }}>
          <div style={{ ...barlow, fontWeight: 800, fontSize: 150, lineHeight: 1 }}>
            {isClosedDay(iso) ? "BOX CHIUSO" : "WOD IN ARRIVO"}
          </div>
          <div style={{ fontSize: 34, color: "#737373", marginTop: 28 }}>
            {isClosedDay(iso) ? "Ci vediamo domani." : "Il coach lo sta scrivendo."}
          </div>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div style={{ flex: 1, minHeight: 0, display: "flex", gap: 64, paddingTop: 44 }}>
          {warm.length > 0 && (
            <div style={{ width: warmWidth, flexShrink: 0, borderRight: "2px solid #262626", paddingRight: 56, overflow: "hidden" }}>
              <div style={{ ...barlow, fontWeight: 800, fontSize: 54, lineHeight: 1, marginBottom: 34, color: "#d4d4d4" }}>
                WARM UP
              </div>
              <TvLines lines={warm} size={warmSize} color="#d4d4d4" />
            </div>
          )}

          <div style={{ flex: 1, minWidth: 0, display: "flex", flexDirection: "column" }}>
            <div style={{ display: "flex", alignItems: "flex-end", justifyContent: "space-between", gap: 32, marginBottom: 40 }}>
              <div style={{ ...barlow, fontWeight: 800, fontSize: wod.title.length > 14 ? 84 : 112, lineHeight: 0.9, whiteSpace: "nowrap", overflow: "hidden", textOverflow: "ellipsis", minWidth: 0 }}>
                {wod.title}
              </div>
              <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 16, flexShrink: 0 }}>
                {wod.cap && (
                  <div style={{ border: "2px solid #404040", borderRadius: 18, padding: "12px 24px", textAlign: "center" }}>
                    <div style={{ fontSize: 18, color: "#a3a3a3", letterSpacing: "0.12em" }}>TIME CAP</div>
                    <div style={{ ...barlow, fontWeight: 800, fontSize: 52, lineHeight: 1.05 }}>{wod.cap}</div>
                  </div>
                )}
                <div style={{ border: "2px solid #404040", borderRadius: 18, padding: "12px 24px", textAlign: "center" }}>
                  <div style={{ fontSize: 18, color: "#a3a3a3", letterSpacing: "0.12em" }}>SCORE</div>
                  <div style={{ ...barlow, fontWeight: 800, fontSize: 52, lineHeight: 1.05 }}>{type.label.toUpperCase()}</div>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div style={{ flex: 1, minHeight: 0, display: "grid", gridTemplateColumns: `repeat(${variants.length}, minmax(0, 1fr))`, gap: 40 }}>
              {variants.map((v, i) => (
                <div key={v} style={{ minWidth: 0, overflow: "hidden" }}>
                  <div
                    style={{
                      ...barlow,
                      ...tagStyle[v],
                      display: "inline-block",
                      fontWeight: 800,
                      fontSize: 36,
                      letterSpacing: "0.08em",
                      padding: "6px 22px",
                      borderRadius: 12,
                      marginBottom: 28,
                    }}
                  >
                    {v}
                  </div>
                  <TvLines lines={variantLines[i]} size={lineSize} />
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

function TvPreview({ iso, wod, onClose }) {
  useLockBodyScroll();
  const boxRef = React.useRef(null);
  const [scale, setScale] = useState(0.3);
  const [now, setNow] = useState(new Date());

  useEffect(() => {
    const fit = () => {
      const el = boxRef.current;
      if (!el) return;
      setScale(Math.min(el.clientWidth / TV_W, el.clientHeight / TV_H));
    };
    fit();
    window.addEventListener("resize", fit);
    const t = setInterval(() => setNow(new Date()), 15000);
    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener("resize", fit);
      clearInterval(t);
    };
  }, []);

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-neutral-950 flex flex-col">
      <div className="shrink-0 flex items-center gap-3 px-4 py-3 border-b border-neutral-800">
        <Tv size={18} className="text-neutral-400" />
        <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
          <div className="text-[14px] text-neutral-100 font-semibold">Anteprima schermo TV</div>
          <div className="text-[11.5px] text-neutral-500 truncate">
            {fmtDayLong(iso)} · il warm up si vede solo qui, non nell'app dei soci
          </div>
        </div>
        <button
          onClick={onClose}
          aria-label="Chiudi anteprima"
          className="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl border border-neutral-800 flex items-center justify-center text-neutral-300 hover:border-neutral-600"
        >
          <X size={18} />
        </button>
      </div>
      <div ref={boxRef} className="flex-1 min-h-0 relative overflow-hidden m-3">
        <div
          style={{
            position: "absolute",
            left: "50%",
            top: "50%",
            transform: `translate(-50%, -50%) scale(${scale})`,
            boxShadow: "0 0 0 1px #262626",
          }}
        >
          <TvCanvas iso={iso} wod={wod} now={now} />
        </div>
      </div>
      <p className="shrink-0 text-center text-[11.5px] text-neutral-600 pb-3 px-4 sm:hidden">
        Ruota il telefono per vederla più grande.
      </p>
    </div>
  );
}

/* ============================================================
   SEZIONE CLASSI E PRESENZE
   Giornata · Dettaglio classe · Conferma presenze

   Regole decise il 7 ottobre 2026:
   - La lista presenze parte vuota: il coach spunta chi c'era.
   - Il no-show resta nello storico e abbassa il tasso di presenza,
     ma non scala ingressi e non blocca le prenotazioni.
   - Chi arriva senza prenotazione si aggiunge in conferma, solo se
     c'è posto. Conta come presenza.
   - Ogni classe ha un coach: conferma solo le sue. Senza coach
     conferma un admin.
   ============================================================ */

const CLASS_MINUTES = 60;
const COACH_BY_TIME = { "06:30": 1, "12:45": 5, "17:30": 4, "18:30": 4, "19:30": 6, "10:30": 5, "11:30": 1 };
const CURRENT_STAFF_ID = 1;
const barlow = (weight) => ({ fontFamily: "Barlow, sans-serif", fontWeight: weight });

function endTime(time) {
  const [h, m] = time.split(":").map(Number);
  const tot = h * 60 + m + CLASS_MINUTES;
  return `${String(Math.floor(tot / 60)).padStart(2, "0")}:${String(tot % 60).padStart(2, "0")}`;
}

/* ---------- Stato della classe ----------
   futura → incorso → daconfermare → confermata.
   Le presenze si possono confermare dall'inizio della classe in poi. */

function phaseOf(s, now = new Date()) {
  const start = new Date(`${s.iso}T${s.time}:00`);
  const end = new Date(start.getTime() + CLASS_MINUTES * 60000);
  if (s.attendance) return "confermata";
  if (now < start) return "futura";
  if (now < end) return "incorso";
  return "daconfermare";
}

const PHASE = {
  futura: { label: "In programma", chip: "bg-neutral-500/15 text-neutral-400 border-neutral-600/40" },
  incorso: { label: "In corso", chip: "bg-neutral-100 text-black border-neutral-100" },
  daconfermare: { label: "Da confermare", chip: "bg-amber-400/15 text-amber-300 border-amber-400/30" },
  confermata: { label: "Confermate", chip: "bg-emerald-500/15 text-emerald-300 border-emerald-500/30" },
};


function buildSessions(members) {
  const out = {};
  const now = new Date();
  const nameOf = (id) => (members.find((m) => m.id === id) || {}).name || "Socio";
  for (let off = -7; off <= 7; off++) {
    const iso = offsetDay(off);
    const times = SCHEDULE_BY_WEEKDAY[new Date(`${iso}T00:00:00`).getDay()] || [];
    times.forEach((time, idx) => {
      const key = `${iso}|${time}`;
      const pool = members.filter((m) => !blockReason(m)).sort(
        (a, b) => seeded(a.id + key, 9972) - seeded(b.id + key, 9972)
      );
      const demand = 7 + seeded("d" + key, 11); // da 7 a 18 richieste
      const s = {
        key,
        iso,
        time,
        // Una classe di domani senza coach, per mostrare il caso.
        coachId: off === 1 && idx === 0 ? null : COACH_BY_TIME[time],
        roster: pool.slice(0, Math.min(CLASS_CAPACITY, demand)).map((m) => m.id),
        waitlist: [],
        walkins: [],
        attendance: null,
      };
      const start = new Date(`${iso}T${time}:00`);
      const endedFor = (now - start) / 60000 - CLASS_MINUTES;
      if (now < start && demand > CLASS_CAPACITY) {
        s.waitlist = pool.slice(CLASS_CAPACITY, demand).map((m) => m.id);
      }
      // Le classi vecchie sono già confermate. Restano aperte l'ultima di
      // ieri e quelle finite da poco oggi.
      const auto =
        off < -1 || (off === -1 && idx < times.length - 1) || (off === 0 && endedFor > 180);
      if (auto) {
        s.attendance = {
          present: s.roster.filter((id) => seeded(id + "p" + key, 9) > 0),
          by: nameOf(s.coachId || CURRENT_STAFF_ID),
        };
      }
      out[key] = s;
    });
  }
  return out;
}

function ClassChip({ className, children }) {
  return (
    <span className={`text-[11px] rounded-lg px-2 py-0.5 border shrink-0 whitespace-nowrap ${className}`}>
      {children}
    </span>
  );
}

function PickSheet({ title, subtitle, children, onClose }) {
  useLockBodyScroll();
  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-40 flex items-end sm:items-center justify-center pt-10 sm:p-6">
      <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-black/75" onClick={onClose} />
      <div className="relative w-full sm:max-w-sm flex flex-col max-h-full bg-neutral-950 border border-neutral-800 rounded-t-3xl sm:rounded-3xl overflow-hidden">
        <div className="shrink-0 px-5 pt-5 pb-3 flex items-start gap-3">
          <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
            <h3 className="text-neutral-50 text-[17px]" style={barlow(700)}>
              {title}
            </h3>
            {subtitle && (
              <div className="text-[13px] text-neutral-400 mt-1.5 leading-relaxed">{subtitle}</div>
            )}
          </div>
          <button
            onClick={onClose}
            aria-label="Chiudi"
            className="w-9 h-9 rounded-xl border border-neutral-800 flex items-center justify-center text-neutral-300 shrink-0"
          >
            <X size={17} />
          </button>
        </div>
        <div
          className="flex-1 min-h-0 overflow-y-auto overscroll-contain px-5"
          style={{ paddingBottom: "max(1.25rem, env(safe-area-inset-bottom))" }}
        >
          {children}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

/* ---------- Scelta del coach ---------- */

function CoachSheet({ session, staff, onClose, onPick }) {
  const options = [...staff.map((m) => ({ id: m.id, name: m.name })), { id: null, name: "Nessun coach" }];
  return (
    <PickSheet
      title="Coach della classe"
      subtitle="Chi tiene la classe è anche chi ne conferma le presenze."
      onClose={onClose}
    >
      <div className="space-y-2">
        {options.map((o) => {
          const active = session.coachId === o.id;
          return (
            <button
              key={String(o.id)}
              onClick={() => onPick(o.id)}
              className={`w-full flex items-center gap-3 px-3.5 py-3 rounded-2xl border text-left ${
                active ? "border-neutral-500 bg-neutral-900" : "border-neutral-800 bg-neutral-900/60"
              }`}
            >
              {o.id ? <Avatar name={o.name} size={34} /> : <Ban size={18} className="text-neutral-500 mx-2" />}
              <span className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                <span className="block text-[13.5px] text-neutral-100 truncate">{o.name}</span>
                {o.id === null && (
                  <span className="block text-[11.5px] text-neutral-500">
                    Le presenze le conferma un admin.
                  </span>
                )}
              </span>
              {active && <Check size={16} className="text-neutral-100 shrink-0" />}
            </button>
          );
        })}
      </div>
    </PickSheet>
  );
}

/* ---------- Aggiunta di un socio ---------- */

function AddMemberSheet({ title, subtitle, members, exclude, onClose, onPick }) {
  const [q, setQ] = useState("");
  const term = q.trim().toLowerCase();
  const list = members
    .map((m) => ({ ...m, block: blockReason(m) }))
    .filter((m) => !exclude.includes(m.id))
    .filter((m) => !term || m.name.toLowerCase().includes(term))
    .sort((a, b) => a.name.localeCompare(b.name));

  return (
    <PickSheet title={title} subtitle={subtitle} onClose={onClose}>
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5 bg-neutral-900 border border-neutral-800 rounded-2xl px-3.5 py-3 mb-3">
        <Search size={17} className="text-neutral-500 shrink-0" />
        <input
          id="add-member-search"
          value={q}
          onChange={(e) => setQ(e.target.value)}
          placeholder="Cerca un socio"
          className="bg-transparent flex-1 text-[14px] text-neutral-100 placeholder:text-neutral-600 outline-none min-w-0"
        />
      </div>
      {list.length ? (
        <div className="space-y-2">
          {list.map((m) => (
            <button
              key={m.id}
              disabled={Boolean(m.block)}
              onClick={() => onPick(m.id)}
              className="w-full flex items-center gap-3 px-3.5 py-2.5 rounded-2xl border border-neutral-800 bg-neutral-900/60 text-left hover:border-neutral-600 disabled:hover:border-neutral-800"
            >
              <span className={m.block ? "opacity-40" : ""}>
                <Avatar name={m.name} size={34} />
              </span>
              <span className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                <span className={`block text-[13.5px] truncate ${m.block ? "text-neutral-500" : "text-neutral-100"}`}>
                  {m.name}
                </span>
                {m.block && <span className="block text-[11.5px] text-red-400/80 truncate">{m.block}</span>}
              </span>
              {!m.block && <Plus size={16} className="text-neutral-400 shrink-0" />}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
      ) : (
        <p className="text-[13px] text-neutral-500 text-center py-6">Nessun socio con questo nome.</p>
      )}
    </PickSheet>
  );
}

/* ---------- Giornata ---------- */

function DayStrip({ days, selected, onSelect, pendingDays }) {
  const activeRef = React.useRef(null);
  const todayIso = offsetDay(0);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (activeRef.current) activeRef.current.scrollIntoView({ inline: "center", block: "nearest" });
  }, [selected]);

  return (
    <div className="flex gap-1.5 overflow-x-auto pb-1 -mx-5 px-5 lg:mx-0 lg:px-0" style={{ scrollbarWidth: "none" }}>
      {days.map((iso) => {
        const d = new Date(`${iso}T00:00:00`);
        const closed = isClosedDay(iso);
        const active = iso === selected;
        const isToday = iso === todayIso;
        return (
          <button
            key={iso}
            ref={active ? activeRef : null}
            onClick={() => onSelect(iso)}
            className={`shrink-0 w-[54px] rounded-2xl py-2.5 flex flex-col items-center border transition-colors ${
              active
                ? "bg-neutral-200 border-neutral-200 text-black"
                : closed
                ? "border-neutral-900 text-neutral-600"
                : "bg-neutral-900/60 border-neutral-800 text-neutral-300"
            }`}
          >
            <span
              className={`text-[10px] uppercase tracking-wide ${
                active ? "text-black/60" : isToday ? "text-neutral-50 font-semibold" : ""
              }`}
            >
              {isToday ? "Oggi" : DAY_FULL[d.getDay()].slice(0, 3)}
            </span>
            <span className="text-[18px] mt-0.5" style={barlow(700)}>
              {d.getDate()}
            </span>
            <span
              className={`w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full mt-1 ${
                pendingDays.has(iso) ? (active ? "bg-amber-600" : "bg-amber-400") : "bg-transparent"
              }`}
            />
          </button>
        );
      })}
    </div>
  );
}

function ClassRow({ s, nameOf, onOpen }) {
  const phase = phaseOf(s);
  const p = PHASE[phase];
  const absent = s.attendance ? s.roster.length - s.attendance.present.length : 0;
  return (
    <button
      onClick={() => onOpen(s.key)}
      className="w-full flex items-center gap-3.5 px-4 py-3.5 text-left hover:bg-neutral-900/60"
    >
      <span className="text-neutral-100 text-[16px] w-12 shrink-0 tabular-nums" style={barlow(700)}>
        {s.time}
      </span>
      <span className="flex-1 min-w-0">
        <span className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <span className="text-[14px] text-neutral-100">Functional</span>
          <ClassChip className={p.chip}>{p.label}</ClassChip>
        </span>
        <span className={`block text-[12px] truncate mt-0.5 ${s.coachId ? "text-neutral-500" : "text-amber-300"}`}>
          {s.coachId ? `Coach ${nameOf(s.coachId)}` : "Coach da assegnare"}
          {s.waitlist.length > 0 && <span className="text-neutral-500"> · {s.waitlist.length} in attesa</span>}
        </span>
      </span>
      <span className="shrink-0 text-right">
        {s.attendance ? (
          <>
            <span className="block text-[14px] text-neutral-100 tabular-nums">
              {s.attendance.present.length} presenti
            </span>
            <span className="block text-[11.5px] text-neutral-500 tabular-nums">
              {absent === 0 ? "nessun assente" : absent === 1 ? "1 assente" : `${absent} assenti`}
            </span>
          </>
        ) : (
          <span className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <span className="text-[14px] text-neutral-300 tabular-nums">
              {s.roster.length}/{CLASS_CAPACITY}
            </span>
            <span className={`w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full ${TONE_DOT[fillTone(s.roster.length, CLASS_CAPACITY)]}`} />
          </span>
        )}
      </span>
      <ChevronRight size={16} className="text-neutral-600 shrink-0" />
    </button>
  );
}

function DayView({ sessions, selected, onSelect, onOpen, nameOf }) {
  const days = useMemo(() => Array.from({ length: 15 }, (_, i) => offsetDay(i - 7)), []);
  const all = Object.values(sessions);
  const pending = all
    .filter((s) => phaseOf(s) === "daconfermare")
    .sort((a, b) => (a.key < b.key ? -1 : 1));
  const pendingDays = new Set(pending.map((s) => s.iso));
  const list = all.filter((s) => s.iso === selected).sort((a, b) => (a.time < b.time ? -1 : 1));
  const closed = isClosedDay(selected);
  const booked = list.reduce((n, s) => n + s.roster.length, 0);
  const elsewhere = pending.filter((s) => s.iso !== selected);

  return (
    <div className="space-y-5">
      <div>
        <h1 className="text-neutral-50 text-[28px] leading-tight" style={barlow(700)}>
          Classi
        </h1>
        <p className="text-[14px] text-neutral-400 mt-1">Iscritti, coach e presenze di ogni classe.</p>
      </div>

      {elsewhere.length > 0 && (
        <div className="bg-amber-400/10 border border-amber-400/25 rounded-2xl overflow-hidden">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5 px-4 pt-3.5 pb-2">
            <AlertTriangle size={16} className="text-amber-300 shrink-0" strokeWidth={1.9} />
            <span className="text-[13.5px] text-amber-200 font-semibold">
              {elsewhere.length === 1 ? "1 classe da confermare" : `${elsewhere.length} classi da confermare`} in altri giorni
            </span>
          </div>
          <div className="divide-y divide-amber-400/15">
            {elsewhere.slice(0, 3).map((s) => (
              <button
                key={s.key}
                onClick={() => onOpen(s.key)}
                className="w-full flex items-center gap-3 px-4 py-2.5 text-left hover:bg-amber-400/10"
              >
                <span className="flex-1 text-[13px] text-amber-100/90 truncate">
                  {fmtDayLong(s.iso)} · {s.time}
                </span>
                <span className="text-[12px] text-amber-200/60 shrink-0">{relativeDay(s.iso)}</span>
                <ChevronRight size={15} className="text-amber-300/60 shrink-0" />
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      <DayStrip days={days} selected={selected} onSelect={onSelect} pendingDays={pendingDays} />

      <div className="flex items-baseline justify-between gap-3">
        <h2 className="text-neutral-50 text-[19px]" style={barlow(700)}>
          {fmtDayLong(selected)}
        </h2>
        <span className="text-[12px] text-neutral-500 shrink-0">
          {closed ? relativeDay(selected) : `${list.length} classi · ${booked} iscritti`}
        </span>
      </div>

      {closed ? (
        <div className="border border-dashed border-neutral-800 rounded-2xl px-6 py-10 text-center">
          <Ban size={26} strokeWidth={1.4} className="mx-auto text-neutral-600" />
          <div className="text-neutral-300 text-[15px] mt-3">Box chiuso</div>
          <p className="text-neutral-500 text-[12.5px] mt-1.5">La domenica non ci sono classi.</p>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <Card className="divide-y divide-neutral-800 overflow-hidden">
          {list.map((s) => (
            <ClassRow key={s.key} s={s} nameOf={nameOf} onOpen={onOpen} />
          ))}
        </Card>
      )}

      <p className="text-[12px] text-neutral-500 leading-relaxed border-t border-neutral-800 pt-4">
        Il pallino ambra sul giorno segnala classi finite con le presenze ancora da confermare.
        Finché non le confermi, quelle sessioni non entrano nelle statistiche dei soci.
      </p>
    </div>
  );
}

/* ---------- Conferma presenze ----------
   La lista parte vuota: si spunta chi c'era. Chi resta senza spunta
   viene registrato come assente. */

function AttendanceScreen({ session, members, nameOf, onCancel, onConfirm }) {
  const correcting = Boolean(session.attendance);
  const [checked, setChecked] = useState(() => new Set(session.attendance ? session.attendance.present : []));
  const [walkins, setWalkins] = useState(session.walkins);
  const [adding, setAdding] = useState(false);
  const [asking, setAsking] = useState(false);

  const roster = [...session.roster.filter((id) => !walkins.includes(id)), ...walkins];
  const free = CLASS_CAPACITY - roster.length;
  const present = roster.filter((id) => checked.has(id)).length;
  const absent = roster.length - present;

  function toggle(id) {
    setChecked((prev) => {
      const next = new Set(prev);
      if (next.has(id)) next.delete(id);
      else next.add(id);
      return next;
    });
  }

  function removeWalkin(id) {
    setWalkins((w) => w.filter((x) => x !== id));
    setChecked((prev) => {
      const next = new Set(prev);
      next.delete(id);
      return next;
    });
  }

  return (
    <div className="space-y-5">
      <BackLink onBack={onCancel} label={`Classe delle ${session.time}`} />

      <div>
        <h1 className="text-neutral-50 text-[24px]" style={barlow(700)}>
          {correcting ? "Correggi presenze" : "Conferma presenze"}
        </h1>
        <p className="text-[13.5px] text-neutral-400 mt-1">
          {fmtDayLong(session.iso)} · {session.time}–{endTime(session.time)} · tocca chi c'era.
        </p>
      </div>

      <Card className="p-4 flex items-end justify-between gap-4">
        <div>
          <div className="text-neutral-50 text-[34px] leading-none tabular-nums" style={barlow(800)}>
            {present}
            <span className="text-[18px] text-neutral-500"> / {roster.length}</span>
          </div>
          <div className="text-[12.5px] text-neutral-400 mt-1.5">presenti</div>
        </div>
        <div className="text-right text-[12.5px] text-neutral-500 leading-relaxed">
          <div>{absent === 1 ? "1 assente" : `${absent} assenti`}</div>
          <div>{walkins.length === 1 ? "1 senza prenotazione" : `${walkins.length} senza prenotazione`}</div>
        </div>
      </Card>

      <Card className="divide-y divide-neutral-800 overflow-hidden">
        {roster.map((id) => {
          const on = checked.has(id);
          const walk = walkins.includes(id);
          return (
            <div key={id} className="flex items-center">
              <button
                onClick={() => toggle(id)}
                aria-pressed={on}
                className="flex-1 min-w-0 flex items-center gap-3.5 px-4 py-3 text-left"
              >
                <span
                  className={`w-7 h-7 rounded-full border-2 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 transition-colors ${
                    on ? "bg-emerald-500 border-emerald-500 text-black" : "border-neutral-600"
                  }`}
                >
                  {on && <Check size={15} strokeWidth={3} />}
                </span>
                <Avatar name={nameOf(id)} size={36} />
                <span className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                  <span className={`block text-[14.5px] truncate ${on ? "text-neutral-50" : "text-neutral-400"}`}>
                    {nameOf(id)}
                  </span>
                  {walk && <span className="block text-[11.5px] text-neutral-500">Senza prenotazione</span>}
                </span>
              </button>
              {walk && (
                <button
                  onClick={() => removeWalkin(id)}
                  aria-label={`Togli ${nameOf(id)}`}
                  className="w-11 h-11 mr-2 flex items-center justify-center text-neutral-500 hover:text-red-400 shrink-0"
                >
                  <X size={17} />
                </button>
              )}
            </div>
          );
        })}
      </Card>

      <button
        onClick={() => setAdding(true)}
        disabled={free <= 0}
        className="w-full border border-dashed border-neutral-800 rounded-2xl px-4 py-3.5 flex items-center gap-3 text-left hover:border-neutral-600 disabled:opacity-50 disabled:hover:border-neutral-800"
      >
        <span className="w-9 h-9 rounded-xl bg-neutral-900 border border-neutral-800 flex items-center justify-center text-neutral-300 shrink-0">
          <UserPlus size={16} />
        </span>
        <span className="flex-1 min-w-0">
          <span className="block text-[13.5px] text-neutral-100">Aggiungi chi è arrivato senza prenotazione</span>
          <span className="block text-[11.5px] text-neutral-500">
            {free > 0
              ? free === 1
                ? "Resta 1 posto su 15."
                : `Restano ${free} posti su 15.`
              : "Classe al completo: 15 su 15, non si aggiunge nessuno."}
          </span>
        </span>
      </button>

      <div className="flex gap-3 bg-neutral-900/60 border border-neutral-800 rounded-2xl p-4">
        <Info size={16} className="text-neutral-400 mt-0.5 shrink-0" strokeWidth={1.8} />
        <div className="text-[12.5px] text-neutral-400 leading-relaxed">
          Chi resta senza spunta viene segnato assente. L'assenza abbassa il suo tasso di presenza,
          ma non scala ingressi e non blocca le prenotazioni.
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="sticky bottom-0 -mx-5 px-5 pt-3 pb-4 bg-black border-t border-neutral-800 flex gap-2.5">
        <button
          onClick={onCancel}
          className="flex-1 py-3.5 rounded-2xl border border-neutral-700 text-neutral-300 text-[14px]"
        >
          Annulla
        </button>
        <button
          onClick={() => setAsking(true)}
          className="flex-1 py-3.5 rounded-2xl bg-neutral-200 text-black font-semibold text-[14px] hover:bg-white"
        >
          Conferma {present} {present === 1 ? "presente" : "presenti"}
        </button>
      </div>

      {adding && (
        <AddMemberSheet
          title="Arrivato senza prenotazione"
          members={members}
          subtitle="Entra nella lista già segnato presente."
          exclude={roster}
          onClose={() => setAdding(false)}
          onPick={(id) => {
            setWalkins((w) => [...w, id]);
            setChecked((prev) => new Set(prev).add(id));
            setAdding(false);
          }}
        />
      )}

      {asking && (
        <ConfirmDialog
          title={present === 0 ? "Nessun presente?" : "Confermare le presenze?"}
          body={
            present === 0
              ? `Non hai spuntato nessuno: tutti i ${roster.length} iscritti risulteranno assenti. Se la classe non si è tenuta, annulla.`
              : `${present} ${present === 1 ? "presente" : "presenti"} e ${
                  absent === 1 ? "1 assente" : `${absent} assenti`
                }. La conferma resta registrata a nome tuo e puoi correggerla dopo.`
          }
          confirmLabel="Conferma"
          onCancel={() => setAsking(false)}
          onConfirm={() => onConfirm({ present: roster.filter((id) => checked.has(id)), walkins, roster })}
        />
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

/* ---------- Dettaglio classe ---------- */

function ClassDetail({ session, members, staff, nameOf, onBack, onUpdate, onToast }) {
  const [mode, setMode] = useState("detail"); // "detail" | "presenze"
  const [sheet, setSheet] = useState(null); // "coach" | "add"
  const [removing, setRemoving] = useState(null);

  const phase = phaseOf(session);
  const p = PHASE[phase];
  const started = phase !== "futura";
  const free = CLASS_CAPACITY - session.roster.length;
  const att = session.attendance;

  if (mode === "presenze") {
    return (
      <AttendanceScreen
        session={session}
        members={members}
        nameOf={nameOf}
        onCancel={() => setMode("detail")}
        onConfirm={({ present, walkins, roster }) => {
          onUpdate({ ...session, roster, walkins, attendance: { present, by: CURRENT_STAFF } });
          setMode("detail");
          onToast(
            `Presenze confermate: ${present.length} ${present.length === 1 ? "presente" : "presenti"}, ${
              roster.length - present.length
            } assenti.`
          );
        }}
      />
    );
  }

  function remove(id) {
    const roster = session.roster.filter((x) => x !== id);
    const [promoted, ...rest] = session.waitlist;
    if (promoted) {
      onUpdate({ ...session, roster: [...roster, promoted], waitlist: rest });
      onToast(`${nameOf(id)} rimosso. ${nameOf(promoted)} entra dalla lista d'attesa e riceve una notifica.`);
    } else {
      onUpdate({ ...session, roster });
      onToast(`${nameOf(id)} rimosso dalla classe.`);
    }
    setRemoving(null);
  }

  return (
    <div className="space-y-5">
      <BackLink onBack={onBack} label="Classi" />

      <div>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5 flex-wrap">
          <h1 className="text-neutral-50 text-[30px] leading-none tabular-nums" style={barlow(800)}>
            {session.time}
          </h1>
          <ClassChip className={p.chip}>{p.label}</ClassChip>
        </div>
        <p className="text-[13.5px] text-neutral-400 mt-2">
          Functional · {fmtDayLong(session.iso)} · fino alle {endTime(session.time)}
        </p>
      </div>

      {phase === "confermata" ? (
        <div className="bg-emerald-500/10 border border-emerald-500/25 rounded-2xl p-4 flex items-center gap-3.5">
          <Check size={18} className="text-emerald-400 shrink-0" strokeWidth={2.2} />
          <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
            <div className="text-[13.5px] text-emerald-200 font-semibold">
              {att.present.length} presenti, {session.roster.length - att.present.length} assenti
            </div>
            <div className="text-[12px] text-emerald-200/60 mt-0.5 truncate">Confermate da {att.by}</div>
          </div>
          <button
            onClick={() => setMode("presenze")}
            className="px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl border border-emerald-500/30 text-emerald-200 text-[12.5px] shrink-0 hover:bg-emerald-500/10"
          >
            Correggi
          </button>
        </div>
      ) : started ? (
        <div className="bg-amber-400/10 border border-amber-400/25 rounded-2xl p-4">
          <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
            <AlertTriangle size={17} className="text-amber-300 mt-0.5 shrink-0" strokeWidth={1.9} />
            <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
              <div className="text-[13.5px] text-amber-200 font-semibold">Presenze da confermare</div>
              <div className="text-[12.5px] text-amber-200/70 mt-1 leading-relaxed">
                {phase === "incorso"
                  ? "La classe è in corso: puoi già segnare chi c'è."
                  : "La classe è finita. Finché non confermi, la sessione non conta per nessuno."}
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
          <button
            onClick={() => setMode("presenze")}
            className="w-full mt-3.5 py-3 rounded-2xl bg-neutral-200 text-black text-[13.5px] font-semibold hover:bg-white"
          >
            Conferma presenze
          </button>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="flex gap-3 bg-neutral-900/60 border border-neutral-800 rounded-2xl p-4">
          <Clock size={16} className="text-neutral-400 mt-0.5 shrink-0" strokeWidth={1.8} />
          <div className="text-[12.5px] text-neutral-400 leading-relaxed">
            Le presenze si confermano dall'inizio della classe, {relativeDay(session.iso)} alle {session.time}.
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      <Card className="divide-y divide-neutral-800 overflow-hidden">
        <button
          onClick={() => setSheet("coach")}
          className="w-full flex items-center gap-3.5 px-4 py-3.5 text-left hover:bg-neutral-900/60"
        >
          <span className="text-[13px] text-neutral-400 w-16 shrink-0">Coach</span>
          <span className={`flex-1 text-[14px] truncate ${session.coachId ? "text-neutral-100" : "text-amber-300"}`}>
            {session.coachId ? nameOf(session.coachId) : "Da assegnare"}
          </span>
          <span className="text-[12.5px] text-neutral-400 shrink-0">Cambia</span>
        </button>
        <div className="px-4 py-3.5">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3.5">
            <span className="text-[13px] text-neutral-400 w-16 shrink-0">Posti</span>
            <span className="flex-1 text-[14px] text-neutral-100 tabular-nums">
              {session.roster.length} su {CLASS_CAPACITY}
            </span>
            <span className="text-[12.5px] text-neutral-500 shrink-0">
              {free > 0 ? (free === 1 ? "1 libero" : `${free} liberi`) : "Al completo"}
            </span>
          </div>
          <div className="h-1.5 w-full bg-neutral-800 rounded-full overflow-hidden mt-3">
            <div
              className={`h-full rounded-full ${TONE_BAR[fillTone(session.roster.length, CLASS_CAPACITY)]}`}
              style={{ width: `${Math.round((session.roster.length / CLASS_CAPACITY) * 100)}%` }}
            />
          </div>
        </div>
      </Card>

      <section>
        <div className="flex items-end justify-between mb-3">
          <h2 className="text-neutral-50 text-[19px]" style={barlow(700)}>
            Iscritti
          </h2>
          {!started && (
            <button
              onClick={() => setSheet("add")}
              disabled={free <= 0}
              className="text-[12.5px] text-neutral-300 hover:text-neutral-50 flex items-center gap-1 disabled:text-neutral-600"
            >
              <Plus size={14} />
              {free > 0 ? "Aggiungi socio" : "Classe piena"}
            </button>
          )}
        </div>
        {session.roster.length ? (
          <Card className="divide-y divide-neutral-800 overflow-hidden">
            {session.roster.map((id) => {
              const walk = session.walkins.includes(id);
              const here = att ? att.present.includes(id) : null;
              return (
                <div key={id} className="flex items-center gap-3.5 px-4 py-3">
                  <Avatar name={nameOf(id)} size={36} />
                  <span className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                    <span className="block text-[14px] text-neutral-100 truncate">{nameOf(id)}</span>
                    {walk && <span className="block text-[11.5px] text-neutral-500">Senza prenotazione</span>}
                  </span>
                  {att ? (
                    <span
                      className={`text-[12px] flex items-center gap-1 shrink-0 ${
                        here ? "text-emerald-400" : "text-neutral-500"
                      }`}
                    >
                      {here ? <Check size={13} /> : <X size={13} />}
                      {here ? "Presente" : "Assente"}
                    </span>
                  ) : (
                    !started && (
                      <button
                        onClick={() => setRemoving(id)}
                        className="text-[12.5px] text-neutral-400 hover:text-red-400 shrink-0"
                      >
                        Rimuovi
                      </button>
                    )
                  )}
                </div>
              );
            })}
          </Card>
        ) : (
          <div className="border border-dashed border-neutral-800 rounded-2xl p-8 text-center text-[13.5px] text-neutral-400">
            Nessun iscritto per ora.
          </div>
        )}
      </section>

      {session.waitlist.length > 0 && (
        <section>
          <div className="flex items-end justify-between mb-3">
            <h2 className="text-neutral-50 text-[19px]" style={barlow(700)}>
              Lista d'attesa
            </h2>
            <span className="text-[12px] text-neutral-500">{session.waitlist.length} in coda</span>
          </div>
          <Card className="divide-y divide-neutral-800 overflow-hidden">
            {session.waitlist.map((id, i) => (
              <div key={id} className="flex items-center gap-3.5 px-4 py-3">
                <span className="w-6 text-center text-[13px] text-neutral-500 tabular-nums shrink-0">{i + 1}</span>
                <Avatar name={nameOf(id)} size={36} />
                <span className="flex-1 text-[14px] text-neutral-100 truncate">{nameOf(id)}</span>
              </div>
            ))}
          </Card>
          <p className="text-[11.5px] text-neutral-500 mt-2.5 leading-relaxed">
            Se un iscritto disdice o lo rimuovi, il primo della coda entra da solo e riceve una notifica.
          </p>
        </section>
      )}

      <p className="text-[12px] text-neutral-500 leading-relaxed border-t border-neutral-800 pt-4">
        Un coach conferma solo le classi che tiene lui. Se la classe non ha un coach assegnato,
        le presenze le conferma un admin.
      </p>

      {sheet === "coach" && (
        <CoachSheet
          session={session}
          staff={staff}
          onClose={() => setSheet(null)}
          onPick={(coachId) => {
            onUpdate({ ...session, coachId });
            setSheet(null);
            onToast(coachId ? `Classe assegnata a ${nameOf(coachId)}.` : "Classe senza coach assegnato.");
          }}
        />
      )}

      {sheet === "add" && (
        <AddMemberSheet
          title="Aggiungi alla classe"
          members={members}
          subtitle={`Restano ${free} ${free === 1 ? "posto" : "posti"}. Il socio riceve una notifica.`}
          exclude={[...session.roster, ...session.waitlist]}
          onClose={() => setSheet(null)}
          onPick={(id) => {
            onUpdate({ ...session, roster: [...session.roster, id] });
            setSheet(null);
            onToast(`${nameOf(id)} iscritto alla classe delle ${session.time}.`);
          }}
        />
      )}

      {removing && (
        <ConfirmDialog
          title={`Rimuovere ${nameOf(removing)}?`}
          body={
            session.waitlist.length
              ? `Il posto passa a ${nameOf(session.waitlist[0])}, primo in lista d'attesa. Entrambi ricevono una notifica.`
              : "Il posto torna disponibile e il socio riceve una notifica."
          }
          confirmLabel="Rimuovi"
          danger
          onCancel={() => setRemoving(null)}
          onConfirm={() => remove(removing)}
        />
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

/* ---------- Sezione ---------- */

function ClassiSection({ members, sessions, setSessions, onToast }) {
  const staff = members.filter((m) => m.role);
  const nameOf = (id) => (members.find((m) => m.id === id) || {}).name || "Socio";
  const [selected, setSelected] = useState(() => {
    const t = offsetDay(0);
    return isClosedDay(t) ? offsetDay(1) : t;
  });
  const [openKey, setOpenKey] = useState(null);

  const open = openKey ? sessions[openKey] : null;

  if (open) {
    return (
      <ClassDetail
        key={open.key}
        session={open}
        members={members}
        staff={staff}
        nameOf={nameOf}
        onBack={() => setOpenKey(null)}
        onUpdate={(next) => setSessions((all) => ({ ...all, [next.key]: next }))}
        onToast={onToast}
      />
    );
  }

  return (
    <DayView
      sessions={sessions}
      selected={selected}
      onSelect={setSelected}
      nameOf={nameOf}
      onOpen={(key) => {
        setSelected(sessions[key].iso);
        setOpenKey(key);
      }}
    />
  );
}

/* ---------- Segnaposto ---------- */

function Placeholder({ label }) {
  return (
    <div className="border border-dashed border-neutral-800 rounded-2xl p-12 text-center">
      <div className="text-neutral-300 text-[15px]">{label}</div>
      <div className="text-neutral-600 text-[12px] mt-2">
        Sezione da prototipare nella prossima fase.
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function MemberView({ onBack }) {
  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-black flex flex-col items-center justify-center px-6 text-center">
      <Logo size={26} />
      <p className="text-neutral-400 text-[14px] mt-8 max-w-xs leading-relaxed">
        Vista socio. Qui gira l'app che hai già costruito: home, classi, WOD,
        progressi, profilo.
      </p>
      <button
        onClick={onBack}
        className="mt-7 px-5 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-neutral-50 text-black text-[12px] font-semibold hover:bg-white flex items-center gap-2"
      >
        <ChevronLeft size={15} />
        Torna alla console admin
      </button>
    </div>
  );
}

/* ---------- App ---------- */

export default function PeakAdmin() {
  const weekday = new Date().getDay();

  const [role, setRole] = useState("admin");
  const [section, setSection] = useState("dashboard");
  const [moreOpen, setMoreOpen] = useState(false);

  // Soci e richieste vivono qui: dashboard, badge e sezione Utenti leggono
  // gli stessi dati, così approvare un socio aggiorna tutto insieme.
  const [members, setMembers] = useState(MEMBERS_SEED);
  const [pendingList, setPendingList] = useState(PENDING_SEED);
  const [plans, setPlans] = useState(SUB_TYPES);
  // WOD e score vivono qui: uscendo dalla sezione non si perdono.
  const [wodSeed] = useState(() => buildWodSeed(MEMBERS_SEED));
  const [wods, setWods] = useState(wodSeed.wods);
  const [wodScores, setWodScores] = useState(wodSeed.scores);
  // Iscritti, coach e presenze di ogni classe: vivono qui cosi restano
  // anche uscendo dalla sezione.
  const [sessions, setSessions] = useState(() => buildSessions(MEMBERS_SEED));
  const [toast, setToast] = useState(null);

  function flash(msg) {
    setToast(msg);
    setTimeout(() => setToast(null), 2800);
  }

  const classes = useMemo(() => buildClasses(weekday), [weekday]);
  const openBox = useMemo(() => buildOpenBox(weekday), [weekday]);
  const hours = weekday === 6 ? OPEN_BOX_HOURS.saturday : OPEN_BOX_HOURS.weekday;
  const pending = pendingList.length;
  const activeMembers = members.filter(isActive).length;

  const fillRate = classes.length
    ? Math.round(
        (classes.reduce((s, c) => s + c.booked, 0) /
          (classes.length * CLASS_CAPACITY)) *
          100
      )
    : 0;

  if (role === "socio") return <MemberView onBack={() => setRole("admin")} />;

  const isDay = section === "openbox";

  function go(key) {
    setSection(key);
  }

  return (
    <PlansContext.Provider value={plans}>
    <div
      className="min-h-screen bg-black flex"
      style={{ fontFamily: "Montserrat, sans-serif" }}
    >
      <style>{`@import url('https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Barlow:wght@600;700;800&family=Montserrat:wght@400;500;600&display=swap');`}</style>

      <Sidebar active={isDay ? section : section} onChange={go} badge={pending} />

      <div className="flex-1 min-w-0 pb-28 lg:pb-8">
        <TopBar
          role={role}
          onRole={setRole}
          title="Dashboard"
          onBack={isDay || section === "classi" ? () => setSection("dashboard") : undefined}
        />

        <main className="px-5 lg:px-8 pt-2 pb-6 max-w-5xl">
          {section === "dashboard" && (
            <Dashboard
              classes={classes}
              openBox={openBox}
              hours={hours}
              pending={pending}
              fillRate={fillRate}
              activeMembers={activeMembers}
              onGo={go}
            />
          )}
          {isDay && (
            <DayScreen
              tab="openbox"
              onTab={go}
              classes={classes}
              openBox={openBox}
              hours={hours}
            />
          )}
          {section === "utenti" && (
            <UtentiSection
              members={members}
              setMembers={setMembers}
              pending={pendingList}
              setPending={setPendingList}
              onToast={flash}
            />
          )}
          {section === "abbonamenti" && (
            <AbbonamentiSection
              members={members}
              setMembers={setMembers}
              plans={plans}
              setPlans={setPlans}
              onToast={flash}
            />
          )}
          {section === "classi" && (
            <ClassiSection
              members={members}
              sessions={sessions}
              setSessions={setSessions}
              onToast={flash}
            />
          )}
          {section === "wod" && (
            <WodSection
              members={members}
              wods={wods}
              setWods={setWods}
              scores={wodScores}
              setScores={setWodScores}
              onToast={flash}
            />
          )}
          {section === "staff" && IS_OWNER && (
            <StaffSection members={members} setMembers={setMembers} />
          )}
          {!isDay &&
            section !== "dashboard" &&
            section !== "utenti" &&
            section !== "abbonamenti" &&
            section !== "classi" &&
            section !== "wod" &&
            section !== "staff" && (
              <Placeholder label={NAV.find((n) => n.key === section)?.label} />
            )}
        </main>
      </div>

      <TabBar active={section} onChange={go} onMore={() => setMoreOpen(true)} badge={pending} />
      {moreOpen && <MoreSheet onClose={() => setMoreOpen(false)} onSelect={go} />}

      {toast && (
        <div className="fixed bottom-24 lg:bottom-6 left-5 right-5 sm:left-auto sm:right-6 sm:w-80 z-50">
          <div className="bg-neutral-100 text-black rounded-2xl px-4 py-3.5 text-[13px] shadow-xl shadow-black/50">
            {toast}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
    </PlansContext.Provider>
  );
}
